📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 该综述共确定了 550 项研究,其中包括 505 项初步研究和 45 项系统评价。大多数研究侧重于针对成人和儿童混合人群的社区一级干预措施,主要集中在南美洲和南亚的高 患病率 地区。社区教育是研究最广泛的干预领域,有 47 个 RCT 和 140 个 NSI。由于方法学上的缺陷,大多数系统评价被评为低质量或极低质量。证据差距图强调需要进行更多高质量的研究,特别是在 疫苗、环境干预措施和 媒介 控制策略方面。
  • 化合物16被确定为新型nSMase2抑制剂,硫代吡啶并嘧啶酮衍生物在细胞水平实验中展现出对登革病毒的抗病毒药物活性。 [10.1002/cmdc.70452]。
  • 研究结果揭示了从低风险登革热流行模式向高风险登革热流行模式的转变,并伴随城市间同步化程度的显著提升。研究还表明,气候异常可增加登革热流行的许可日数量。结果提示此类许可日与登革热同步化显著相关。
  • 多个区域的年气候变量与蚊媒密度相关,其中降雨频率对埃及伊蚊密度的影响强于累积降雨量;温度与滞后侵扰指数与两种蚊媒及登革热发病率均显示强关联,且滞后效应可持续至8周。 [10.1371/journal.pntd.0014201]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The review identified 550 studies in total, comprising 505 primary studies and 45 systematic reviews.
  • Compound 16 was identified as a novel nSMase2 inhibitor, and the thienopyrimidinone derivatives demonstrated antiviral activity against Dengue virus in cell-based assays. [10.1002/cmdc.70452].
  • Our results reveal a transition from a low-transmission regime to a high-transmission regime accompanied by a marked increase in synchronization across cities.
  • Annual climatic variables were associated with mosquito abundance in several districts, with rainfall frequency showing a stronger effect on Aedes aegypti abundance than accumulated rainfall, and temperature and lagged infestation indices showing strong associations with both species and dengue incidence, with lag effects detected up to eight weeks.

  • A study suggests preliminary evidence of benefit from monoclonal antibodies against dengue virus in hospitalized cases, reported by MedPage Today. [news-0d3a3f38871a9318].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: PloS one  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-21  |  DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0356838  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 21, 8, e0356838

登革热感染后的急性和长期心血管影响:系统评价方案。

Post-acute and long-term cardiovascular effects following dengue infection: A systematic review protocol.
作者： Niroshan Chandrajith Lokunarangoda, Akhila Rakshitha Wimalasundera, Manikka H. M. Thisara S. Perera, Damayanthi Idampitiya, H. Naveen Tharaka Perera, K. Gayana Dilki Kothalawala, Arunasalam Pathmeswaran, Namal P. M. Liyanage, Ananda Wijewickrama, U. Arjuna Bandara Medagama
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:登革热是热带和亚热带地区的主要虫媒病毒疾病,传统上被视为一种急性自限性疾病。然而,新兴证据表明其影响可能超出急性期,特别是涉及心血管系统。已报道的临床表现包括重大不良心血管事件、心律失常、血栓栓塞并发症、亚临床心肌功能障碍、内皮功能障碍及血管改变。由于研究设计、人群特征、结局定义及随访时长的差异,现有证据仍较为零散。本系统性综述旨在评估和综合登革热感染后急性期后及长期心血管结局的证据,包括其谱系、比较风险及决定因素。方法与伦理:本方案概述了一项系统性综述,旨在评估登革热感染后急性期后及长期心血管结局。纳入标准为年龄不限的人类研究,且需报告急性登革热发病至少4周后的心血管结局。实验室确诊登革热将被优先用于主要比较性综合;临床诊断登革热将用于描述性综合,并在适当情况下用于亚组或敏感性分析。采用队列研究、病例对照、横断面或病例系列设计的研究均符合纳入条件。比较性研究若采用登革热阴性或明确定义的对照组,可在可行时估计效应;非比较性随访研究将用于描述性综合。结局包括重大临床心血管事件(如卒中或短暂性脑缺血发作)、亚临床心脏或血管异常及与心血管相关的生物标志物。检索将在PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus、Cochrane图书馆、CINAHL、ScienceDirect、ProQuest及Google Scholar数据库中进行。两名审稿人将独立完成筛选、数据提取及偏倚风险与关键评估。证据将以叙述性综合形式呈现;在可比较情况下,将进行随机效应荟萃分析及推荐分级评估、制定和评价(GRADE)评估。PROSPERO注册号:CRD420261354181。讨论:本综述将总结登革热后心血管后遗症,并识别证据缺口。研究结果可能阐明登革热后心血管风险,并为未来研究、临床随访及登革热流行地区的公共卫生规划提供参考。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是热带和亚热带地区的主要虫媒病毒疾病,传统上被视为一种急性自限性疾病。然而,新兴证据表明其影响可能超出急性期,特别是涉及心血管系统。
设计与对象
本系统性综述方案概述了一项针对报告急性登革热发病至少4周后心血管结局的人类研究的研究。采用队列研究、病例对照、横断面或病例系列设计的研究均符合纳入条件。
核心方法
证据将以叙述性综合形式呈现;在可比较情况下,将进行随机效应荟萃分析及推荐分级评估、制定和评价(GRADE)评估。两名审稿人将独立完成筛选、数据提取及偏倚风险与关键评估。
主要结果
已报道的临床表现包括重大不良心血管事件、心律失常、血栓栓塞并发症、亚临床心肌功能障碍、内皮功能障碍及血管改变。
解释与创新
本综述将总结登革热后心血管后遗症,并识别证据缺口。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可能阐明登革热后心血管风险,并为未来研究、临床随访及登革热流行地区的公共卫生规划提供参考。
局限与证据强度
由于研究设计、人群特征、结局定义及随访时长的差异,现有证据仍较为零散。

Post-acute and long-term cardiovascular effects following dengue infection: A systematic review protocol.

Authors: Niroshan Chandrajith Lokunarangoda, Akhila Rakshitha Wimalasundera, Manikka H. M. Thisara S. Perera, Damayanthi Idampitiya, H. Naveen Tharaka Perera, K. Gayana Dilki Kothalawala, Arunasalam Pathmeswaran, Namal P. M. Liyanage, Ananda Wijewickrama, U. Arjuna Bandara Medagama
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND AND AIM: Dengue is a major arboviral disease in tropical and subtropical regions, traditionally regarded as an acute, self-limiting illness. However, emerging evidence suggests that its consequences may extend beyond the acute phase, especially involving the cardiovascular system. Reported manifestations include major adverse cardiovascular events, arrhythmias, thromboembolic complications, subclinical myocardial dysfunction, endothelial dysfunction, and vascular changes. Evidence remains fragmented due to variation in study designs, population characteristics, outcome definitions, and follow-up durations. This systematic review aims to appraise and synthesise evidence on post-acute and long-term cardiovascular outcomes following dengue infection, including their spectrum, comparative risk, and determinants. METHODS AND ANALYSIS: This protocol outlines a systematic review of cardiovascular outcomes in the post-acute and long-term phases of dengue infection. Human studies of any age group will be included if they report cardiovascular outcomes at least 4 weeks after acute dengue illness. Laboratory-confirmed dengue will be prioritised for primary comparative synthesis; clinically diagnosed dengue will contribute to descriptive synthesis and, where appropriate, subgroup or sensitivity analyses. Studies using cohort, case-control, cross-sectional, or case-series designs will be eligible. Comparative studies with dengue-negative or clearly defined reference controls will estimate effects where available, while non-comparative follow-up studies will contribute to descriptive synthesis. Outcomes will include major clinical cardiovascular events, including stroke or transient ischaemic attacks, subclinical cardiac or vascular abnormalities, and cardiovascular-related biomarkers. Searches will be conducted in PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, Cochrane Library, CINAHL, ScienceDirect, ProQuest, and Google Scholar. Two reviewers will independently perform screening, data extraction, and risk-of-bias and critical appraisal assessments. Evidence will be synthesized narratively; where comparable, random-effects meta-analysis and Grading of Recommendations Assessment, Development and Evaluation (GRADE) assessment will be conducted. PROSPERO registration: CRD420261354181. DISCUSSION: This review will summarize post-dengue cardiovascular sequelae and identify evidence gaps. Findings may clarify post-dengue cardiovascular risk and inform future research, clinical follow-up, and public health planning in dengue-endemic settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a major arboviral disease in tropical and subtropical regions, traditionally regarded as an acute, self-limiting illness. However, emerging evidence suggests that its consequences may extend beyond the acute phase, especially involving the cardiovascular system.
Design and population
This systematic review protocol outlines a study of human studies of any age group that report cardiovascular outcomes at least 4 weeks after acute dengue illness. Studies using cohort, case-control, cross-sectional, or case-series designs will be eligible.
Core methods
Evidence will be synthesized narratively; where comparable, random-effects meta-analysis and Grading of Recommendations Assessment, Development and Evaluation (GRADE) assessment will be conducted. Two reviewers will independently perform screening, data extraction, and risk-of-bias and critical appraisal assessments.
Main results
Reported manifestations include major adverse cardiovascular events, arrhythmias, thromboembolic complications, subclinical myocardial dysfunction, endothelial dysfunction, and vascular changes.
Interpretation and novelty
This review will summarize post-dengue cardiovascular sequelae and identify evidence gaps.
Scientific and public-health significance
Findings may clarify post-dengue cardiovascular risk and inform future research, clinical follow-up, and public health planning in dengue-endemic settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
Evidence remains fragmented due to variation in study designs, population characteristics, outcome definitions, and follow-up durations.

基因组与流行病学证据:广东省一例季前登革热病例与当地越冬登革病毒的关联

Genomic and epidemiological evidence linking a pre-season dengue case to local overwintering transmission in Guangdong, China.
作者： Liling Lin, Man Liu, Jianqian Chen, Qihua Guan, Siyang Jiang, Xiaohua Tan, Xin Zhang, Penghui Jia, Jinhua Duan, Hui Deng
摘要中文翻译背景:2025年3月,广东省报告了中国最早的本地获得性登革热病例,发生时间较典型登革热流行季提前两个月。这一前所未有的事件引发了关于登革热在亚热带地区潜在越冬机制的关键问题。本次调查旨在确定该病例是否代表2024年登革热流行后的再次暴发,或为一起新的输入性事件。方法:为追踪感染源,开展了综合流行病学、媒介生物学与实验室调查。采用标准化病例访谈记录患者14天暴露史,包括旅行模式。在距离暴露场所100米范围内开展了针对性调查。在距离暴露场所200米范围内实施了主动登革热媒介与媒介生物学评估,包括布雷图指数计算与诱卵器布放。采用实时RT-PCR、NS1抗原及IgM/IgG抗体检测血清样本中的登革病毒。利用Sanger测序对包膜(E)基因进行测序,随后在MEGA软件中采用邻接法对全球变异株进行系统发育分析。主要发现:包膜(E)基因基因组分析显示,该病例登革病毒血清型1毒株与当地2024年获得性病例的同源性为99.93%,仅存在单核苷酸变异。高风险区域在前一登革热流行年内显示相对较高的登革热媒介密度。气象数据证实,在26天的暴露窗口期内,有22天满足20-25°C的温度阈值,有利于蚊媒活动。结论:本次调查提供了基因组与流行病学证据,提示中国存在登革热越冬现象。研究结果与2024年登革热流行后的本地持续存在一致,垂直传播是越冬蚊媒的主要假说。这些结果挑战了传统季节性登革热模式的假设,并强调在经历气候变暖的亚热带地区开展全年监测与媒介控制的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2025年3月,广东省报告了中国最早的本地获得性登革热病例,发生时间较典型登革热流行季提前两个月。这一前所未有的事件引发了关于登革热在亚热带地区潜在越冬机制的关键问题。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 3 月在中国广东省进行,重点关注单个季前登革热病例。对病例暴露地点半径100米范围内进行有针对性排查。
核心方法
为追踪感染源,开展了综合流行病学、媒介生物学与实验室调查。采用标准化病例访谈记录患者14天暴露史,包括旅行模式。采用实时RT-PCR、NS1抗原及IgM/IgG抗体检测血清样本中的登革病毒。利用Sanger测序对包膜(E)基因进行测序,随后在MEGA软件中采用邻接法对全球变异株进行系统发育分析。
主要结果
包膜(E)基因基因组分析显示,该病例登革病毒血清型1毒株与当地2024年获得性病例的同源性为99.93%,仅存在单核苷酸变异。高风险区域在前一登革热流行年内显示相对较高的登革热媒介密度。气象数据证实,在26天的暴露窗口期内,有22天满足20-25°C的温度阈值,有利于蚊媒活动。
解释与创新
本次调查提供了基因组与流行病学证据,提示中国存在登革热越冬现象。研究结果与2024年登革热流行后的本地持续存在一致,垂直传播是越冬蚊媒的主要假说。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果挑战了传统季节性登革热模式的假设,并强调在经历气候变暖的亚热带地区开展全年监测与媒介控制的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于单一病例的关注范围及其他登革热传播途径的可能性。基因组分析与气象数据支持登革热越冬假说,证据较为充分。

Genomic and epidemiological evidence linking a pre-season dengue case to local overwintering transmission in Guangdong, China.

Authors: Liling Lin, Man Liu, Jianqian Chen, Qihua Guan, Siyang Jiang, Xiaohua Tan, Xin Zhang, Penghui Jia, Jinhua Duan, Hui Deng
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In March 2025, Guangdong Province reported China's earliest locally acquired dengue case, occurring two months before the typical transmission season. This unprecedented event raised critical questions about potential overwintering transmission mechanisms in subtropical regions. The investigation sought to determine whether this case represented a resurgence from the 2024 outbreak or a new importation event. METHODS: We conducted comprehensive epidemiological, entomological, and laboratory investigations to trace the origin of infection. Standardized case interviews were conducted to record the patient's 14-day exposure history, including travel patterns. A targeted investigation within a 100-meter radius of the case exposure sites was conducted. Active surveillance and entomological assessments-including Breteau Index calculations and ovitrap deployment-were implemented within 200-meter radius of exposure sites. Serum samples were tested for DENV using real-time RT-PCR, NS1 antigen, and IgM/IgG antibodies. The envelope (E) gene was sequenced using Sanger sequencing, followed by phylogenetic analysis against global variants with the Neighbor-Joining method in MEGA software. PRINCIPAL FINDINGS: Genomic analysis of the envelope (E) gene revealed 99.93% identity between the case's dengue virus serotype 1 strain and a locally acquired 2024 case, differing by only a single-nucleotide variant. The high-risk area showed a relatively high dengue prevalence within the previous outbreak year. Meteorological data confirmed that 22 of 26 days during the exposure window met temperature thresholds (20-25°C) conducive to mosquito activity. CONCLUSIONS: This investigation provides genomic and epidemiological evidence suggesting overwintering dengue transmission in China. The findings are consistent with local persistence from the 2024 outbreak, with vertical transmission in overwintering mosquitoes as a leading hypothesis.. These results challenge traditional assumptions about seasonal transmission patterns and underscore the need for year-round vector surveillance in subtropical regions experiencing climate warming.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In March 2025, Guangdong Province reported China's earliest locally acquired dengue case, occurring two months before the typical transmission season. This unprecedented event raised critical questions about potential overwintering transmission mechanisms in subtropical regions.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Guangdong Province, China, during March 2025, focusing on a single pre-season dengue case. A targeted investigation within a 100-meter radius of the case exposure sites was conducted.
Core methods
Comprehensive epidemiological, entomological, and laboratory investigations were conducted to trace the origin of infection. Standardized case interviews were performed to record the patient's 14-day exposure history, including travel patterns. Serum samples were tested for DENV using real-time RT-PCR, NS1 antigen, and IgM/IgG antibodies. The envelope (E) gene was sequenced using Sanger sequencing, followed by phylogenetic analysis against global variants with the Neighbor-Joining method in MEGA software.
Main results
Genomic analysis of the envelope (E) gene revealed 99.93% identity between the case's dengue virus serotype 1 strain and a locally acquired 2024 case, differing by only a single-nucleotide variant. The high-risk area showed a relatively high dengue prevalence within the previous outbreak year. Meteorological data confirmed that 22 of 26 days during the exposure window met temperature thresholds (20-25°C) conducive to mosquito activity.
Interpretation and novelty
This investigation provides genomic and epidemiological evidence suggesting overwintering dengue transmission in China. The findings are consistent with local persistence from the 2024 outbreak, with vertical transmission in overwintering mosquitoes as a leading hypothesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results challenge traditional assumptions about seasonal transmission patterns and underscore the need for year-round vector surveillance in subtropical regions experiencing climate warming.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single case and the potential for other transmission pathways. The evidence is strong, as genomic analysis and meteorological data support the hypothesis of overwintering transmission.

气候驱动下的埃及伊蚊侵扰与登革热传播的时空动态:巴西南部阿雷格里港

Climate-driven spatiotemporal dynamics of Aedes infestation and dengue transmission in Porto Alegre, Southern Brazil
作者： Adryan Aparecido da Silva, Álvaro Gil Araujo Ferreira, José Lourenço, Amanda Cupertino de Freitas, Ferreira ÁGA
摘要中文翻译登革热传播受气候条件强烈影响,这些条件会影响蚊媒种群动态与病毒循环。在巴西南部,登革热历史上发病水平较低,但近期气候异常可能正在推动埃及伊蚊媒介种群扩张并增加当地登革热发病率发病。本研究调查了气候变量、埃及伊蚊侵扰与登革热病例在阿雷格里港(巴西南部,2018–2025年)的时空关联。研究采用了蚊媒学、监测与气候数据,并运用Moran’s I与LISA进行空间关联分析、Kendall相关性分析、多项式回归与LASSO,以识别关键驱动因子并构建蚊媒侵扰与登革热发病率的预测模型。埃及伊蚊与白纹伊蚊间存在显著空间关联,且在所有年份均检测到持续的局部聚类。多个区域的年气候变量与蚊媒密度相关。总体而言,降雨频率对埃及伊蚊密度的影响强于累积降雨量。温度与滞后侵扰指数与两种蚊媒及登革热发病率均显示强关联,且滞后效应可持续至8周。预测模型在观测值与预测值间显示出良好一致性,尤以低至中等侵扰水平为佳。滞后变量在蚊媒密度与登革热发病率模型中均被持续保留,凸显时间预测变量在预测媒介动态与登革热风险中的重要性。该方法可广泛用于预测埃及伊蚊侵扰与疾病发病率,并强调整合蚊媒学与气候监测数据以提升对登革热高风险期的预测与早期检测能力,从而为大型城市环境中的公共卫生干预提供更有效的信息支撑。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
巴西南部的登革热传播历史上发病水平较低,但近期气候异常可能正在推动埃及伊蚊媒介种群扩张并增加当地登革热发病率发病,亟需量化气候因子对媒介动态与疾病风险的驱动作用。
设计与对象
本研究分析了巴西南部阿雷格里港的蚊媒学、监测与气候数据(2018–2025年),以调查气候、埃及伊蚊侵扰与登革热病例的时空关联。
核心方法
研究采用了蚊媒学、监测与气候数据,并运用Moran’s I与LISA进行空间关联分析、Kendall相关性分析、多项式回归与LASSO,以识别关键驱动因子并构建蚊媒侵扰与登革热发病率的预测模型。
主要结果
多个区域的年气候变量与蚊媒密度相关,其中降雨频率对埃及伊蚊密度的影响强于累积降雨量;温度与滞后侵扰指数与两种蚊媒及登革热发病率均显示强关联,且滞后效应可持续至8周。
解释与创新
研究强调整合蚊媒学与气候监测数据以预测登革热高风险期的重要性,并证明滞后变量在蚊媒密度与登革热发病率模型中均被持续保留。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持利用预测模型提升大型城市环境中登革热高风险期的早期检测能力,为更有效的公共卫生干预与监测策略提供信息支撑。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖生态关联与模型预测而缺乏直接实验验证或对未测量混杂因素的控制,证据强度为中等。

Climate-driven spatiotemporal dynamics of Aedes infestation and dengue transmission in Porto Alegre, Southern Brazil

Authors: Adryan Aparecido da Silva, Álvaro Gil Araujo Ferreira, José Lourenço, Amanda Cupertino de Freitas, Ferreira ÁGA
Original abstract (English)
Dengue transmission is strongly influenced by climatic conditions that affect mosquito population dynamics and virus circulation. In Southern Brazil, where dengue historically occurred at low levels, recent climatic anomalies may be contributing to the expansion of Aedes vectors and increased local dengue incidence. This study investigated the spatiotemporal association between climatic variables, Aedes infestation and dengue cases in Porto Alegre (Southern Brazil, from 2018 to 2025). Entomological, surveillance and climatic data were analyzed using Moran’s I and LISA for spatial association, Kendall correlation, polynomial regression and LASSO to identify relevant drivers and develop predictive models of mosquito infestation and dengue incidence. Strong spatial association between Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus was observed, with persistent local clusters detected across all years. Annual climatic variables were associated with mosquito abundance in several districts. Overall, rainfall frequency had a stronger effect on Ae. aegypti abundance than accumulated rainfall. Temperature and lagged infestation indices showed strong associations with both species and dengue incidence, with lag effects detected up to eight weeks. Predictive models demonstrated good agreement between observed and predicted values, particularly at low to moderate infestation levels. Lagged variables were consistently retained in both mosquito abundance and dengue incidence models, highlighting the importance of temporal predictors in anticipating vector dynamics and dengue risk. This approach is broadly applicable for predicting Aedes infestation and disease incidence and it underscores the importance of integrating entomological and climatic surveillance data to enhance anticipation and early detection of dengue risk periods in large urban settings and inform more effective public health interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue transmission in Southern Brazil has historically been low, but recent climatic anomalies may be expanding Aedes vector populations and increasing local dengue incidence, creating a need to quantify climatic drivers of vector dynamics and disease risk.
Design and population
The study analyzed entomological, surveillance and climatic data from Porto Alegre, Southern Brazil, covering the period from 2018 to 2025 to investigate spatiotemporal associations between climate, Aedes infestation and dengue cases.
Core methods
Entomological, surveillance and climatic data were analyzed using Moran’s I and LISA for spatial association, Kendall correlation, polynomial regression and LASSO to identify relevant drivers and develop predictive models of mosquito infestation and dengue incidence.
Main results
Annual climatic variables were associated with mosquito abundance in several districts, with rainfall frequency showing a stronger effect on Aedes aegypti abundance than accumulated rainfall, and temperature and lagged infestation indices showing strong associations with both species and dengue incidence, with lag effects detected up to eight weeks.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the importance of integrating entomological and climatic surveillance data to anticipate dengue risk periods, demonstrating that lagged variables are consistently retained in models for mosquito abundance and dengue incidence.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of predictive models to enhance early detection of dengue risk in large urban settings, informing more effective public health interventions and surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is moderate in strength due to reliance on ecological associations and model-based predictions without direct experimental validation or control for unmeasured confounders.

登革病毒-2感染小鼠时滋养层代谢重编程触发不良妊娠结局

Trophoblast metabolic reprogramming triggers adverse pregnancy outcomes during DENV-2 infection in mice
作者： Han Wang, Fei-Yang Xue, Shi-Qi He, Jin-Fan Yang, Hao Zhang, Jia-Ying Cao, Jia-Tong Chang, Chen Zhang, Jing An, Zi-Yang Sheng
摘要中文翻译摘要 与登革病毒(DENV)感染相关的不良妊娠结局是登革热的严重临床表现之一;然而,其潜在机制仍不明确。胎盘滋养层功能障碍是多种妊娠并发症的核心因素。作为胎盘的功能单位,合体滋养层(STB)需持续融合前体滋养细胞并获得充足能量供应。本研究通过感染Ifnar1−/−妊娠小鼠建立不良妊娠模型。研究未检测到病毒在胎盘滋养层内复制,但观察到STB结构严重受损。RNA测序分析显示,前体滋养细胞的葡萄糖代谢受扰乱,胎盘组织上调促融合基因,但无法完成合胞体形成。代谢检测显示ATP水平降低、乳酸浓度升高,提示能量供应不足阻碍滋养细胞分化。体内使用糖酵解抑制剂2-DG可恢复胎盘氧化磷酸化(OXPHOS)活性,并显著改善病理结局。进一步机制研究证实,DENV感染诱导氧化应激,激活缺氧诱导因子Hif1α,进而上调乳酸脱氢酶(LDHA)表达。研究识别出该级联反应是滋养细胞代谢重编程的核心机制。本研究不仅阐明了驱动DENV诱导不良妊娠结局的关键病理过程,还提出了新的临床干预治疗策略。重要性 妊娠期登革热感染与不良胎儿结局相关,但其潜在机制尚不清楚。在Ifnar1−/−妊娠小鼠中,研究显示胎儿生长受限由破坏母体环境并损害胎盘功能所驱动。研究识别出合体滋养层损伤及前体滋养细胞融合缺陷,尽管存在促融合基因的代偿性上调。胎盘代谢分析显示线粒体氧化磷酸化向糖酵解转变,伴随ATP生成减少及合胞体形成受损。体内抑制糖酵解可部分恢复胎盘结构并改善胎儿结局,支持代谢重编程在其中的关键作用。从机制上看,活性氧(ROS)激活HIF-1α并上调其下游靶标LDHA,从而将代谢重编程与胎盘功能障碍联系起来,并凸显该通路为潜在治疗靶点。本研究揭示了登革病毒相关妊娠并发症的发生机制,并将代谢通路作为潜在治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
妊娠期登革病毒(DENV)感染与不良胎儿结局相关,但其潜在机制仍不明确。本研究旨在探讨DENV感染如何影响胎盘功能并导致不良妊娠结局。
设计与对象
The study used a model of adverse pregnancy by infecting Ifnar 1 −/− pregnant mice with DENV-2. The research focused on placental trophoblasts and their structural and metabolic changes during infection.
核心方法
RNA-seq 分析用于研究前体滋养层和胎盘组织中的基因表达。代谢测定测量 ATP 水平和乳酸浓度,以评估能量供应及其对滋养层分化的影响。给予糖酵解抑制剂2-DG以评估其对胎盘功能的影响。
主要结果
RNA测序分析显示前体滋养细胞葡萄糖代谢受扰乱,胎盘组织上调促融合基因,但未完成合胞体形成。代谢检测显示ATP水平降低、乳酸浓度升高,提示能量供应不足及滋养细胞分化受损。
解释与创新
作者解读认为,DENV感染通过代谢重编程破坏胎盘功能,进而损害滋养细胞融合并导致不良妊娠结局。本研究识别出一种新机制,将代谢变化与胎盘功能障碍联系起来。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为登革病毒诱导不良妊娠结局的机制提供了见解,并将代谢通路作为改善母婴健康的潜在治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
研究基于动物模型,可能无法完全代表人类妊娠。研究证据较为充分,直接将DENV感染与胎盘代谢变化及结构损伤联系起来,支持所提机制。

Trophoblast metabolic reprogramming triggers adverse pregnancy outcomes during DENV-2 infection in mice

Authors: Han Wang, Fei-Yang Xue, Shi-Qi He, Jin-Fan Yang, Hao Zhang, Jia-Ying Cao, Jia-Tong Chang, Chen Zhang, Jing An, Zi-Yang Sheng
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Adverse pregnancy outcomes associated with dengue virus (DENV) infection are a severe manifestation of dengue fever; however, we still poorly understand the underlying mechanisms. Trophoblast dysfunction in the placenta contributes centrally to various pregnancy complications. As the functional unit of the placenta, the syncytiotrophoblast (STB) requires continuous fusion of precursor trophoblasts and adequate energy supply. In this study, we established a model of adverse pregnancy by infecting Ifnar 1 −/− pregnant mice with DENV-2. We did not detect viral replication within the placental trophoblast layer but observed substantial structural damage to the STB. RNA-seq analysis demonstrated that precursor trophoblasts exhibit disrupted glucose metabolism, and placental tissue upregulates pro-fusion genes—yet fails to complete syncytialization. Metabolic assays revealed reduced ATP levels and elevated lactate concentrations, indicating that an inadequate energy supply impedes trophoblast differentiation. Treatment with the glycolytic inhibitor 2-DG restored oxidative phosphorylation (OXPHOS) activity in the placenta and markedly improved pathological outcomes. Further mechanistic studies established that DENV infection induces oxidative stress, which activates hypoxia-inducible factor Hif1α, leading to subsequent upregulation of lactate dehydrogenase (LDHA) expression. We identify this cascade as a fundamental mechanism underlying metabolic reprogramming in trophoblasts. Our findings not only clarify the key pathological processes driving DENV-induced adverse pregnancy outcomes but also propose novel therapeutic strategies for clinical intervention. IMPORTANCE Dengue virus infection during pregnancy is linked to adverse fetal outcomes, but the underlying mechanisms remain unclear. In Ifnar 1 −/− pregnant mice, we show that fetal growth restriction is driven by disruption of the maternal environment that impairs placental function. We identify syncytiotrophoblast damage and defective trophoblast fusion despite compensatory upregulation of pro-fusion genes. Placental metabolic analysis reveals a shift from oxidative phosphorylation to glycolysis, accompanied by reduced ATP production and impaired syncytialization. Inhibition of glycolysis in vivo partially restores placental structure and improves fetal outcomes, supporting a key role for metabolic reprogramming. Mechanistically, ROS activates HIF-1α and upregulates its downstream target LDHA, thereby linking metabolic reprogramming to placental dysfunction and highlighting this pathway as a potential therapeutic target. These findings reveal a mechanism underlying DENV-associated pregnancy complications and highlight metabolic pathways as potential therapeutic targets.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) infection during pregnancy is linked to adverse fetal outcomes, but the underlying mechanisms remain unclear. The study aimed to investigate how DENV infection affects placental function and leads to adverse pregnancy outcomes.
Design and population
The study used a model of adverse pregnancy by infecting Ifnar 1 −/− pregnant mice with DENV-2. The research focused on placental trophoblasts and their structural and metabolic changes during infection.
Core methods
RNA-seq analysis was used to study gene expression in precursor trophoblasts and placental tissue. Metabolic assays measured ATP levels and lactate concentrations to assess energy supply and its impact on trophoblast differentiation. The glycolytic inhibitor 2-DG was administered to evaluate its effect on placental function.
Main results
RNA-seq analysis showed disrupted glucose metabolism in precursor trophoblasts and upregulated pro-fusion genes in placental tissue, yet syncytialization was not completed. Metabolic assays revealed reduced ATP levels and elevated lactate concentrations, indicating impaired energy supply and trophoblast differentiation.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that DENV infection disrupts placental function through metabolic reprogramming, which impairs trophoblast fusion and leads to adverse pregnancy outcomes. This study identifies a novel mechanism linking metabolic changes to placental dysfunction.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the mechanisms of DENV-induced adverse pregnancy outcomes and highlight metabolic pathways as potential therapeutic targets for improving maternal and fetal health.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model, which may not fully represent human pregnancy. The evidence is strong as it directly links DENV infection to metabolic changes and structural damage in the placenta, supporting the proposed mechanism.

来源于血浆外泌体的微小RNA作为非重症登革热感染疾病进展的潜在生物标志物

Plasma-derived extracellular vesicle microRNA as a potential biomarker associated with disease progression in non-severe dengue infection
作者： Gokulakannan Venkatesan, Wan Suriana Wan Ab Rahman, Wan Nazatul Shima Shahidan, Norliana Ghazali, Fatmawati Lambuk, Hisham Atan Edinur, Salfarina Iberahim, Azura Hussin, Saiful Azlan Mohamed, Alwi Muhd Besari Hashim
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热可表现为轻度发热性疾病到危及生命的出血热。在非重症病例中,早期识别有疾病进展风险的患者仍然具有挑战性。因此,本研究旨在探讨血浆外泌体微小RNA(miRNA)表达作为登革热感染生物标志物的能力。方法:共纳入52名登革热患者(26名有预警症状的登革热患者和26名无预警症状的登革热患者)。利用微阵列分析对来源于血浆外泌体的miRNA进行谱型分析,以识别与疾病进展相关的潜在生物标志物。通过生物信息学预测目标基因,构建miRNA-mRNA相互作用网络并识别关键枢纽基因。通过定量逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-qPCR)验证miRNA的表达水平,并利用接收者操作特征(ROC)曲线分析评估其诊断效能。还进行了额外的功能富集分析、基因本体(GO)分析和共表达研究。使用THP-1细胞进行体外实验,研究hsa-miR-107如何调控P2RY12表达,并通过RT-qPCR进一步验证P2RY12水平,以GAPDH为参考。结果:微阵列谱型分析显示,在有预警症状的登革热患者中,有24种上调的miRNA,其中显著的包括hsa-miR-107和hsa-miR-6879-5p,被选为潜在生物标志物。相关性分析显示,血小板计数与hsa-miR-107表达值之间存在统计学上显著的中度正相关。ROC分析显示这些miRNA具有强大的诊断性能,将其与疾病严重程度相关的血小板功能障碍和血管通透性改变联系起来。此外,功能分析支持这些通路在登革热发病机制中的参与,体外转染实验确认hsa-miR-107能够主动降低P2RY12的表达。结论:本研究突出了hsa-miR-107和hsa-miR-6879-5p的表达作为与登革热感染临床预警症状相关的候选生物标志物。这些发现可能为改进诊断和监测策略铺平道路。科学与公共卫生意义:该研究提供了关于登革热发病机制分子机制的见解,这可能有助于未来对疾病进展和治疗靶点的研究。局限性与证据强度:提供的证据未报告局限性。该研究展示了强大的诊断性能,并提供了登革热发病机制的分子见解,表明这些miRNA作为生物标志物的潜在应用具有中等证据强度。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热可表现为轻度发热疾病至威胁生命的出血热。早期识别有疾病进展风险的患者仍具有挑战性,尤其是在非重症病例中。
设计与对象
共纳入52名登革热患者(其中26名伴有警示征象的登革热患者和26名无警示征象的登革热患者)。研究采用微阵列分析技术对血浆外泌体来源的miRNA进行表征,以识别与疾病进展相关的潜在生物标志物。
核心方法
微阵列分析鉴定出24个在登革热警示征兆患者中上调的miRNA,其中显著的有hsa-miR-107和hsa-miR-6879-5p,被选为潜在生物标志物。miRNA的表达水平通过实时荧光定量逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-qPCR)进行验证,并采用受试者工作特征(ROC)曲线分析评估其诊断效能。使用THP-1细胞的体外实验探究了hsa-miR-107对P2RY12表达的调控作用,并通过以GAPDH为参考的RT-qPCR进一步验证了P2RY12水平。
主要结果
相关性分析显示,血小板计数与hsa-miR-107表达值呈现统计学显著的中等程度正相关。ROC分析表明这些miRNA与血小板功能障碍及与疾病严重程度相关的血管通透性改变具有较强的诊断性能。此外,功能分析支持这些通路参与登革发病机制的发病机制,且体外转染实验证实hsa-miR-107可主动降低P2RY12的表达。
解释与创新
本研究强调了hsa-miR-107和hsa-miR-6879-5p作为与登革热感染临床预警体征相关的候选生物标志物的表达。这些发现有望为改进诊断和监测策略铺平道路。
科研与公卫意义
该研究深入探讨了登革病毒发病机制的分子机制,为未来疾病进展与治疗靶点的相关研究提供参考。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告局限性。该研究展示了较强的诊断性能,并为登革病毒发病机制提供了分子层面的见解,表明这些miRNA作为生物标志物潜在应用的证据水平为中等。

Plasma-derived extracellular vesicle microRNA as a potential biomarker associated with disease progression in non-severe dengue infection

Authors: Gokulakannan Venkatesan, Wan Suriana Wan Ab Rahman, Wan Nazatul Shima Shahidan, Norliana Ghazali, Fatmawati Lambuk, Hisham Atan Edinur, Salfarina Iberahim, Azura Hussin, Saiful Azlan Mohamed, Alwi Muhd Besari Hashim
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue can manifest as a mild febrile illness to life-threatening haemorrhagic fever. The early identification of patients at risk of disease progression remains challenging, particularly in non-severe cases. Therefore, this study aims to investigate the capability of extracellular vesicle plasma microRNA (miRNA) expression as a biomarker in dengue infection. METHODOLOGY: A total of 52 dengue patients (26 dengue with warning signs and 26 dengue without warning signs) were enrolled. Microarray analysis was utilised to profile miRNA derived from plasma extracellular vesicles to identify potential biomarkers associated with disease progression. Target genes were predicted bioinformatically to construct a miRNA-mRNA interaction network and identify key hub genes. The expression levels of miRNAs were validated through quantitative reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR), and their diagnostic efficacy was evaluated using receiver operating characteristic (ROC) curve analysis. Additional functional enrichment analysis, gene ontology, and co-expression studies were conducted. An in vitro experiment using THP-1 cells investigated how hsa-miR-107 regulates P2RY12 expression, with further validation of P2RY12 levels through RT-qPCR using GAPDH as a reference. RESULTS: Microarray profiling identified 24 upregulated miRNAs in dengue patients with warning signs, notably hsa-miR-107 and hsa-miR-6879-5p, which were selected as potential biomarkers. Correlation analysis revealed that platelet count exhibited a statistically significant, moderate positive correlation with the hsa-miR-107 expression value. ROC analysis demonstrated strong diagnostic performance, linking these miRNAs to the platelet dysfunction and altered vascular permeability associated with disease severity. Furthermore, functional analysis supported the involvement of these pathways in dengue pathogenesis, and in vitro transfection confirmed that hsa-miR-107 actively decreases P2RY12 expression. CONCLUSION: This study highlights the expression of hsa-miR-107 and hsa-miR-6879-5p as candidate biomarkers associated with clinical warning signs in dengue infection. These findings could pave the way for improved diagnostic and monitoring strategies.
View research seven-element analysis

战术与战略:基于血清型和气候协变量的数据驱动方法在孟加拉国登革热预测中的地平线依赖性可适应框架

Tactical vs. strategic: An adaptable framework for horizon-dependent dengue forecasting using data-driven approaches with serotype and climate covariates in Bangladesh
作者： Md. Muqtadir Fuad, Md. Sajib Milki, Ridwan Al Aziz
摘要中文翻译登革热在包括孟加拉国在内的流行地区仍是一个主要的公共卫生问题。依赖气候变量的预测算法可能无法捕捉登革热血清型模式等流行病学信息。本研究提出了一种地平线依赖性登革热预测框架,并将其应用于孟加拉国。该流程在区域面板流程中纳入了时间、气象、人口统计和流行病学协变量,并使用聚合、地平线依赖、政权依赖、暴发检测及不确定性指标,比较了经典、机器学习和深度学习算法。通过系数解释、SHAP、排列重要性和非线性因果依赖性分析,研究了预测变量在不同地平线上的影响。SARIMAX被用作基线模型。TFT生成了分位数预测,但在暴发期间显示出较差的区间校准。结果表明,没有单一模型在所有预测地平线上表现最佳:SARIMAX在一步暴发预警中排名最高(精确率=0.886,召回率=0.824,F1分数=0.854,ROC-AUC=0.950),而Prophet在2–6地平线的汇总规模预测中表现最佳。MLR在政权依赖评估中表现具有竞争力。这些发现表明,登革热预测算法应根据预期决策目标进行选择,并使用任务相关的评估协议进行评估。

Tactical vs. strategic: An adaptable framework for horizon-dependent dengue forecasting using data-driven approaches with serotype and climate covariates in Bangladesh

Authors: Md. Muqtadir Fuad, Md. Sajib Milki, Ridwan Al Aziz
Original abstract (English)
Dengue remains a major public health problem in endemic regions, including Bangladesh. Forecasting algorithms relying on climatic variables may not capture epidemiological information such as dengue serotype patterns. This study proposes a horizon-dependent dengue forecasting framework and applies it to Bangladesh. This pipeline included temporal, meteorological, demographic, and epidemiological covariates in a district-panel pipeline and compares classical, machine-learning, and deep-learning algorithms using aggregate, horizon-wise, regime-wise, outbreak-detection, and uncertainty metrics. Coefficient-based interpretation, SHAP, permutation importance, and nonlinear causal-dependence analysis were used to examine predictor impacts across horizons. SARIMAX was used as the baseline model. TFT produced quantile forecasts but showed poor interval calibration during outbreak periods. Results show that no single model performed best across all forecasting horizons: SARIMAX ranked highest for one-step outbreak alerting (precision = 0.886, recall = 0.824, F1 score = 0.854, and ROC-AUC = 0.950), whereas Prophet performed best for pooled magnitude forecasting at horizons 2–6. MLR showed competitive performance in regime-wise evaluation. These findings show that dengue forecasting algorithms should be selected according to the intended decision objective and evaluated using task-relevant protocols.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue remains a major public health problem in endemic regions, including Bangladesh. This study proposes a horizon-dependent dengue forecasting framework and applies it to Bangladesh.
Design and population
This pipeline included temporal, meteorological, demographic, and epidemiological covariates in a district-panel pipeline and compares classical, machine-learning, and deep-learning algorithms using aggregate, horizon-wise, regime-wise, outbreak-detection, and uncertainty metrics.
Core methods
Coefficient-based interpretation, SHAP, permutation importance, and nonlinear causal-dependence analysis were used to examine predictor impacts across horizons. SARIMAX was used as the baseline model.
Main results
TFT produced quantile forecasts but showed poor interval calibration during outbreak periods. Results show that no single model performed best across all forecasting horizons: SARIMAX ranked highest for one-step outbreak alerting (precision = 0.886, recall = 0.824, F1 score = 0.854, and ROC-AUC = 0.950), whereas Prophet performed best for pooled magnitude forecasting at horizons 2–6. MLR showed competitive performance in regime-wise evaluation.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings show that dengue forecasting algorithms should be selected according to the intended decision objective and evaluated using task-relevant protocols.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can cautiously inform the development of more accurate and adaptable dengue forecasting systems tailored to specific decision-making needs in Bangladesh.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

气候与登革热同步化:巴西南部市级分析及跨州验证

Climate and dengue synchronization in southern Brazil: a municipal analysis with cross-state validation
作者： Enrique C. Gabrick, Antonio M. Batista, Iberê L. Caldas, Jürgen Kurths, Maíra Aguiar
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热正迅速扩张至其历史上的热带分布范围之外,引发人们对气候变化如何改变疫情集体动态的担忧。尽管大多数研究关注登革热风险,但气候如何影响登革热同步化的研究却鲜有报道。本研究利用巴西南部巴拉那州(PR)2010—2024年间74个市级单位的流行病学与气候数据,采用事件同步化(ES)方法量化登革热同步化程度。研究结果揭示了从低风险登革热流行模式向高风险登革热流行模式的转变,并伴随城市间同步化程度的显著提升。研究还表明,气候异常可增加登革热流行的许可日数量。结果提示此类许可日与登革热同步化显著相关。研究识别出一个二阶段气候机制:有利的气候条件首先降低异步状态的概率,并与同步化登革热流行的出现同时发生,随后维持更高的同步化水平。通过在塞阿腊州与米纳斯吉拉斯州开展对比分析,研究发现气候始终放大同步化效应,尽管其在同步化起始阶段的作用依赖于区域气候模式。研究结果凸显了气候驱动的同步化作为塑造登革热动态的新兴特征。

主要结果
研究结果揭示了从低风险登革热流行模式向高风险登革热流行模式的转变,并伴随城市间同步化程度的显著提升。研究还表明,气候异常可增加登革热流行的许可日数量。结果提示此类许可日与登革热同步化显著相关。
解释与创新
研究结果凸显了气候驱动的同步化作为塑造登革热动态的新兴特征。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提示气候异常可用于预测与监测登革热同步化,为登革热监测与干预策略提供科学依据。
局限与证据强度
所提供证据未报告研究局限性。证据强度较高,因研究采用了综合数据集与稳健分析方法量化登革热同步化程度并识别气候机制。

Climate and dengue synchronization in southern Brazil: a municipal analysis with cross-state validation

Authors: Enrique C. Gabrick, Antonio M. Batista, Iberê L. Caldas, Jürgen Kurths, Maíra Aguiar
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue transmission is rapidly expanding beyond its historical tropical range, raising concerns about how climate change may alter the collective dynamics of epidemics. While most studies focus on transmission risk, much less is known about how climate affects the synchronization of outbreaks. In this work, we investigate dengue synchronization using epidemiological and climate data from 74 municipalities in the state of Paraná (southern Brazil) between 2010 and 2024. We quantify outbreak coherence using the event synchronization (ES) method. Our results reveal a transition from a low-transmission regime to a high-transmission regime accompanied by a marked increase in synchronization across cities. We also show that climate anomalies increase the number of permissive days for dengue transmission. Our results suggest that such days are significantly associated with outbreak synchronization. We identify a two-stage climate mechanism: conducive climatic conditions first reduce the probability of asynchronous states and coincide with the emergence of synchronized outbreaks, and subsequently sustain higher synchronization levels. Extending the analysis through comparative analyses in Ceará and Minas Gerais, we uncover that climate consistently amplifies synchronization, although its role in the onset of synchronization depends on regional climatic regimes. These findings highlight climate-driven synchronization as an emerging feature shaping dengue dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis

Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that climate anomalies can be used to predict and monitor dengue outbreak synchronization, which may inform public health strategies for surveillance and intervention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations. The evidence strength is high as the study uses a comprehensive dataset and robust analytical methods to quantify outbreak coherence and identify climate mechanisms.

胎源性PF4+组织驻留巨噬细胞通过招募中性粒细胞作为关键机制介导小鼠DENV-2感染诱导的宫内发育迟缓

Fetal-derived PF4+ tissue-resident macrophages recruit neutrophils as a key mechanism underlying DENV-2-induced intrauterine growth restriction in mice
作者： Hao Zhang, Wanchen Hao, Han Wang, Na Gao, Shiqi He, Ruiyang Li, Feiyang Xue, Dongying Fan, Yanhua Wu, Peigang Wang
摘要中文翻译大量临床病例与流行病学分析表明,有症状的登革病毒(DENV)感染可能导致妊娠期不良结局;然而,其确切机制仍未明确。在先前的研究中,我们证明DENV-2感染通过触发中性粒细胞介导的胎盘血管系统破坏,在小鼠中诱导宫内发育迟缓。然而,驱动中性粒细胞向胎盘浸润的具体机制仍不清楚,尤其是考虑到胎盘构成一个独特的免疫豁免微环境,专用于维持母胎耐受。在本研究中,我们构建了干扰素-α/β受体缺陷小鼠感染DENV-2后胎盘的时间分辨单细胞图谱。通过整合单细胞转录组与单核苷酸多态性测序分析,揭示感染主要激活了胎源性组织驻留PF4+巨噬细胞。激活的胎源性巨噬细胞通过分泌结合中性粒细胞CXCR2的CXCL2,介导中性粒细胞优先向胎盘血管区域浸润并随后异常积聚。值得注意的是,招募的中性粒细胞进一步表现出增强的自我招募表型。阻断CXCL2-CXCR2信号通路可有效抑制中性粒细胞招募、恢复胎盘微血管密度并缓解胎儿宫内发育迟缓。本研究揭示了胎盘组织驻留巨噬细胞的激活是DENV-2感染后胎盘异常中性粒细胞浸润的责任细胞。从机制上看,巨噬细胞启动自我放大的中性粒细胞招募,而CXCL2-CXCR2信号通路在此过程中发挥关键作用。这些结果为针对DENV-2诱导的不良妊娠结局的临床治疗策略开发提供了重要信息。

主要结果

Fetal-derived PF4+ tissue-resident macrophages recruit neutrophils as a key mechanism underlying DENV-2-induced intrauterine growth restriction in mice

Authors: Hao Zhang, Wanchen Hao, Han Wang, Na Gao, Shiqi He, Ruiyang Li, Feiyang Xue, Dongying Fan, Yanhua Wu, Peigang Wang
Original abstract (English)
Extensive clinical cases and epidemiological analyses have indicated that symptomatic dengue virus (DENV) infection may lead to adverse outcomes during pregnancy; however, the precise mechanisms remain elusive. In our previous work, we demonstrated that DENV-2 infection induces intrauterine growth restriction by triggering neutrophil-mediated destruction of the placental vascular system in mice. However, the specific mechanisms driving neutrophil infiltration into the placenta remain unclear, especially given that the placenta constitutes a unique immune-privileged niche dedicated to maintaining maternal–fetal tolerance. In this study, we generated a time-resolved single-cell atlas of placentas from interferon-alpha/beta receptor-deficient mice infected with DENV-2. Integrated analysis of single-cell transcriptomes and single-nucleotide polymorphism sequencing revealed that infection primarily activated fetal-origin tissue-resident PF4 + macrophages. Activated fetal macrophages mediate preferential recruitment and the subsequent aberrant accumulation of neutrophils in the labyrinth zone through the secretion of CXCL2, which binds to neutrophil CXCR2. Notably, the recruited neutrophils further exhibited an enhanced self-recruitment phenotype. Blocking CXCL2-CXCR2 signaling effectively inhibited neutrophil recruitment, restored placental microvascular density, and alleviated fetal intrauterine growth restriction. This study revealed that the activation of placental tissue-resident macrophages is responsible for the aberrant neutrophil infiltration in the placenta following DENV-2 infection. Mechanistically, macrophages initiate the recruitment of self-amplifying neutrophils, and the CXCL2-CXCR2 signaling pathway plays a crucial role in this process. These results provide important information for the clinical development of therapeutic strategies against DENV-2-induced adverse pregnancy outcomes.

Extensive clinical cases and epidemiological analyses have indicated that symptomatic dengue virus (DENV) infection may lead to adverse outcomes during pregnancy; however, the precise mechanisms remain elusive.
Design and population
In this study, we generated a time-resolved single-cell atlas of placentas from interferon-alpha/beta receptor-deficient mice infected with DENV-2.
Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

气候变化对登革热的影响。

Impacts of climate change on dengue.
作者： Erin Mordecai, Mordecai E., Erin A. Mordecai
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究探讨了气候变化为何导致登革热和其他蚊媒疾病激增,并确定了理解气候和社会经济因素如何相互作用以改变登革热 传播 动态的关键知识差距。
设计与对象
该论文是一篇叙述性综述,综合了气候变化对登革热影响的证据,利用全球研究和荟萃分析来评估 传播 模式和风险因素。
核心方法
该综述采用荟萃分析方法来评估温度对登革热 发病率 的影响,整合气候和社会经济中介因素来模拟非线性关系。
主要结果
该评论报告称,气候变暖正在扩大美洲和亚洲的登革热负担,温度对登革热的影响是非线性的,并受到气候和社会经济因素的调节。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为气候变化正在通过复杂的相互作用重塑登革热 流行病学 的证据,强调需要采取综合气候适应策略来缓解 传播 的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了加强 媒介 控制、监测 和气候信息疾病负担预测的紧迫性,以增强气候变化中登革热的抵御能力。
局限与证据强度
该审查依赖于综合证据而不是原始数据,限制了因果推断和普遍性;由于纳入研究的异质性和潜在的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

Impacts of climate change on dengue.

Authors: Erin Mordecai, Mordecai E., Erin A. Mordecai
Original abstract (English)
Combined effects of hydrometeorological hazards and urbanisation on dengue risk in Brazil: a spatiotemporal modelling study. Climate-sensitive disease outbreaks in the aftermath of extreme climatic events: a scoping review. The impact of climate change on transmission season length: West Nile virus as a case study. A scoping review of mosquito vector range shifts: widespread expansions and evidence gaps in climate attribution. Climate change, extreme weather and socioeconomic change drive surges in dengue and other mosquito-borne infectious diseases. Strengthening dengue resilience in a changing climate requires improved vector control, surveillance, climate-informed disease burden projections, sustainable urban and environmental management, and stronger community engagement. Partnerships that span health, environment, climate, economic and community sectors are critical for creating climate-resilient systems that improve health, environment and well-being. Question and background
The study examines why climate change is driving surges in dengue and other mosquito-borne diseases, identifying a critical knowledge gap in understanding how climatic and socioeconomic factors interact to alter dengue transmission dynamics.
Design and population
The paper is a narrative review synthesizing evidence on climate change impacts on dengue, drawing on global studies and meta-analyses to assess transmission patterns and risk factors.
Core methods
The review employs a meta-analysis approach to evaluate temperature impacts on dengue incidence, integrating climatic and socioeconomic mediators to model nonlinear relationships.
Main results
The review reports that climate warming is expanding the dengue burden in the Americas and Asia, with temperature effects on dengue incidence being nonlinear and mediated by climatic and socioeconomic factors.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that climate change is reshaping dengue epidemiology through complex interactions, highlighting the need for integrated climate-resilient strategies to mitigate transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the urgency of strengthening vector control, surveillance, and climate-informed disease burden projections to build dengue resilience in a changing climate.
Limitations and evidence strength
The review relies on synthesized evidence rather than primary data, limiting causal inference and generalisability; the strength of evidence is moderate due to heterogeneity in included studies and potential confounding.

基于配体的虚拟筛选发现硫代吡啶并嘧啶酮衍生物作为别构nSMase2调节剂及抗病毒药物对西尼罗病毒的抑制剂

Ligand‐Based Virtual Screening Discovery of Thienopyrimidinone Derivatives as Allosteric nSMase2 Modulators and Antivirals Against West Nile Virus.
作者： Hadrián Álvarez-Fernández, Patricia Mingo-Casas, Ana-Belén Blázquez, Flavia Caridi, Miguel A Martín-Acebes, María-Jesús Pérez-Pérez, Eva-María Priego
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决正黄病毒属病毒(包括登革病毒)缺乏获批抗病毒药物疗法的问题,这些病毒构成了日益严峻的全球公共卫生威胁,每年导致数百万感染病例。
设计与对象
该研究采用基于配体的虚拟筛选活动,以DPTIP为模板配体筛选潜在nSMase2抑制剂,随后在细胞水平实验中对化合物进行针对登革病毒的评估。
核心方法
A ligand-based virtual screening (LBVS) approach was employed using DPTIP as a template to screen over 5 million ZINC20 compounds, followed by refinement through ADMET-properties prediction and molecular docking.
主要结果
化合物16被确定为新型nSMase2抑制剂,硫代吡啶并嘧啶酮衍生物在细胞水平实验中展现出对登革病毒的抗病毒药物活性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,硫代吡啶并嘧啶酮衍生物代表了nSMase2抑制的新型骨架,为开发针对黄病毒属病毒的广谱抗病毒药物疗法提供了有前景的策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持探索靶向宿主调节鞘脂代谢作为治疗登革病毒及其他相关黄病毒属病毒的治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外实验结果,且由于缺乏体内或临床数据,证据强度为中等。

Ligand‐Based Virtual Screening Discovery of Thienopyrimidinone Derivatives as Allosteric nSMase2 Modulators and Antivirals Against West Nile Virus.

Authors: Hadrián Álvarez-Fernández, Patricia Mingo-Casas, Ana-Belén Blázquez, Flavia Caridi, Miguel A Martín-Acebes, María-Jesús Pérez-Pérez, Eva-María Priego
Original abstract (English)
Orthoflaviviruses such as West Nile virus (WNV), Zika virus (ZIKV), or dengue virus (DENV) represent a growing global health threat, with millions of annual infections and no approved antiviral therapies. Targeting host pathways represents an alternative antiviral strategy that may overcome limitations associated with direct-acting antivirals, including resistance and narrow antiviral spectra. Neutral sphingomyelinase-2 (nSMase2) plays a central role in sphingolipid metabolism and has emerged as a host factor involved in the replication of several flaviviruses including WNV and ZIKV. Herein, we report a new family of thienopyrimidinone derivatives as nSMase2 inhibitors. A ligand-based virtual screening (LBVS) campaign using DPTIP as a template ligand identified 13 candidates from over 5 million ZINC20 compounds, which were refined through ADMET-properties prediction and molecular docking. Experimental evaluation identified compound 16 as a novel nSMase2 inhibitor. Rational design guided by computational affinity maps enabled the synthesis of several derivatives bearing a thienopyrimidinone core. These compounds inhibited nSMase2 and showed antiviral activity against WNV in cell‐based assays. These results identify thienopyrimidinone derivatives as a new scaffold for nSMase2 inhibition and support host-directed modulation of sphingolipid metabolism as a promising strategy for the development of broad-spectrum antivirals against flaviviruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of approved antiviral therapies for orthoflaviviruses, including Dengue virus, which pose a growing global health threat with millions of annual infections.
Design and population
The research involved a ligand-based virtual screening campaign using DPTIP as a template ligand to identify potential nSMase2 inhibitors, with subsequent experimental evaluation of the compounds against Dengue virus.
Core methods
A ligand-based virtual screening (LBVS) approach was employed using DPTIP as a template to screen over 5 million ZINC20 compounds, followed by refinement through ADMET-properties prediction and molecular docking.
Main results
Compound 16 was identified as a novel nSMase2 inhibitor, and the thienopyrimidinone derivatives demonstrated antiviral activity against Dengue virus in cell-based assays.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that thienopyrimidinone derivatives represent a new scaffold for nSMase2 inhibition, offering a promising strategy for developing broad-spectrum antivirals against flaviviruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results support the exploration of host-directed modulation of sphingolipid metabolism as a therapeutic approach for Dengue virus and other related flaviviruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro findings, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of in vivo or clinical data.

登革病毒单克隆抗体在成人患者中的安全性与初步疗效

Safety and Preliminary Efficacy of Dengue Monoclonal Antibody in Adult Patients
作者： Prasad S. Kulkarni, Anirudha Vyankatesh Potey, Sandeep Kumar Gupta, Aparna Kodre, Indraneel Basu, Vineet Shukla, Vijaykumar Barge, Renuka Munshi, Akhilesh Chandra Mishra, Rajeev Vadakkedath
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 暴发 的频率和强度正在增加。该研究旨在通过评估一种治疗方案来解决登革热缺乏特异性 治疗 的问题。
设计与对象
A phase 2, single-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled clinical trial was conducted from September 19, 2021, to May 13, 2023, at 12 hospitals in India. Study participants were aged 18 to 60 years with dengue and a history of fever onset within 48 hours.
核心方法
参与者通过交互式网络响应系统以1:1:1:1:1的比例随机分配,接受4种不同剂量水平的登革病毒单克隆抗体(3、5、7或9 mg/kg)或安慰剂,通过缓慢静脉输注给药。数据分析时间为2024年1月11日至2024年3月26日。
主要结果
共有250名参与者被随机分配,每组50名参与者。未出现与因果相关的严重不良事件。调整后的平均值(标准误)在24小时的病毒对数减少量在登革病毒单克隆抗体组显著高于安慰剂组。
解释与创新
登革-mAb(单克隆抗体)在5 mg/kg和7 mg/kg剂量下迅速降低了登革病毒血症和发热症状,这使其成为首个针对野生型登革病毒毒株在人体中显示出初步疗效的治疗药物。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,登革病毒单克隆抗体(Dengue-mAb)可能成为登革热的一种有前景的治疗选择,有望改善治疗策略并降低受影响人群的疾病严重程度。
局限与证据强度
该研究为2期试验,样本量有限,且没有长期随访,可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于研究设计和数据有限,证据不充分。

Safety and Preliminary Efficacy of Dengue Monoclonal Antibody in Adult Patients

Authors: Prasad S. Kulkarni, Anirudha Vyankatesh Potey, Sandeep Kumar Gupta, Aparna Kodre, Indraneel Basu, Vineet Shukla, Vijaykumar Barge, Renuka Munshi, Akhilesh Chandra Mishra, Rajeev Vadakkedath
Original abstract (English)
Importance Dengue outbreaks are increasing in frequency and intensity. In the absence of any specific treatment, a pan-serotype recombinant monoclonal antibody (Dengue-mAb) was tested in adult patients with dengue as a therapeutic option. Objective To assess the safety and effect of a single dose of Dengue-mAb on dengue viremia at different dose levels among patients with dengue. Design, Setting, and Participants A phase 2, single-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled clinical trial was conducted from September 19, 2021, to May 13, 2023, at 12 hospitals in India. Study participants were aged 18 to 60 years with dengue and a history of fever onset within 48 hours. Key exclusion criteria were severe dengue, hemoglobin less than 10 g/dL, absolute neutrophil count less than 500/mm 3 , total leukocyte count less than 1500/mm 3 , platelet count less than 50 000/mm 3 , and any other clinically significant disorders. Data were analyzed from January 11, 2024, to March 26, 2024. Interventions Participants were randomly allocated 1:1:1:1:1 using an interactive web response system to receive 1 of 4 dose levels of Dengue-mAb (3, 5, 7, or 9 mg/kg) or placebo by slow intravenous infusion. Main Outcomes and Measures Primary outcomes were reduction in viremia at 24 hours and causally related serious adverse events (SAEs). Secondary outcomes were RNAemia, fever, and hematologic and pharmacokinetic parameters. Findings A total of 250 participants were randomized, with 50 participants in each group. The overall median age was 30 years (IQR, 24-37 years), and 180 participants (72.3%) were male. There were no causally related SAEs. The adjusted mean (SE) viral log reduction at 24 hours was significantly higher with Dengue-mAb—from 1.93 (0.23) with 3 mg/kg ( P = .04) to 2.35 (0.27) with 9 mg/kg ( P = .003)—compared with placebo (1.29 [0.22]), using a mixed model for repeated measures. Participants with detectable viremia (n = 32) at baseline were negative for dengue virus by 8 hours when treated with a 5- to 9-mg/kg dose of Dengue-mAb compared with 72 hours with placebo. Median time to fever clearance was 3.5 (95% CI, 1.0-24.0) hours (hazard ratio [HR], 2.6 [95% CI, 1.0-6.4]) with Dengue-mAb 5 mg/kg (n = 14) and 2.0 (95% CI, 0.5-6.0) hours (HR, 2.8 [95% CI, 1.2-6.8]) with Dengue-mAb 7 mg/kg (n = 14), compared with 26.8 (95% CI, 1.0-50.5) hours with placebo (n = 12). At 24 hours, between 13 of 14 participants (92.9%; 95% CI, 66.1%-99.8%) with Dengue-mAb (3 mg/kg), 14 of 14 participants (100.0%; 95% CI, 76.8%-100.0%) with Dengue-mAb (5 mg/kg and 7 mg/kg), and 16 of 17 participants (94.1%; 95% CI 71.3%-99.9%) with Dengue-mAb (9 mg/kg); and 7 of 12 participants (58.3%; 95% CI, 27.7%-84.8%) with placebo had fever clearance. Conclusions and Relevance In this randomized clinical trial, Dengue-mAb was safe and well tolerated. Dengue-mAb rapidly reduced dengue viremia and fever at doses of 5 mg/kg and 7 mg/kg, making this the first therapeutic against wild-type dengue virus to show preliminary efficacy in humans. Trial Registration Clinical Trial Registry-India Identifier: CTRI/2021/07/035290
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Importance Dengue outbreaks are increasing in frequency and intensity. The study aimed to address the lack of specific treatments for dengue by evaluating a therapeutic option.
Design and population
A phase 2, single-blind, randomized, placebo-controlled clinical trial was conducted from September 19, 2021, to May 13, 2023, at 12 hospitals in India. Study participants were aged 18 to 60 years with dengue and a history of fever onset within 48 hours.
Core methods
Participants were randomly allocated 1:1:1:1:1 using an interactive web response system to receive 1 of 4 dose levels of Dengue-mAb (3, 5, 7, or 9 mg/kg) or placebo by slow intravenous infusion. Data were analyzed from January 11, 2024, to March 26, 2024.
Main results
A total of 250 participants were randomized, with 50 participants in each group. There were no causally related serious adverse events. The adjusted mean (SE) viral log reduction at 24 hours was significantly higher with Dengue-mAb compared with placebo.
Interpretation and novelty
Dengue-mAb rapidly reduced dengue viremia and fever at doses of 5 mg/kg and 7 mg/kg, making this the first therapeutic against wild-type dengue virus to show preliminary efficacy in humans.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that Dengue-mAb could be a promising therapeutic option for dengue, potentially improving treatment strategies and reducing disease severity in affected populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was a phase 2 trial with a limited sample size and no long-term follow-up, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is moderate due to the study design and limited data.

传播动态与疟疾-登革热共感染的最优控制:成本效益分析

Transmission Dynamics and Optimal Control of Malaria-Dengue Co-infection: A Cost-Effectiveness Analysis
作者： Francis Agono, Godwin Onuche Acheneje, Benedict Celestine Agbata, A.K. Awasthi, Hambeer Singh, Aseel Smerat, M. Manivel, Bolarinwa Bolaji
摘要中文翻译本研究构建了一个综合的确定性分隔模型,用于模拟疟疾-登革热共感染,该模型包含不同的媒介种群(疟疾媒介按蚊与登革热媒介伊蚊)、疾病特异性进展路径及共感染分隔。模型通过严格分析验证了正性、有界性、无病平衡与地方病平衡及基本再生数。应用最优控制理论评估了六种时间依赖性干预策略:蚊帐(ITNs)、室内残留喷洒(IRS)、环境管理、个人防护措施、治疗依从性及疫苗接种。模型参数基于巴西2011-2023年疟疾与登革热病例数据进行估计。敏感性分析识别出影响疾病传播的关键参数。成本效益分析采用感染避免比(IAR)、平均成本效益比(ACER)及增量成本效益比(ICER)显示,六种控制措施的联合实施(策略9)成本效益最高,可避免9,200例感染(减少35.38%),ACER为3,756.536。研究结果为在资源受限的共流行区设计经济高效的干预策略提供了关键指导。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在理解疟疾-登革热共感染的传播动态,并评估最优控制策略以减轻两种疾病的负担。研究目标是深入分析共感染的影响,并为制定成本效益干预措施提供指导。
设计与对象
本研究构建了一个确定性分隔模型,用于模拟疟疾-登革热共感染,该模型包含不同的媒介种群(疟疾媒介按蚊与登革热媒介伊蚊)及疾病特异性进展路径。模型分析基于巴西2011-2023年疟疾与登革热病例数据。
核心方法
本研究采用最优控制理论评估六种时间依赖性干预策略:蚊帐(ITNs)、室内残留喷洒(IRS)、环境管理、个人防护措施、治疗依从性及疫苗接种。通过敏感性分析识别影响疾病传播的关键参数。
主要结果
成本效益分析显示,六种控制措施的联合实施(策略9)成本效益最高,可避免9,200例感染(减少35.38%),平均成本效益比(ACER)为3,756.536。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了整合多种干预策略以实现疟疾-登革热混合感染最佳控制的重要性,为设计经济高效的公共卫生干预措施提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为在资源有限且疟疾与登革热共存的地区制定有针对性且成本效益的干预策略提供了关键指导,支持疾病控制的科学决策。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于确定性模型,并依赖历史数据,可能无法捕捉现实世界的变异性。由于分析仅基于摘要且存在未纳入的潜在偏倚,证据强度为中等。

Transmission Dynamics and Optimal Control of Malaria-Dengue Co-infection: A Cost-Effectiveness Analysis

Authors: Francis Agono, Godwin Onuche Acheneje, Benedict Celestine Agbata, A.K. Awasthi, Hambeer Singh, Aseel Smerat, M. Manivel, Bolarinwa Bolaji
Original abstract (English)
This study presents a comprehensive deterministic compartmental model for malaria-dengue co-infection incorporating distinct vector populations (Anopheles for malaria and Aedes for dengue), disease-specific progression pathways, and co-infection compartments. The model is rigorously analyzed to establish positivity, boundedness, disease-free and endemic equilibria, and basic reproduction numbers. Optimal control theory is applied to evaluate six time-dependent intervention strategies: insecticide-treated bed nets (ITNs), indoor residual spraying (IRS), environmental management, personal protection measures, treatment compliance, and vaccination. Model parameters are estimated using Brazilian malaria and dengue case data (2011-2023). Sensitivity analysis identifies key parameters influencing disease transmission. Cost-effectiveness analysis using infection averted ratio (IAR), average cost-effectiveness ratio (ACER), and incremental cost-effectiveness ratio (ICER) reveals that the combined implementation of all six control measures (Strategy 9) is most cost-effective, averting 9,200 infections (35.38% reduction) at an ACER of 3,756.536. These findings provide crucial guidance for designing economically efficient intervention strategies in resource-constrained co-endemic settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study addresses the need for understanding the transmission dynamics of malaria-dengue co-infection and evaluating optimal control strategies to mitigate the burden of these diseases. The research aims to provide insights into the co-infection's impact and guide the development of cost-effective interventions.
Design and population
A deterministic compartmental model is developed to simulate malaria-dengue co-infection, incorporating distinct vector populations (Anopheles for malaria and Aedes for dengue) and disease-specific progression pathways. The model is analyzed using Brazilian malaria and dengue case data from 2011 to 2023.
Core methods
The study employs optimal control theory to assess six time-dependent intervention strategies: insecticide-treated bed nets (ITNs), indoor residual spraying (IRS), environmental management, personal protection measures, treatment compliance, and vaccination. Sensitivity analysis is conducted to identify key parameters influencing disease transmission.
Main results
The cost-effectiveness analysis reveals that the combined implementation of all six control measures (Strategy 9) is most cost-effective, averting 9,200 infections (35.38% reduction) at an average cost-effectiveness ratio (ACER) of 3,756.536.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of integrating multiple intervention strategies to achieve optimal control of malaria-dengue co-infection, offering a novel approach to designing economically efficient public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide critical guidance for developing targeted and cost-effective intervention strategies in resource-limited regions where malaria and dengue co-exist, supporting informed decision-making for disease control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a deterministic model and relies on historical data, which may not capture real-world variability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the abstract-only nature of the analysis and the potential for unaccounted biases.

孟加拉国64个县区中登革热的时空分布:基于报告医院入院数据的回顾性生态分析

Spatiotemporal epidemiology of dengue fever across all 64 districts of Bangladesh: a retrospective ecological analysis of reported hospital admissions
作者： Istiaque Ahamed Nabed, Azka Touhida Daiby, Md. Muzahidul Islam, Abul Kashem Faruki Fahim, Tanzim Hayat
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解孟加拉国登革热的时空模式,因为医院入院病例已越来越多地扩展到历史上集中在达卡的区域。
设计与对象
本研究在孟加拉国所有64个县区进行了回顾性生态时空分析,使用了2022年1月1日至2025年12月31日的每日医院入院报告。
核心方法
时间模式通过描述性分析评估,结合季节性Mann-Kendall检验和Benjamini-Hochberg假发现率校正;空间聚集则通过Queen连通性权重下的全球Moran’s I、局部空间自相关指标(LISA)和Getis-Ord Gi*进行评估,并进行了k=6的最近邻敏感性分析。
主要结果
Annual reported admission rates per 100,000 population were computed using district mid-year population projections. Hospital admissions increased sharply from 2022 to 2023, declined substantially in 2024, and remained stable in 2025. Dhaka’s share of national admissions fell from 63.5% to 31.5%, while Barishal Division’s share increased from 5.1% to 21.1%. Barguna recorded the highest reported admission rate in 2025 (959.5 per 100,000 population).
解释与创新
这些发现表明,登革热入院病例正在从达卡向其他地区重新分布,2023年后多个南部县区出现重复集中现象,提示流行病学格局发生了变化。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为非传统登革热热点地区的针对性监测和应对策略提供了信息,特别是在南部县区,同时强调了需要区域性特定干预措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于分析基于医院报告的入院数据,且使用的是设施位置而非居住位置数据,这可能限制了空间模式的准确性。由于这些限制,证据具有建议性,但并非决定性。

Spatiotemporal epidemiology of dengue fever across all 64 districts of Bangladesh: a retrospective ecological analysis of reported hospital admissions

Authors: Istiaque Ahamed Nabed, Azka Touhida Daiby, Md. Muzahidul Islam, Abul Kashem Faruki Fahim, Tanzim Hayat
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh has experienced increasingly widespread dengue activity, with hospital admissions reported beyond the historical concentration in Dhaka. District-level evidence is needed to support preparedness, but nationwide-analyses combining temporal patterns, population-standardised reported admission rates, and spatial clustering remain limited. We conducted a retrospective ecological spatiotemporal analysis of reported dengue hospital admissions across all 64 districts of Bangladesh using daily Directorate General of Health Services Health Emergency Operations Centre reports from 1 January 2022 to 31 December 2025. The analytical dataset contained 93,504 district-day observations after imputing 19 missing dates using district-wise linear interpolation. Annual reported admission rates per 100,000 population were computed using district mid-year population projections from the 2022 census. Temporal patterns were assessed descriptively, with exploratory Seasonal Mann-Kendall assessment and Benjamini-Hochberg false-discovery-rate correction. Spatial clustering was evaluated using Global Moran’s I, Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA), and Getis-Ord Gi* under queen-contiguity weights, with K-nearest-neighbour (k = 6) sensitivity analysis. Additional sensitivity analyses examined the impact of missing-date handling. DGHS yearly totals reported 62,382 dengue admissions in 2022, 321,017 in 2023, 101,214 in 2024, and 102,861 in 2025. Corresponding totals in the interpolated dataset were 62,034, 320,943, 100,803, and 102,929, respectively, reflecting minor differences attributable to interpolation of missing dates. Reported admissions increased sharply from 2022 to 2023, declined substantially in 2024, and remained stable in 2025. Dhaka district’s share of national admissions fell from 63.5% to 31.5% between 2022 and 2025, while Barishal Division’s share increased from 5.1% to 21.1%. In 2025, Barguna recorded the highest reported admission rate (959.5 per 100,000 population). Spatial autocorrelation was strongest from 2023 onward, with weak positive autocorrelation detected in 2022. Dhaka was not identified as a statistically significant LISA or Gi* hotspot core, unlike spatially coherent southern clusters emerging 2023 onwards. Reported dengue hospital admissions show an apparent redistribution beyond Dhaka, with repeated concentration in several southern districts after 2023. Because the analysis is based on hospital-reported admissions, a study-period of four years, and facility-location rather than residence-location data, the findings should be interpreted as suggestive evidence of changing dengue-admission patterns.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the spatiotemporal patterns of dengue fever in Bangladesh, as hospital admissions have increasingly spread beyond historical concentrations in Dhaka.
Design and population
A retrospective ecological spatiotemporal analysis was conducted across all 64 districts of Bangladesh using daily hospital admission reports from 1 January 2022 to 31 December 2025.
Core methods
Temporal patterns were assessed descriptively with Seasonal Mann-Kendall tests and Benjamini-Hochberg false-discovery-rate correction, while spatial clustering was evaluated using Global Moran’s I, Local Indicators of Spatial Association (LISA), and Getis-Ord Gi* under queen-contiguity weights, with k = 6 sensitivity analysis.
Main results
Annual reported admission rates per 100,000 population were computed using district mid-year population projections. Hospital admissions increased sharply from 2022 to 2023, declined substantially in 2024, and remained stable in 2025. Dhaka’s share of national admissions fell from 63.5% to 31.5%, while Barishal Division’s share increased from 5.1% to 21.1%. Barguna recorded the highest reported admission rate in 2025 (959.5 per 100,000 population).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a redistribution of dengue admissions beyond Dhaka, with repeated concentration in several southern districts after 2023, indicating a shift in the epidemiological landscape.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results inform targeted surveillance and preparedness strategies in non-traditional dengue hotspots, particularly in southern districts, and highlight the need for regional-specific interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The analysis is based on hospital-reported admissions and facility-location rather than residence-location data, which may limit the accuracy of spatial patterns. The evidence is suggestive but not definitive due to these limitations.

护理个案:阿依西娅·帕里阿曼医院穆兹达利法病房2025年丁. N女士登革出血热(DHF)护理

Asuhan Keperawatan Pada Ny. N Dengan Dengue Haemoragic Fever (DHF) Di Ruangan Muzdalifah Rumah Sakit Aisyiyah Pariaman Tahun 2025
作者： Rahmahtul Fadillah, Mike Asmaria, Aulia Asman, Linda Marni
摘要中文翻译登革出血热(DHF)是由*埃及伊蚊*(Aedes aegypti)传播的疾病,患者以高热为特征。世界卫生组织(WHO)报告全球累计2,997,097例病例,死亡1,302例。2024年,阿依西娅·帕里阿曼医院共报告登革出血热(DHF)病例180例。本研究旨在使作者能够为登革出血热患者提供直接护理。研究于2025年5月20日至5月24日在阿依西娅·帕里阿曼医院穆兹达利法病房进行。采用病例研究设计,包括护理评估、诊断、干预、实施、评估与记录。研究对象为登革出血热患者丁. N女士;评估后确立的护理诊断为体温过高、血容量不足及营养缺乏风险。在为期四天的实施与评估期间,体温过高、血容量不足及营养缺乏风险等诊断均获解决。期望患者及其家属理解居家护理要点并实践预防措施——具体为保持环境清洁以避免登革热。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在通过为登革出血热(DHF)患者丁. N女士提供直接护理,满足其护理需求,改善健康结局并推广预防策略。
设计与对象
研究于2025年5月20日至5月24日在阿依西娅·帕里阿曼医院穆兹达利法病房进行,以确诊登革出血热的丁. N女士为单一研究对象。
核心方法
采用病例研究设计,包括护理评估、诊断、干预、实施、评估与记录,以满足患者特定健康需求。
主要结果
在为期四天的实施与评估期间,体温过高、血容量不足及营养缺乏风险等护理诊断均获解决。
解释与创新
该研究强调了直接护理在管理 DHF 中的重要性,并强调了环境清洁在预防该疾病中的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 DHF 患者的护理实践提供参考,并支持公共卫生部门在促进环境卫生以预防登革热方面所做的努力。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅为单一病例,结果不具普遍推广性;但为登革出血热患者护理流程提供了有价值的见解。

Asuhan Keperawatan Pada Ny. N Dengan Dengue Haemoragic Fever (DHF) Di Ruangan Muzdalifah Rumah Sakit Aisyiyah Pariaman Tahun 2025

Authors: Rahmahtul Fadillah, Mike Asmaria, Aulia Asman, Linda Marni
Original abstract (English)
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) is a disease transmitted by the *Aedes aegypti* mosquito, characterized by high fever in patients. The World Health Organization (WHO) has reported a global total of 2,997,097 cases and 1,302 deaths. In 2024, there were 180 cases of DHF at Aisyiyah Pariaman Hospital. The objective of this study was to enable the author to provide direct nursing care to a DHF patient. The study was conducted in the Muzdhalifah Ward of Aisyiyah Pariaman Hospital from May 20 to May 24, 2025. A case study design was employed, encompassing nursing assessment, diagnosis, intervention, implementation, evaluation, and documentation. The subject of the study was Mrs. N, who was suffering from DHF; following assessment, the identified nursing diagnoses were hyperthermia, hypovolemia, and risk for nutritional deficit. Over the four-day period of implementation and evaluation, the diagnoses of hyperthermia, , and risk for nutritional deficit were all resolved. It is hoped that the patient and her family will understand home care requirements and practice prevention—specifically by maintaining environmental cleanliness to avoid DHF.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the nursing care needs of patients with Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) by providing direct care to a specific patient, Mrs. N, to improve her health outcomes and promote prevention strategies.
Design and population
The study was conducted in the Muzdhalifah Ward of Aisyiyah Pariaman Hospital from May 20 to May 24, 2025, and focused on a single patient, Mrs. N, who was diagnosed with DHF.
Core methods
A case study design was used, which included nursing assessment, diagnosis, intervention, implementation, evaluation, and documentation to address the patient's specific health needs.
Main results
Over the four-day period of implementation and evaluation, the nursing diagnoses of hyperthermia, hypovolemia, and risk for nutritional deficit were all resolved.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the importance of direct nursing care in managing DHF and emphasizes the role of environmental cleanliness in preventing the disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform nursing practices for DHF patients and support public health efforts in promoting environmental hygiene to prevent dengue transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case and does not provide generalizable results; however, it offers valuable insights into the nursing care process for DHF patients.

Social Determinant Factors as Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever Prevention in Banjar Pedungan, South Denpasar

Authors: Anny Eka Pratiwi, Made Indra Wijaya
Original abstract (English)
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains an endemic health problem in Denpasar, with incidence rates increasing over the past five years, reaching 43.97 (2021), 109.87 (2022), 184.23 (2023), 126.01 (2024), and 169.32 per 100,000 population (2025). Cadres play an important role in the success of DHF prevention programs by facilitating information delivery, periodic monitoring, and community participation in preventive actions. This study aimed to evaluate community knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding DHF prevention in Banjar Pedungan, South Denpasar. A mixed-methods approach was employed using purposive sampling involving 74 householders in South Denpasar. The findings showed that 66.7% of families had good knowledge and attitudes, 61.9% had good knowledge and practices, and 76.3% had good attitudes and practices related to DHF prevention. Fogging implementation was adjusted based on regional evaluations of increasing DHF cases. These findings indicate that community knowledge, attitudes, and practices toward DHF prevention were generally good. The study recommends strengthening DHF prevention through effective social media programs, adequate funding, regular monitoring and evaluation, and increased human resources to optimize prevention efforts.

Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains an endemic health problem in Denpasar, with incidence rates increasing over the past five years, reaching 43.97 (2021), 109.87 (2022), 184.23 (2023), 126.01 (2024), and 169.32 per 100,000 population (2025).
Design and population

Core methods
The study aimed to evaluate community knowledge, attitudes, and practices regarding DHF prevention in Banjar Pedungan, South Denpasar.
Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
Fogging implementation was adjusted based on regional evaluations of increasing DHF cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

登革热 并发脑出血、急性呼吸衰竭和院内肺炎:A 病例报告

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report
作者： Ronak Shah, Parth R Shah, Mittal Sindhav
摘要中文翻译登革热 是一种由 登革病毒 引起的蚊媒病毒性疾病。大多数病例具有自限性病程,极少数情况下重症登革热会导致致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、一天感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。血清学检测证实登革热 IgM 呈阳性。格拉斯哥昏迷评分 (GCS) 从 15 分快速下降至 11 分,导致临床病程变得复杂,在肺部分泌物带血的情况下,需要在第二天进行气管插管。胸部高分辨率计算机断层扫描(CT)提示合并肺水肿。脑部CT显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血(IPH),周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室参数记录了严重的血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。第五天,气管内抽吸培养物中出现了广泛耐药的鲍曼不动杆菌,需要将抗菌治疗升级为美罗培南和多粘菌素。支持性治疗包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆(FFP)输注。患者在第九天成功拔管,并于第十二天出院回家。值得注意的是,在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血 (ICH)、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药 (MDR) 院内肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持以及针对 ICH 的保守治疗的靶向抗菌治疗仍然是良好结果的关键决定因素。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是一种蚊媒病毒性疾病,病程主要为自限性,但严重病例可导致罕见但致命的神经系统并发症,如脑炎和出血。该研究强调需要记录登革热感染的非典型和严重表现,以提高临床识别和管理。
设计与对象
这是一名 38 岁男性的 病例报告,他出现三天高烧、感觉改变、头痛和全身无力。患者接受了支持治疗,包括血小板和新鲜冰冻血浆输注。
核心方法
血清学检测显示登革热 IgM 阳性,证实了登革热 诊断。支持治疗包括输血,并在整个住院期间进行临床监测。
主要结果
该患者的格拉斯哥昏迷评分从 15 分下降至 11 分,由于肺部分泌物带血,需要在第二天进行气管插管。脑部CT扫描显示3.9×3.8×3.5厘米的脑实质出血,周围水肿,造成明显的占位效应。实验室检查结果包括严重血小板减少症(最低 0.6 lac/cumm)、凝血病、肝细胞损伤(SGOT/SGPT 峰值为 227/276 IU/L)以及 C 反应蛋白升高(85.6 mg/L)。
解释与创新
在免疫功能正常的年轻成人中同时发生登革热相关脑出血、急性低氧血症性呼吸衰竭和多重耐药医院肺炎是已发表文献中很少记录的三联征。
科研与公卫意义
该病例强调了早期识别神经功能恶化、及时进行神经影像学检查、积极的血液制品支持和靶向抗菌治疗在管理 重症登革热 并发症中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
作为单个 病例报告,证据的普遍性有限,无法建立因果关系。由于缺乏比较数据或更广泛的临床背景,证据强度较低。

Dengue Fever Complicated by Intracerebral Haemorrhage, Acute Respiratory Failure and Nosocomial Pneumonia: A Case Report

Authors: Ronak Shah, Parth R Shah, Mittal Sindhav
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness caused by the dengue virus. Majority of cases have self-limiting course, rarely severe dengue causes fatal neurological complications like encephalitis and haemorrhage. This is case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, one day of altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. Serological testing confirmed dengue IgM positivity. The clinical course was complicated by a rapid decline in Glasgow Coma Scale (GCS) from 15 to 11, necessitating endotracheal intubation on day two in the setting of blood-stained pulmonary secretions. Highresolution Computed Tomography (CT) of the thorax suggested consolidation with pulmonary oedema. CT of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm Intracerebral Parenchymal Haemorrhage (IPH) with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory parameters documented severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/ SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L. Endotracheal aspirate cultures grew extensively drug-resistant Acinetobacter baumannii on day five, requiring escalation of antimicrobial therapy to meropenem and polymyxin. Supportive management included platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma (FFP) transfusion. The patient was successfully extubated on day nine and discharged home on day twelve. Notably, the simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated Intracerebral Haemorrhage (ICH), acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and Multi-Drug Resistant (MDR) nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a triad that is rarely documented in the published literature. Early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy with conservative management of ICH remains critical determinants of a favourable outcome.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a mosquito-borne viral illness with a predominantly self-limiting course, but severe cases can lead to rare but fatal neurological complications such as encephalitis and haemorrhage. The study highlights the need to document atypical and severe presentations of dengue infection to improve clinical recognition and management.
Design and population
This is a case report of a 38-year-old male who presented with three days of high-grade fever, altered sensorium, headache, and generalised weakness. The patient received supportive management including platelet and Fresh Frozen Plasma transfusions.
Core methods
The diagnosis of dengue was confirmed by serological testing showing dengue IgM positivity. Supportive care included blood product transfusions, and clinical monitoring was performed throughout the hospital course.
Main results
The patient developed a Glasgow Coma Scale decline from 15 to 11, requiring endotracheal intubation on day two due to blood-stained pulmonary secretions. A CT scan of the brain revealed a 3.9×3.8×3.5 cm intracerebral parenchymal haemorrhage with surrounding oedema causing significant mass effect. Laboratory findings included severe thrombocytopaenia (nadir 0.6 lac/cumm), coagulopathy, hepatocellular injury with peak SGOT/SGPT of 227/276 IU/L, and elevated C-reactive protein at 85.6 mg/L.
Interpretation and novelty
The simultaneous occurrence of dengue-associated intracerebral haemorrhage, acute hypoxaemic respiratory failure, and multi-drug resistant nosocomial pneumonia in a young immunocompetent adult represents a rarely documented triad in the published literature.
Scientific and public-health significance
The case underscores the importance of early recognition of neurological deterioration, timely neuroimaging, aggressive blood product support, and targeted antimicrobial therapy in managing severe dengue complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a single case report, the evidence is limited in generalisability and cannot establish causality. The strength of evidence is low due to the absence of comparative data or broader clinical context.
