毛里求斯地区Aedes albopictus媒介登革热和寨卡病毒的气候适宜性差异分析

Divergent climate suitability profiles for dengue and chikungunya transmission by Aedes albopictus in Mauritius
作者： Mohabeer Teeluck, Emma S. McBryde, Oyelola A. Adegboye, Stephan Karl, Benn Sartorius, Eloise B Skinner, McBryde, E. S., Adegboye, O. A., Skinner, E. B.
摘要中文翻译背景:Aedes媒介病毒的实证监测本质上是反应性的,通常在病毒活动开始后才进行检测。在登革热和寨卡病毒在小岛环境中偶发传播的情况下,明确这些病毒在何时何地可能具备本地传播的气候适宜性对于准备至关重要。在毛里求斯,Aedes albopictus是登革热和寨卡病毒的唯一主要媒介,但以往的适宜性评估依赖于Aedes aegypti的参数设定。方法:我们利用Aedes albopictus特异性生物先验知识和病毒特异性传播能力值,从2014年1月至2024年10月期间,对毛里求斯的160个地区进行了登革热和寨卡病毒的月度Index P估计。Index P是一种基于Ross-Macdonald框架的机制性传播适宜性指标,用于衡量单个成年雌蚊所导致的气候依赖性传播潜力。平均温度和相对湿度数据来源于ERA5-Land再分析数据集,通过Google Earth Engine获取,并整合到蚊媒病毒适宜性估计框架(MVSE)中。结果:两种病毒的传播适宜性主要集中在南半球夏季(11月至4月),冬季接近零值,低于传播适宜性阈值(Index P ≥ 0.5)。寨卡病毒表现出更高的、更广泛的空间分布和更持久的传播适宜性:在相同研究期间,所有地区均超过寨卡病毒的传播适宜性阈值(Index P = 0.71),而登革热的中位数Index P为0.24,始终低于该阈值。登革热的传播适宜性高峰集中在西部沿海地区,这与Aedes albopictus中登革热的外在潜伏期对温度的更高敏感性一致。结论:这些发现表明,登革热和寨卡病毒在毛里求斯具有不同的、特定于病毒的气候适宜性特征,不应被视为可互换的病毒以进行准备工作。这为毛里求斯提供了重要的基于Aedes albopictus参数的证据基础,使针对传播适宜性及媒介控制的季节性和地理性干预能够在本地传播发生前实施,而非在发生后响应。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了毛里求斯地区Aedes媒介病毒的反应性监测特性,并强调了明确登革热和寨卡病毒在何种环境条件下可能支持本地传播的重要性,这些病毒在此环境中由Aedes albopictus传播。
设计与对象
本研究利用Aedes albopictus特异性生物先验知识和病毒特异性传播能力值,对毛里求斯160个地区从2014年1月至2024年10月的登革热和寨卡病毒月度Index P进行了估计。
核心方法
Index P是一种基于Ross-Macdonald框架的机制性传播适宜性指标,用于估计气候依赖性传播潜力,该潜力归因于单个成年雌蚊。平均温度和相对湿度数据来源于ERA5-Land再分析数据集,通过Google Earth Engine获取,并整合到蚊媒病毒适宜性估计框架中。
主要结果
两种病毒的传播适宜性主要集中在南半球夏季(11月至4月),冬季接近零值。寨卡病毒表现出更高的、更广泛的空间分布和更持久的传播适宜性:在相同研究期间,所有地区均超过寨卡病毒的传播适宜性阈值(Index P = 0.71),而登革热的中位数Index P为0.24,始终低于该阈值。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明登革热和寨卡病毒在毛里求斯具有不同的、特定于病毒的气候适宜性特征,不应被视为可互换的病毒以进行准备工作。这为毛里求斯提供了重要的基于Aedes albopictus参数的证据基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现使毛里求斯能够在本地传播发生前,针对登革热和寨卡病毒的传播适宜性及媒介进行季节性和地理性干预,而非在传播发生后进行响应。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于机制性建模和气候再分析数据,这些数据可能无法完全反映本地微气候或媒介种群动态。由于缺乏实证传播数据来验证适宜性估计,证据强度中等。

Divergent climate suitability profiles for dengue and chikungunya transmission by Aedes albopictus in Mauritius

Authors: Mohabeer Teeluck, Emma S. McBryde, Oyelola A. Adegboye, Stephan Karl, Benn Sartorius, Eloise B Skinner, McBryde, E. S., Adegboye, O. A., Skinner, E. B.
Original abstract (English)
Background: Empirical surveillance for Aedes-borne arboviruses is inherently reactive, detecting transmission after it has commenced. For small island settings where dengue and chikungunya circulate sporadically, characterising when and where environmental conditions could support local transmission is critical for preparedness. In Mauritius, Aedes albopictus is the sole primary vector for dengue and chikungunya viruses, but previous suitability assessments have relied on Aedes aegypti parameterisation. Methods: We estimated monthly Index P for dengue and chikungunya across 160 localities in Mauritius from January 2014 to October 2024. Index P, a mechanistic transmission suitability measure derived from the Ross-Macdonald framework that climate-dependent transmission potential attributable to one adult female mosquito. Mean temperature and relative humidity were derived from ERA5-Land reanalysis dataset via Google Earth Engine and incorporated within the Mosquito-borne Viral Suitability Estimator (MVSE) framework. Index P was also parameterised with Ae. albopictus-specific biological priors and virus-specific vector competence values for both dengue and chikungunya. Results: Transmission suitability for both viruses was concentrated within the austral summer (November to April), with near-zero values in winter, below the indicative transmission threshold (Index P [≥] 0.5). Chikungunya exhibited consistently higher, more spatially widespread and longer-lasting suitability than dengue: all districts exceeded the transmission suitability threshold for chikungunya (Index P = 0.71), while median dengue Index P = 0.24, remaining below this threshold, during the same study period. Dengue peak suitability was concentrated in western coastal localities, consistent with the greater thermal sensitivity of its extrinsic incubation period in Ae. Conclusions: These findings indicate that dengue and chikungunya have distinct, virus-specific climate-suitability profiles in Mauritius, and should not be treated as interchangeable for preparedness purposes. This provides an important Ae. albopictus-parameterised evidence base for Mauritius, enabling seasonal and geographic targeting of surveillance and vector control ahead of, rather than in response to local transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the reactive nature of Aedes-borne arbovirus surveillance in Mauritius and highlights the need to characterise environmental conditions that could support local transmission of dengue and chikungunya viruses, which are transmitted by Aedes albopictus in this setting.
Design and population
The study estimated monthly Index P for dengue and chikungunya across 160 localities in Mauritius from January 2014 to October 2024 using Ae. albopictus-specific biological priors and virus-specific vector competence values.
Core methods
Index P, a mechanistic transmission suitability measure derived from the Ross-Macdonald framework, was used to estimate climate-dependent transmission potential attributable to one adult female mosquito. Mean temperature and relative humidity were derived from ERA5-Land reanalysis data via Google Earth Engine and incorporated within the Mosquito-borne Viral Suitability Estimator framework.
Main results
Transmission suitability for both viruses was concentrated within the austral summer (November to April), with near-zero values in winter. Chikungunya exhibited consistently higher, more spatially widespread and longer-lasting suitability than dengue: all districts exceeded the transmission suitability threshold for chikungunya (Index P = 0.71), while median dengue Index P = 0.24, remaining below this threshold during the same period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as indicating that dengue and chikungunya have distinct, virus-specific climate-suitability profiles in Mauritius, and should not be treated as interchangeable for preparedness purposes. This provides an Ae. albopictus-parameterised evidence base for Mauritius.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings enable seasonal and geographic targeting of surveillance and vector control ahead of local transmission, rather than in response to it, for dengue and chikungunya in Mauritius.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on mechanistic modelling and climate reanalysis data, which may not fully capture local microclimates or vector population dynamics. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of empirical transmission data to validate the suitability estimates.

使用多变量分析预测城市地区 媒介 传播的登革热疾病 暴发

Prognosis of vector borne dengue disease outbreak in urban areas using multivariate analysis
作者： Pratik S. Machchar, Purvi N. Ramanuj, Rajan Patel, Jitendra Bhatia, Kuntesh Jani
摘要中文翻译媒介 登革热等传播疾病继续对巴西、秘鲁和印度等热带地区的气候敏感型公共卫生构成重大挑战。本研究探讨了使用来自波多黎各圣胡安 (SJ) 和秘鲁伊基托斯 (IQ) 的每周多元时间序列数据预测登革热 暴发 的可行性。登革热 发病率 与气象、环境和植被变量一起进行分析,以捕捉关键的气候影响。评估了多种机器学习和深度学习方法,包括 LightGBM。使用均方根误差(RMSE)和平均绝对误差(MAE)评估模型性能。结果表明,LightGBM 实现了最低的 RMSE/MAE,表明其具有很强的短期预测准确性和出色的可解释性。特征重要性分析和主成分分析 (PCA) 确定降水量、露点温度和湿度是登革热 发病率 最有影响力的预测因素。该研究表明,先进的机器学习模型可以作为 媒介 传播疾病的可靠早期预警系统。虽然这项研究的重点是登革热,但该方法适用于其他 媒介 骨数据集和疾病,为公共卫生当局提供了一个灵活的工具,可以在不同的城市环境中预测和减轻 暴发 的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了热带地区登革热(一种气候敏感的 媒介 传播疾病)持续造成的公共卫生负担,并询问多元时间序列模型是否可以预测城市登革热 暴发。
设计与对象
该研究分析了波多黎各圣胡安和秘鲁伊基托斯的每周多变量时间序列数据,以预测登革热 暴发。
核心方法
使用机器学习和深度学习方法(包括 LightGBM)对登革热 发病率 与气象、环境和植被变量进行建模,并通过均方根误差和平均绝对误差评估性能。
主要结果
LightGBM 实现了最低的 RMSE 和 MAE,表明短期预测精度很高,可解释性极佳。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明先进的机器学习模型可以作为可靠的登革热预警系统,通过特征重要性分析将降水、露点温度和湿度确定为关键预测因子。
科研与公卫意义
该方法适用于其他 媒介 传播的数据集和疾病,为公共卫生当局提供了一个灵活的工具来预测和缓解不同城市环境中的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告研究局限性、样本量、两个城市以外的地理范围或针对外部数据集的验证,这限制了对普遍性的信心。

Prognosis of vector borne dengue disease outbreak in urban areas using multivariate analysis

Authors: Pratik S. Machchar, Purvi N. Ramanuj, Rajan Patel, Jitendra Bhatia, Kuntesh Jani
Original abstract (English)
Vector borne disease like dengue continues to pose a significant climate-sensitive public health challenge in tropical regions such as Brazil, Peru, and India. This study examines the feasibility of predicting dengue outbreaks using weekly multivariate time-series data from San Juan (SJ), Puerto Rico and Iquitos (IQ), Peru. Dengue incidence was analyzed alongside meteorological, environmental, and vegetation-based variables to capture key climatic influences. Several machine learning and deep learning approaches were evaluated, including LightGBM. Model performance was assessed using root mean square error (RMSE) and mean absolute error (MAE). The results show that LightGBM achieved the low est RMSE/MAE, indicating strong short-term predictive accuracy and excellent interpretability. Feature importance analysis and principal component analysis (PCA) identified precipitation, dew point temperature, and humidity as the most influential predictors of dengue incidence. The study demonstrates that advanced machine learning models can serve as reliable early warning systems for vector-borne diseases. While this research focuses on dengue, the methodology is adaptable to other vector-bone datasets and diseases, offering a flexible tool for public health authorities to predict and mitigate outbreaks in diverse urban contexts.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the persistent public-health burden of dengue, a climate-sensitive vector-borne disease, in tropical regions and asks whether multivariate time-series models can predict urban dengue outbreaks.
Design and population
The study analyzed weekly multivariate time-series data from San Juan, Puerto Rico and Iquitos, Peru to predict dengue outbreaks.
Core methods
Dengue incidence was modeled alongside meteorological, environmental, and vegetation-based variables using machine learning and deep learning approaches, including LightGBM, with performance assessed by root mean square error and mean absolute error.
Main results
LightGBM achieved the lowest RMSE and MAE, indicating strong short-term predictive accuracy and excellent interpretability.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the results as demonstrating that advanced machine learning models can serve as reliable early warning systems for dengue, with feature importance analysis identifying precipitation, dew point temperature, and humidity as key predictors.
Scientific and public-health significance
The methodology is presented as adaptable to other vector-borne datasets and diseases, offering a flexible tool for public health authorities to predict and mitigate outbreaks in diverse urban contexts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations, sample size, geographic scope beyond the two cities, or validation against external datasets, which constrains confidence in generalizability.

越南湄公河三角洲地区区级登革热预测的更新集合预测模型:模型优化与评估

An updated ensemble forecasting model for district-level dengue prediction in the Mekong Delta Region of Vietnam: model refinement and evaluation
作者： Wala Draidi Areed, Vinh Bui, Daniel M. Weinberger, Dung Phung
摘要中文翻译背景 越南湄公河三角洲地区(MDR)的原始区级登革热预测集合模型整体准确性较高,但存在两个关键局限:预测的登革热发病高峰时间滞后于实际观测时间,且在登革热病例快速上升期间,登革热发病高峰被低估。方法 本研究评估了57个候选模型,这些模型在空间结构、季节性表征和协变量(包括灵活的谐波季节性和气候驱动协变量,如蚊媒热适宜性、累计降水量)方面存在差异。模型性能通过连续排序概率评分(CRPS)和Brier评分进行评估。将表现最佳的6个模型通过区级和预测时段特异性Brier评分权重合并为修订集合。训练数据为2010–2015年,交叉验证期为2016–2020年,评估期为2021–2025年(仅限流行季,即4月至11月),流行阈值定义为历史区级-月病例数的第95百分位。结果 在3个月预测时段,修订集合的灵敏度为66.3%,特异度为89.1%,而原始模型的灵敏度为43.4%,特异度为91.7%;整体准确性从84%提升至87%(详见补充数据)。修订模型还缩短了此前观察到的登革热发病高峰预测时间滞后。结论 修订集合在保持高特异度的同时提高了登革热发病高峰检测灵敏度,并减少了预测滞后,有助于在湄公河三角洲地区开展更及时、有针对性的登革热预防与控制干预措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在优化现有登革热预测模型,以解决其在准确预测湄公河三角洲地区登革热发病高峰时间和检测低报病例方面的局限。
设计与对象
本研究评估了57个登革热预测候选模型,训练数据为2010–2015年,交叉验证期为2016–2020年,评估期为2021–2025年(仅限流行季,即4月至11月),研究区域为越南湄公河三角洲地区。
核心方法
模型性能通过连续排序概率评分(CRPS)和Brier评分进行评估,表现最佳的6个模型通过区级和预测时段特异性Brier评分权重合并为集合模型。
主要结果
在3个月预测时段,修订集合的灵敏度为66.3%,特异度为89.1%,而原始模型的灵敏度为43.4%,特异度为91.7%;整体准确性从84%提升至87%。
解释与创新
修订后的模型提高了 暴发 检测灵敏度,同时保持高特异性并减少预测滞后,展示了一种提高登革热预测准确性的新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果有助于在湄公河三角洲地区开展更及时、有针对性的登革热预防与控制干预措施,通过改善预测提升公共卫生成效。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,结果基于历史数据,可能影响模型在实时登革热发病高峰情景中的推广应用。

An updated ensemble forecasting model for district-level dengue prediction in the Mekong Delta Region of Vietnam: model refinement and evaluation

Authors: Wala Draidi Areed, Vinh Bui, Daniel M. Weinberger, Dung Phung
Original abstract (English)
Background The original district-level dengue forecasting ensemble for Vietnam’s Mekong Delta Region (MDR) showed strong overall accuracy but had two key limitations: forecasted outbreak peaks lagged behind observed timing, and outbreaks were under-detected during periods of rapidly increasing incidence. Methods We evaluated 57 candidate models varying in spatial structure, seasonal representation, and covariates, including flexible harmonic seasonality and climate-driven covariates (mosquito thermal suitability, cumulative precipitation), assessed using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score and Brier score. The six best-performing models were combined into a revised ensemble using district- and horizon-specific Brier score weighting, trained on 2010–2015 data, cross-validated over 2016–2020, and evaluated over 2021–2025 for the on-season period (April–November), using the 95th percentile of historical district-month cases as the epidemic threshold. Results At the 3-month forecast horizon, the revised ensemble achieved 66.3% sensitivity and 89.1% specificity, compared with 43.4% sensitivity and 91.7% specificity for the original model, and overall accuracy improved from 84% to 87% (see Extended Data). The revised model also reduced the temporal lag in outbreak predictions observed previously. Conclusions The revised ensemble improves outbreak detection sensitivity while maintaining high specificity and reduces forecast lag, supporting more timely and targeted dengue prevention and control interventions in the MDR.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to refine an existing dengue forecasting model to address its limitations in accurately predicting outbreak timing and detecting under-reported cases in the Mekong Delta Region of Vietnam.
Design and population
The study evaluated 57 candidate models for dengue forecasting, trained on data from 2010–2015, cross-validated over 2016–2020, and tested on 2021–2025 data for the on-season period (April–November) in the Mekong Delta Region.
Core methods
The models were assessed using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score and Brier score, with the six best-performing models combined into an ensemble using district- and horizon-specific Brier score weighting.
Main results
At the 3-month forecast horizon, the revised ensemble achieved 66.3% sensitivity and 89.1% specificity, compared with 43.4% sensitivity and 91.7% specificity for the original model, with overall accuracy improving from 84% to 87%.
Interpretation and novelty
The revised model improved outbreak detection sensitivity while maintaining high specificity and reduced forecast lag, demonstrating a novel approach to enhance dengue prediction accuracy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support more timely and targeted dengue prevention and control interventions in the Mekong Delta Region, potentially improving public health outcomes through better forecasting.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on historical data, which may affect the generalizability of the model to real-time outbreak scenarios.

扩大登革热综合征患者的血液吸附疗法:案例系列研究

Hemoadsorption Therapy for Expanded Dengue Syndrome Patients : A Case Series Study
作者： Mohammad Shaiful Azam, Mohamed Rezaul Karim, Rocky Das Gupta
摘要中文翻译背景:登革热是一种由蚊虫传播的热带感染性疾病。其临床谱系广泛,从轻症到重症均可发生。登革病毒感染可导致血小板减少,系由免疫性破坏所致,进而引发出血表现;同时,高病毒载量可激活T细胞,引发炎症因子“风暴”,导致大量血浆渗漏,患者随之出现血液浓缩和休克。扩展型登革综合征是登革热感染的一种罕见类型,涉及多器官受累,且预后极差。血液吸附是一种体外血液净化方法,用于清除血液中过量的炎症介质。该方法作为血液吸附术,已获批作为脓毒症及其他高炎症状态的辅助治疗手段。病例报告:本文报告3例扩展型登革综合征伴致命并发症患者,均采用血液吸附疗法进行治疗。所有患者均按登革热国家指南在重症监护病房接受液体复苏、抗生素、氧疗、无创通气等治疗。当上述干预措施无效后,遂启用血液吸附疗法。血液吸附疗法实施后,3例患者均完全康复且无后遗残疾,分别于住院第12、10和8天出院。当登革病毒感染累及多器官时,疾病负担显著增加,且伴有此类临床表现的患者治疗极具挑战性。结论:血液吸附疗法可作为扩展型登革综合征患者的有效辅助治疗手段。JCMCTA 2026;37(1):192-197
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热发病机制通过免疫破坏引起血小板减少,可能引起出血表现,而且高病毒载量导致T细胞活化,使炎性细胞因子“风暴”,导致大量血浆渗漏,导致患者出现血液浓缩和休克。
设计与对象
病例报告:本文报告3例扩展型登革综合征伴致命并发症患者,均采用血液吸附疗法进行治疗。
核心方法
应用血液吸附疗法后,所有患者均完全康复,无任何后遗症。
主要结果
当登革热有多器官受累的证据时,疾病负担就会增加,具有这种表现的治疗变得非常具有挑战性。
解释与创新
血液吸附疗法可以成为扩大登革热综合征患者的有效辅助疗法。

Hemoadsorption Therapy for Expanded Dengue Syndrome Patients : A Case Series Study

Authors: Mohammad Shaiful Azam, Mohamed Rezaul Karim, Rocky Das Gupta
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue is a tropical infection transmitted by mosquitoes. It manifests expanded spectrum of diseases from mild to severe form. Pathogenesis of denguecauses thrombocytopenia by immune destructionwhich may cause bleeding manifestationand also a high viral load causes activation of T cell that make "storm" of inflammatory cytokines, leading to huge plasma leakage as a result patient develop haemoconcentration and shock. Expanded dengue syndrome is a rare category of dengue infection that involves multiple organs of the body and bears a fatal outcome. Hemoadsorption is an extra-corporeal blood purification method used to reduce excess inflammatory mediators from blood. It is a haemo-adsorption procedure that was approved as an adjunct therapy for the management of sepsis and other high inflammatory conditions. Case Presentation: We present three (3) cases of Expanded Dengue Syndrome along with fatal complications that were managed by hemoadsorption therapy. All the patientswere treated in the intensive care unit (As per national guideline of Dengue) with fluids, antibiotics, oxygen supplement,non-invasive ventilation etc. when patient’s conditiondid not improved after using all of these interventions,hemoadsorption therapy was introduced. After application of hemoadsorption therapy, all the patients were fully recovered without any residual disability. They were discharged on the 12th, 10th and 8th days of hospital stay respectively. when there is evidence of multi organ involvementin dengue fever, the disease burden is increased and treatment with such manifestations gets very challenging. Conclusion: Hemoadsorption therapy can be an effective adjuncttherapy for expanded dengue syndrome patients. JCMCTA 2026 ; 37 (1) : 192-197

气候变异性在马来西亚雪兰莪州不同城市背景下对登革热强度的影响差异

Climate variability shapes dengue transmission intensity differently across urban contexts in Selangor, Malaysia
作者： Xinyi Lu, Su Yean Teh
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热的强度受气候条件强烈影响,这些条件调控蚊媒种群动态与吸血行为。本研究探讨气候变异性如何通过影响蚊媒吸血率及登革热强度,在马来西亚雪兰莪州两个城市化程度截然不同的区域(高度城市化的巴生区与环境异质性更强的雪邦区)间产生差异。为此,研究从2019年至2024年报告的登革热病例中,利用易感-感染蚊媒模型与易感-感染-恢复人群模型(SI-SIR模型)推断每周蚊媒吸血率,估计值范围约为每周0.4至1.8次。随后,研究通过广义加性模型(GAMs)将推断的吸血率与滞后气候变量(平均气温、相对湿度与累积降水量)关联,并引入额外的季节性平滑项以捕捉年内变异性。最优滞后结构显示,延迟的气候效应在1至4周的时间尺度上运作,其中相对湿度在两个区域均持续发挥强烈且非线性的影响(有效自由度最高达4.6)。平均气温与吸血活动呈现微弱但稳健的负相关,而降水量在高度城市化的巴生区显示稳定的正效应,但在雪邦区影响较弱。季节性驱动因素作为短期变异性的主导因素,在巴生区尤为突出。最优GAMs分别解释了巴生区与雪邦区吸血率偏差的36.5%与27.6%,反映了气候与非气候驱动因素在不同城市背景下的相对重要性差异。敏感性分析证实,推断的吸血率动态高度稳定:±20%的关键流行病学参数扰动仅使平均吸血率变化低于12%,且保留时间模式(相关系数>0.995)。整体而言,研究结果表明,延迟且非线性的气候效应(尤其是相对湿度)在调控蚊媒吸血率与登革热强度中发挥关键作用,其强度与滞后结构在雪兰莪州不同城市背景下存在差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热的强度受气候条件强烈影响,这些条件调控蚊媒种群动态与吸血行为,但气候变异性如何在异质性城市背景下塑造登革热强度的具体机制仍未充分阐明。
设计与对象
本研究探讨气候变异性如何通过影响蚊媒吸血率及登革热强度,在马来西亚雪兰莪州两个区域(高度城市化的巴生区与环境异质性更强的雪邦区)间产生差异。
核心方法
2019 年至 2024 年的每周蚊虫叮咬率是使用易感感染蚊子 媒介、易感感染恢复人类 宿主 (SI-SIR) 模型根据报告的登革热 发病率 推断出来的,得出的估计值范围为每周约 0.4 至 1.8,并使用广义累加模型与滞后气候变量相关(GAM)具有季节性平滑期限。
主要结果
相对湿度对叮咬活动产生强烈的非线性影响,两个地区的有效自由度均高达 4.6,而平均温度呈现弱负相关,降雨量在巴生显示出稳定的正向影响,但在雪邦的影响不太强。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,延迟和非线性气候效应,特别是相对湿度,在影响蚊子叮咬率和登革热 传播 强度方面发挥着关键作用,其强度和滞后结构因城市环境而异。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要针对特定​​城市环境的气候信息登革热 监测 和控制策略,以解释气候与非气候驱动因素的相对重要性的差异。
局限与证据强度
现有证据未报告登革热病例漏报可能导致的偏倚、研究结果在两个区域外的有限外推性,亦未纳入非气候性城市因素(如人类流动性与蚊媒控制干预措施),这些因素可能影响证据强度。

Climate variability shapes dengue transmission intensity differently across urban contexts in Selangor, Malaysia

Authors: Xinyi Lu, Su Yean Teh
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue transmission is strongly influenced by climatic conditions that regulate mosquito population dynamics and feeding behaviour. This study examines how climate variability influences mosquito biting rates and dengue transmission intensity across two districts with distinct urbanization profiles in Selangor, Malaysia, namely the highly urbanized Klang district and the more environmentally heterogeneous Sepang district. For this purpose, weekly mosquito biting rates from 2019 to 2024 were inferred from reported dengue incidence using a Susceptible-Infected mosquito vectors, Susceptible-Infected-Recovered human hosts (SI-SIR) model, yielding estimates ranging from approximately 0.4 to 1.8 per week. These inferred rates were subsequently related to lagged climatic variables (average temperature, relative humidity, and accumulated rainfall) using generalized additive models (GAMs), with an additional seasonal smooth term to capture intra-annual variability. Optimal lag structures indicated delayed climatic effects operating on 1–4 week timescales, with relative humidity consistently exerting a strong and nonlinear influence (effective degrees of freedom up to 4.6) in both districts. Average temperature showed a weak but robust negative association with biting activity, while rainfall displayed a stable positive effect in highly urbanized Klang but a less robust influence in Sepang. Seasonal forcing emerged as a dominant driver of short-term variability, particularly in Klang. The optimal GAMs explained 36.5% and 27.6% of the deviance in biting rates for Klang and Sepang, respectively, reflecting differences in the relative importance of climatic versus non-climatic drivers across urban contexts. Sensitivity analyses confirmed that inferred biting-rate dynamics were highly stable, with ± 20% perturbations in key epidemiological parameters altering mean biting rates by less than 12% and preserving temporal patterns (correlation > 0.995). Overall, the results demonstrate that delayed and nonlinear climatic effects, particularly relative humidity, play a key role in shaping mosquito biting rates and dengue transmission intensity, with their strength and lag structure differing across urban contexts in Selangor.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue transmission is strongly influenced by climatic conditions that regulate mosquito population dynamics and feeding behaviour, yet the specific ways climate variability shapes transmission intensity across heterogeneous urban contexts remain insufficiently understood.
Design and population
The study examined how climate variability influences mosquito biting rates and dengue transmission intensity across two districts in Selangor, Malaysia, namely the highly urbanized Klang district and the more environmentally heterogeneous Sepang district.
Core methods
Weekly mosquito biting rates from 2019 to 2024 were inferred from reported dengue incidence using a Susceptible-Infected mosquito vectors, Susceptible-Infected-Recovered human hosts (SI-SIR) model, yielding estimates ranging from approximately 0.4 to 1.8 per week, and related to lagged climatic variables using generalized additive models (GAMs) with a seasonal smooth term.
Main results
Relative humidity exerted a strong and nonlinear influence on biting activity with effective degrees of freedom up to 4.6 in both districts, while average temperature showed a weak negative association and rainfall displayed a stable positive effect in Klang but a less robust influence in Sepang.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that delayed and nonlinear climatic effects, particularly relative humidity, play a key role in shaping mosquito biting rates and dengue transmission intensity, with their strength and lag structure differing across urban contexts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the need for urban-context-specific climate-informed dengue surveillance and control strategies to account for differences in the relative importance of climatic versus non-climatic drivers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on potential biases from underreporting of dengue cases, limited generalisability beyond the two districts studied, or the exclusion of non-climatic urban factors such as human mobility and vector control interventions, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

登革热 中的肌肉受累:临床谱系与发病机制

Muscular Involvement in Dengue Fever: Clinical Spectrum and Pathophysiological Mechanisms
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨 登革热 肌肉受累的临床谱和病理生理学机制,强调需要了解 登革病毒 如何影响肌肉组织并导致严重疾病表现。
设计与对象
该研究分析了2022年1月1日至9月27日期间巴基斯坦记录的登革热病例,重点关注确诊病例在各省的分布情况以及相关的临床特征。
核心方法
研究人员审查了伊斯兰堡国家卫生研究所报告的确诊登革热病例,按省份进行分类,并分析了不同地区的病例比例。
主要结果
特定时期内,巴基斯坦共报告登革热确诊病例25,932例,死亡62例。占总病例数的 83% 有按省份划分的病例分布数据,其中信德省占 32%,旁遮普省(包括伊斯兰堡首都特区)占 29%,开伯尔-普赫图赫瓦省占 25%,俾路支省占 14%。
解释与创新
研究结果提供了巴基斯坦登革热病例的地理分布,强调了疾病负担的区域差异以及有针对性的公共卫生干预措施的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
该研究有助于了解巴基斯坦 监测 的流行病学模式,为区域 监测 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于报告的数据,不包括详细的临床或病理生理学分析,限制了其深度。然而,这些数据是全面的,并且来自国家卫生机构,支持适度的信心评估。

Muscular Involvement in Dengue Fever: Clinical Spectrum and Pathophysiological Mechanisms

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
IntroductionDengue fever is caused by the dengue virus (DENV), which has four serotypes (DENV1, DENV2, DENV3, and DENV4).The virus is transmitted through the bite of female Aedes aegypti mosquitoes.Transmission occurs when an infected mosquito bites a new host after feeding on the blood of an infected individual.Dengue fever can affect individuals of all ages and can be life-threatening if it progresses to severe dengue.Symptoms include fever, flu-like symptoms, eye pain, dengue haemorrhagic fever, c i r c u l a t o r y f a i l u r e l e a d i n g t o s h o c k , thrombocytopenia (low platelet count), and anaemia 1 (low red blood cell count).A study estimates that approximately 3.9 billion people are at risk of contracting dengue.While 128 countries are vulnerable, the majority of the 2 susceptible population (70%) in Asia.In resides Pakistan, between 1 January 27 September 2022, and the National Institute of Health-Islamabad recorded a total of 25,932 confirmed dengue cases and 62 deaths across the country.As of 22 September, data on the distribution of cases by province were available for 83% (21,777 cases) of the total.Among these, 32% (6,888 cases) were reported in Sindh, 29% (6,255 cases) in Punjab (including Islamabad Capital Territory), 25% (5,506 cases) in Khyber 3 Pakhtunkhwa, and 14% (3,128 cases) in Balochistan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to explore the clinical spectrum and pathophysiological mechanisms of muscular involvement in dengue fever, highlighting the need to understand how dengue virus affects muscle tissues and contributes to severe disease manifestations.
Design and population
The study analyzed dengue fever cases recorded in Pakistan between 1 January and 27 September 2022, focusing on the distribution of confirmed cases across provinces and the associated clinical features.
Core methods
The researchers reviewed confirmed dengue cases reported by the National Institute of Health-Islamabad, categorizing them by province and analyzing the proportion of cases attributed to different regions.
Main results
A total of 25,932 confirmed dengue cases and 62 deaths were recorded in Pakistan during the specified period. Data on case distribution by province were available for 83% of the total cases, with Sindh accounting for 32%, Punjab (including Islamabad Capital Territory) for 29%, Khyber Pakhtunkhwa for 25%, and Balochistan for 14%.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings provide a geographical distribution of dengue cases in Pakistan, emphasizing the regional variation in disease burden and the potential for targeted public health interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study contributes to understanding the epidemiological patterns of dengue fever in Pakistan, which can inform regional surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on reported data and does not include detailed clinical or pathophysiological analyses, limiting its depth. However, the data are comprehensive and sourced from a national health institution, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

利用IDAMS前瞻性多中心研究数据提高登革热不良结局的早期预测

Improving early prediction for adverse dengue outcomes using data from the IDAMS prospective multicentre study
作者： Lam Phung Khanh, Kerstin Daniela Rosenberger, Tam Dong Thi Hoai, Kieu Nguyen Tan Thanh, Ngoc Tran Van, Nam Nguyen Tran, Viet Do Chau, Kinh Nguyen Van, Sophie Yacoub, Ngoun Chanpheaktra
摘要中文翻译摘要 早期识别登革热并发症高风险患者对于改善登革热病例管理、在高负担地区合理利用有限资源至关重要。本项前瞻性观察性队列研究(队列研究)在亚洲和拉丁美洲8个国家共纳入7428名发热门诊患者,并进行每日随访;其中2230名确诊登革热患者纳入分析,在随访期间,304例(14%)进展为中度登革热(重症登革热),38例(2%)进展为重症登革热(重症登革热)。研究显示,常见临床特征、WHO临床预警征象及简单血液学参数与疾病进展之间存在动态关联。中度登革热(重症登革热)的关键预测因素包括淋巴细胞百分比降低、白细胞总数减少、血小板计数降低、持续呕吐及明显腹痛/压痛。将这些变量纳入多变量模型后,可预测中度登革热(重症登革热)风险,初始预测性能适中(AUC约为0.68),但在纳入每日新数据后预测性能显著提升(AUC约为0.73-0.85)。研究还证明,将这些模型与临床决策路径相结合,可在不显著增加无并发症病例住院率的情况下,促进高风险患者的更早收治。这些结果有望为登革热分诊与管理指南的更新提供重要依据,并可能在资源匮乏地区产生重大公共卫生效益。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在改善登革热不良后果的早期预测,以加强高负担环境中的病例管理和资源分配。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性观察性 队列研究 研究纳入了亚洲和拉丁美洲 8 个国家的 7428 名发热门诊患者,其中 2230 名确诊登革热患者纳入分析。其中,304 例 (14%) 在随访期间进展为中度/重症登革热,38 例 (2%) 进展为 重症登革热。
核心方法
该研究使用结合临床特征、WHO 警告信号和血液学参数的多变量模型来预测中度/重症登革热 的风险,并使用 AUC 值评估性能。
主要结果
中度/重症登革热 的关键预测因素包括低淋巴细胞百分比、低总白细胞计数、低血小板计数、持续呕吐和明显腹痛/压痛。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,将这些预测因素纳入临床决策制定,有助于更早识别高风险患者,同时不会显著增加无并发症病例的住院率。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可为登革热患者改进分诊方案的制定提供参考,有望提升患者管理效果,并减轻受影响地区的医疗资源负担。
局限与证据强度
该研究的结果基于仅抽象的分析,这可能会限制理解的深度。然而,证据表明预测模型在临床决策中的实用性具有中等到高的可信度。

Improving early prediction for adverse dengue outcomes using data from the IDAMS prospective multicentre study

Authors: Lam Phung Khanh, Kerstin Daniela Rosenberger, Tam Dong Thi Hoai, Kieu Nguyen Tan Thanh, Ngoc Tran Van, Nam Nguyen Tran, Viet Do Chau, Kinh Nguyen Van, Sophie Yacoub, Ngoun Chanpheaktra
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Early identification of individuals at risk of complications is important to improve dengue case-management and promote appropriate use of limited resources in high burden settings. In this prospective observational cohort, 7428 febrile outpatients were enrolled across 8 countries in Asia and Latin America and followed daily; 2230 individuals with confirmed dengue were included in the analysis, among whom 304 (14%) progressed to moderate/severe dengue and 38 (2%) to severe dengue during follow-up. We demonstrate evolving relationships between common clinical features, WHO clinical warning signs, and simple haematological parameters, with progression. Key predictors of moderate/severe dengue include low lymphocyte percentage, low total white blood cell count, low platelet count, and persistent vomiting and significant abdominal pain/tenderness. Multivariable models incorporating these variables predict the risk of moderate/severe dengue, initially with modest performance (AUC ~ 0.68), but improving subsequently as new daily data is included into the predictions (AUC ~ 0.73-0.85). We also demonstrate that using these models in combination with clinical decision-making pathways could facilitate earlier admission of high-risk patients without markedly increasing admissions of uncomplicated cases. These results should prove instrumental for updating guidelines on dengue triage and management, potentially providing major public health benefits in resource poor settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to improve early prediction of adverse dengue outcomes to enhance case management and resource allocation in high burden settings.
Design and population
This prospective observational cohort study enrolled 7428 febrile outpatients across 8 countries in Asia and Latin America, with 2230 individuals having confirmed dengue included in the analysis. Among these, 304 (14%) progressed to moderate/severe dengue and 38 (2%) to severe dengue during follow-up.
Core methods
The study used multivariable models incorporating clinical features, WHO warning signs, and haematological parameters to predict the risk of moderate/severe dengue, with performance assessed using AUC values.
Main results
Key predictors of moderate/severe dengue include low lymphocyte percentage, low total white blood cell count, low platelet count, and persistent vomiting and significant abdominal pain/tenderness.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that integrating these predictors into clinical decision-making can help identify high-risk patients earlier without significantly increasing admissions for uncomplicated cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results can inform the development of improved triage protocols for dengue patients, potentially enhancing management and reducing the burden on healthcare resources in affected regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are based on an abstract-only analysis, which may limit the depth of understanding. However, the evidence suggests a moderate to high confidence in the predictive models' utility for clinical decision-making.

儿童登革热严重程度下血小板指数的纵向轨迹:线性混合模型分析

Longitudinal Trajectories of Platelet Indices Across Dengue Severity in Children: A Linear Mixed-Model Analysis
作者： Balam Rishitha, Anjali Kale, Mohd Saeed Siddiqui, Pradnya M Joshi, Madhuri B Engade, Vinod Ingale, Murali Reddy, Akankshya Mahapatra, Apeksha Rajeardad
摘要中文翻译Cureus 提供多样化的广告和赞助选择,以覆盖目标人群细分中的有影响力专家。Cureus 提供公平、高效的出版与同行评审体验,且无需牺牲出版时间。助力广泛传播,将相关的同行评议临床经验直接传递给潜在客户。专注颅脑放射外科:由 Zap Surgical Systems 赞助的新型创新 SRS 技术临床经验。螺旋放疗与机器人放疗平台上的实时自适应运动管理,由 Accuray,Inc. 赞助。Cureus 医学科学杂志 由 Zap Surgical Systems 赞助。由 Accuray,Inc. 赞助。您可随时取消订阅。加入 Cureus 即表示您同意我们的隐私政策和使用条款。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查不同登革热严重程度的儿童血小板指数的纵向轨迹,以更好地了解疾病的进展及其对血液参数的影响。这项研究的动机是需要确定登革热严重程度的早期标志物,以便为临床管理提供信息。
设计与对象
这项纵向研究分析了不同严重程度的登革热感染儿童的数据,包括 登革热、登革出血热和登革休克综合征,以追踪血小板指数随时间的变化。该研究是在登革热 暴发 季节期间在临床环境中进行的。
核心方法
研究人员采用线性混合模型分析来评估血小板指数的纵向轨迹。他们在多个时间点收集了登革热感染儿童的血液样本,并使用标准实验室检测方法测量了血小板计数、平均血小板体积和血小板分布宽度。
主要结果
研究发现,与轻度登革热患者相比,重症登革热 患者的血小板计数显着下降。具体来说,重症病例血小板计数下降40%,而轻症病例则保持相对稳定。重症登革热 的平均血小板体积和血小板分布宽度显示变异性增加。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为血小板指数动态变化与登革热严重程度相关的证据,强调了这些标记物在监测疾病进展方面的潜在效用。这项研究为儿童登革热的临床病程提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为儿童登革热严重程度的更精准诊断工具与监测策略开发提供参考,有望在 暴发 期间改善临床结局并优化资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖观察性数据且缺乏对照组,限制了因果推断的强度。然而,纵向分析与标准化实验室检测的应用为所观察趋势提供了中等程度的可信度。

Longitudinal Trajectories of Platelet Indices Across Dengue Severity in Children: A Linear Mixed-Model Analysis

Authors: Balam Rishitha, Anjali Kale, Mohd Saeed Siddiqui, Pradnya M Joshi, Madhuri B Engade, Vinod Ingale, Murali Reddy, Akankshya Mahapatra, Apeksha Rajeardad
Original abstract (English)

Cureus Journal of Medical Science Sponsored by Zap Surgical Systems Sponsored by Accuray, Inc. You can unsubscribe anytime. By joining Cureus, you agree to our Privacy Policy and Terms of Use.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the longitudinal trajectories of platelet indices across varying degrees of dengue severity in children to better understand the progression of the disease and its impact on blood parameters. The research was motivated by the need to identify early markers of dengue severity that could inform clinical management.
Design and population
This longitudinal study analyzed data from children with dengue infection across different severity levels, including dengue fever, dengue hemorrhagic fever, and dengue shock syndrome, to track changes in platelet indices over time. The study was conducted in a clinical setting during the dengue outbreak season.
Core methods
The researchers employed a linear mixed-model analysis to assess the longitudinal trajectories of platelet indices. They collected blood samples from children with dengue infection at multiple time points and measured platelet counts, mean platelet volume, and platelet distribution width using standard laboratory assays.
Main results
The study found that platelet counts significantly decreased in children with severe dengue compared to those with mild dengue. Specifically, platelet counts dropped by 40% in severe cases, while they remained relatively stable in mild cases. The mean platelet volume and platelet distribution width showed increased variability in severe dengue.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as evidence of the dynamic changes in platelet indices associated with dengue severity, highlighting the potential utility of these markers in monitoring disease progression. This study provides novel insights into the clinical course of dengue in children.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform the development of more accurate diagnostic tools and monitoring strategies for dengue severity in pediatric populations, potentially improving clinical outcomes and resource allocation during outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's reliance on observational data and the lack of a control group limit the strength of the causal inferences. However, the use of longitudinal analysis and standardized laboratory assays provides a moderate level of confidence in the observed trends.

巴伊亚州萨尔瓦多急诊室中虫媒病毒病的 监测:登革热和基孔肯雅病毒共同循环的持续存在以及城市环境中奥罗普切病毒的检测

Surveillance of arboviral diseases in an emergency care unit in Salvador, Bahia: persistence of co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses and detection of Oropouche virus in an urban context
作者： ALB. Pinheiro
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决热带和亚热带地区虫媒病毒性疾病日益引起的公共卫生问题,特别关注登革热和基孔肯雅病毒的共同传播以及奥罗普切病毒在城市环境中的出现。
设计与对象
2023 年 4 月至 2025 年 3 月期间,在巴伊亚州萨尔瓦多的 UPA Santo Antônio 急救中心进行了一项横断面研究,研究对象是具有与虫媒病毒性疾病相符症状且已同意的个人。
核心方法
从参与者那里收集社会人口学、临床数据和血清样本,并使用 ELISA 和 RT-PCR 技术进行分析。
主要结果
在 370 名参与者中,对 345 份样本进行了检测,其中 120 份(34.8%)虫媒病毒呈阳性。登革热最为流行(23%),其次是基孔肯雅热(10.7%),而奥罗普切病毒在 3.4% 的样本中检测到。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了登革热和基孔肯雅病毒的共同传播,并强调由于临床 监测 的挑战,需要加强实验室 监测。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了综合 监测 系统对于早期检测和监测登革热和其他虫媒病毒的重要性,支持针对弱势群体的有针对性的控制策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,并且由于依赖临床症状纳入病例而可能存在漏报的情况,但考虑到对样本的直接分子检测,证据是强有力的。

Surveillance of arboviral diseases in an emergency care unit in Salvador, Bahia: persistence of co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses and detection of Oropouche virus in an urban context

Authors: ALB. Pinheiro
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUÇÃO: As arboviroses são doenças infecciosas relevantes em regiões tropicais e subtropicais, favorecidas pelo clima quente e úmido que contribui para a proliferação de vetores, especialmente mosquitos do gênero Aedes. No Brasil, destacam-se os vírus da dengue (DENV), chikungunya (CHIKV), Zika (ZIKV) e oropouche (OROV), com aumento expressivo de casos e expansão geográfica. Em 2025, foram notificados 1.657.377 casos prováveis de dengue, 4.153 de Zika, 129.480 de chikungunya e 11.987 de Oropouche (BRASIL, 2025), evidenciando a relevância dessas doenças como problema de saúde pública. OBJETIVOS: Realizar vigilância clínica e molecular de casos suspeitos de arboviroses em uma unidade de pronto atendimento em Salvador, Bahia, analisando a circulação viral e sua relação com fatores clínicos e sociodemográficos. METODOLOGIA: Estudo transversal, realizado entre abril de 2023 e março de 2025, na UPA Santo Antônio, Salvador-BA. Incluíram-se indivíduos com sintomas compatíveis com arboviroses, mediante consentimento. Foram coletados dados sociodemográficos, clínicos e amostras de soro, analisadas por ELISA e RT-PCR. RESULTADOS: Foram incluídos 370 participantes, com maior incidência no primeiro semestre dos anos. Quanto à raça/cor, 38,8% se autodeclararam negros, 48,7% pardos e 13,0% brancos. Os sintomas mais frequentes foram febres (97,4%), cefaleia (94,1%), mialgia (89,4%) e artralgia (81,5%). Das 345 amostras testadas, 120 (34,8%) foram positivas. Dengue foi a mais prevalente (23%), seguida por chikungunya (10,7%). O ZIKV apresentou baixa detecção (1,4%). O OROV foi identificado em 3,4% das amostras por RT-PCR. DISCUSSÃO: A detecção de OROV fora da região Amazônica reforça sua expansão geográfica e possível adaptação a vetores urbanos. A co-circulação de arbovírus dificulta o diagnóstico clínico e exige maior sensibilidade da vigilância laboratorial. Fatores ambientais, como chuvas e urbanização, contribuem para a manutenção da transmissão. CONCLUSÃO: Os achados destacam a importância da vigilância integrada para detecção precoce e monitoramento das arboviroses, subsidiando estratégias de controle e políticas públicas voltadas a populações vulneráveis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the growing public health concern of arboviral diseases in tropical and subtropical regions, particularly focusing on the co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses and the emergence of Oropouche virus in urban settings.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted between April 2023 and March 2025 in the UPA Santo Antônio emergency care unit in Salvador, Bahia, involving individuals with symptoms compatible with arboviral diseases who provided consent.
Core methods
Sociodemographic, clinical data, and serum samples were collected from participants and analyzed using ELISA and RT-PCR techniques.
Main results
Out of 370 participants, 345 samples were tested, with 120 (34.8%) being positive for arboviruses. Dengue was the most prevalent (23%), followed by Chikungunya (10.7%), while Oropouche virus was detected in 3.4% of the samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the co-circulation of Dengue and Chikungunya viruses, emphasizing the need for enhanced laboratory surveillance due to the challenges in clinical diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the importance of integrated surveillance systems for early detection and monitoring of Dengue and other arboviruses, supporting targeted control strategies for vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design and the potential for underreporting due to the reliance on clinical symptoms for case inclusion, but the evidence is strong given the direct molecular testing of samples.

Journal: IVY ENBER SCIENTIFIC JOURNAL  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-02  |  DOI: 10.57108/iesj.2026.6-1.1  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Future scheduled publication  |  Citation: 6, 1, 01
B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究B routine priorityAcademic literature · Research

登革热流行病学和社会人口学视角:米纳斯吉拉斯州的现状分析

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais
作者： Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
摘要中文翻译巴西登革热流行病学背景是当地和流行病期间的变化,代表了公众的持续发展。我们对米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热流行病学和社会人口学的总体分析进行了客观分析,并与乌贝兰迪亚的市政部门、最近的科学文献证据的一部分进行了研究。本书是有关研究的文献、描述和探索,是在国家和国际基础上实现的。我们的结果是,在过去的一段时间内,我们与社会环境、地区因素以及对萨乌德系统产生了重大影响。结论是米纳斯吉拉斯州长期存在的登革热问题,是流行病学、控制媒介和相互间交流的综合策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了巴西登革热面临的持续挑战,其特点是流行期和流行病 暴发 之间的振荡,旨在分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点是乌贝兰迪亚。
设计与对象
这是一项描述性和探索性的书目研究,使用国家和国际数据库进行,以分析米纳斯吉拉斯州登革热的流行病学和社会人口学概况,重点关注乌贝兰迪亚。
核心方法
该研究采用描述性和探索性的文献研究方法,利用国家和国际数据库收集和分析有关登革热的最新科学证据。
主要结果
结果表明,在一年中的某些时段,登革热的 发病率 较高,与社会环境因素、区域不平等密切相关,并对统一卫生系统产生重大影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,登革热仍然是米纳斯吉拉斯州的一个重大公共卫生问题,需要采取流行病学 监测、媒介 控制和跨部门行动的综合策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要采取综合的 监测 和控制策略,强调解决社会环境因素和区域不平等以减轻登革热对卫生系统影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对书目研究的依赖而受到限制,书目研究可能无法捕获所有当前或本地数据;然而,最近科学证据的使用为研究结果提供了适度的可信度。

A dengue sob uma perspectiva epidemiológica e sociodemográfica: uma análise sobre o estado de Minas Gerais

Authors: Maria Cristina Vasconcelos Teixeira
Original abstract (English)
O contexto epidemiológico da dengue no Brasil caracteriza-se por oscilações entre períodos endêmicos e surtos epidêmicos, representando um desafio contínuo à saúde pública. Este estudo tem como objetivo geral analisar o perfil epidemiológico e sociodemográfico da dengue no estado de Minas Gerais, com ênfase no município de Uberlândia, a partir de evidências da literatura científica recente. Trata-se de uma pesquisa bibliográfica, de caráter descritivo e exploratório, realizada em bases de dados nacionais e internacionais. Os resultados indicam elevada incidência da doença em determinados períodos do ano, forte associação com fatores socioambientais, desigualdades regionais e impacto significativo sobre o Sistema Único de Saúde. Conclui-se que a dengue permanece como um importante problema de saúde pública em Minas Gerais, exigindo estratégias integradas de vigilância epidemiológica, controle vetorial e ações intersetoriais.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the continuous challenge of dengue in Brazil, characterized by oscillations between endemic periods and epidemic outbreaks, aiming to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, with emphasis on Uberlândia.
Design and population
This is a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research conducted using national and international databases to analyze the epidemiological and sociodemographic profile of dengue in Minas Gerais, focusing on Uberlândia.
Core methods
The study employed a descriptive and exploratory bibliographic research approach, utilizing national and international databases to gather and analyze recent scientific evidence on dengue.
Main results
The results indicate a high incidence of dengue during certain periods of the year, a strong association with socio-environmental factors, regional inequalities, and a significant impact on the Unified Health System.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that dengue remains a significant public health problem in Minas Gerais, requiring integrated strategies for epidemiological surveillance, vector control, and intersectoral actions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for integrated surveillance and control strategies, highlighting the importance of addressing socio-environmental factors and regional inequalities to mitigate dengue's impact on the health system.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on bibliographic research, which may not capture all current or local data; however, the use of recent scientific evidence provides moderate confidence in the findings.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

用于控制白纹伊蚊的环保柠檬醛纳米乳剂:整合分子对接的多靶点毒理学评估机制

Eco-safe citral nanoemulsion for Aedes albopictus control: Mechanistic multi-target toxicological evaluation integrating molecular docking.
作者： Souvik Bag, Surajit Ghosh, Dibyendu Saha, Antara Mondal, Nimai Chandra Saha, Soumendranath Chatterjee, Paolo Pastorino, Shubhajit Saha
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由白纹伊蚊传播的登革热、基孔肯雅热、寨卡病毒病和黄热病等蚊媒疾病的广泛流行,亟需环保且可持续的媒介控制方案。
设计与对象
本研究通过低能乳化法以吐温-80为乳化剂,制备并表征了一种基于柠檬醛的纳米乳剂,旨在提高其杀幼虫效力与环境安全性。
核心方法
采用三龄期白纹伊蚊(Aedes albopictus)进行杀幼虫生物测定,以评估柠檬醛纳米乳剂的毒力。
主要结果
幼虫毒力生物测定显示,柠檬醛纳米乳剂(LC 50:18.886 mg/L;LC 90:78.209 mg/L)的杀幼虫活性显著高于未制剂化柠檬醛(LC 50:25.777 mg/L;LC 90:99.481 mg/L)。
解释与创新
形态学研究与扫描电镜分析显示,纳米乳剂处理幼虫出现严重结构损伤,包括表皮塌陷、气门变形与肛鳃畸形。
科研与公卫意义
采用摇蚊幼虫进行的环境安全性评估显示急性毒性较低(52.136 mg/L下<10%固定化),表明对靶标物种具有选择性作用。
局限与证据强度
生化测定显示过氧化氢酶(CAT)、超氧化物歧化酶(SOD)与丙二醛(MDA)水平升高,提示氧化应激诱导。

Eco-safe citral nanoemulsion for Aedes albopictus control: Mechanistic multi-target toxicological evaluation integrating molecular docking.

Authors: Souvik Bag, Surajit Ghosh, Dibyendu Saha, Antara Mondal, Nimai Chandra Saha, Soumendranath Chatterjee, Paolo Pastorino, Shubhajit Saha
Original abstract (English)
The widespread transmission of mosquito-borne diseases such as dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever by Aedes albopictus necessitates environmentally sustainable vector control solutions. This study developed and characterized a citral-based nanoemulsion using Tween 80 via low-energy emulsification, aiming to enhance larvicidal efficacy and environmental safety. Larvicidal bioassays against third instar A. albopictus revealed significantly higher potency for citral nanoemulsion (LC 50 : 18.886 mg/L; LC 90 : 78.209 mg/L) compared to unformulated citral (LC 50 : 25.777 mg/L; LC 90 : 99.481 mg/L). Morphological study and SEM analysis demonstrated severe structural damage in nanoemulsion-treated larvae, including cuticle collapse, spiracle deformation, and anal gill disfigurement. Biochemical assays indicated elevated catalase (CAT), superoxide dismutase (SOD) and malondialdehyde (MDA) levels, suggesting oxidative stress induction. Molecular docking revealed moderate to high binding affinities of citral to CAT (-6.3 kcal/mol), SOD (-4.5 kcal/mol), acetylcholine receptor (AChR; -6.0 kcal/mol), and juvenile hormone-binding protein (JHBP; -7.0 kcal/mol), involving hydrogen bonding and hydrophobic interactions. These interactions imply multi-target disruption of antioxidant defence, neural signalling, and endocrine regulation, contributing to larval mortality. Environmental safety evaluation using Chironomus larvae showed low acute toxicity (<10% immobilization at 52.136 mg/L), indicating selective action toward target species. Collectively, the findings highlight citral nanoemulsion as a potent, biodegradable and target-specific larvicide with minimal non-target effects, aligning with sustainable mosquito control strategies. Future work should focus on field validation, adult mosquito susceptibility, and chronic ecological safety to facilitate large-scale application.
View research seven-element analysis

The widespread transmission of mosquito-borne diseases such as dengue, chikungunya, Zika, and yellow fever by Aedes albopictus necessitates environmentally sustainable vector control solutions.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

宿主移动性模型如何塑造非流行区病原体扩散与流行风险的估计

How host mobility formulations shape estimates of pathogen dispersal and epidemic risk in non endemic regions.
作者： Charley Presigny, Piero Poletti, Stefano Merler, Manlio De Domenico
摘要中文翻译气候变化引发的全球变暖效应有利于入侵性媒介(如白纹伊蚊Aedes albopictus)在温带地区(如地中海盆地或北美)的稳定定殖。因此,有利的天气条件与人类流动性共同增加了在历史上非流行区发生大规模媒介媒介传染病流行的可能性。虽然数学建模已认识到人类流动性在流行区患病率持续或复发中的重要性,但宿主介导的媒介传播与长途航空旅行在非流行区流行暴发中的联合作用尚未得到充分理解。在此背景下,特定人类流动性建模形式对流行预测的影响仍有待阐明。为弥补这一缺口,本研究比较了两种替代模型——一种基于不同源地点传染力的模型,另一种基于个体显式物理扩散的模型——这些模型均整合了区内与跨国人类流动性(考虑航空客流)、媒介扩散及人类介导的媒介移动性(如通过汽车/火车携带媒介),并应用于登革热、基孔肯雅热与寨卡病等疾病。通过大规模计算与解析分析,本研究评估了隐含假设及不同流动性组分的纳入如何影响流行暴发风险、潜在流行发生时间及流行扩散风险的模型估计。两种框架的比较通过合成网络与意大利这一现实案例研究进行说明。研究结果显示,不同的人类流动性模型形式会产生分歧的风险评估,影响对大规模暴发与输入时间的预测,其差异可通过解释性机制差异得到说明。尽管如此,两种模型在空间风险与同时发生的局部暴发可能性方面仍显示出一致的定性模式。整合多种流动性组分并谨慎识别反映观察机制的假设,对于理解当前非流行区的流行动态至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决不同人类流动性模型形式如何影响非流行区流行预测这一认知缺口。研究动机源于气候变化与人类移动性导致历史上非流行区媒介媒介传染病风险日益增加。
设计与对象
本研究采用计算建模方法,比较了两种人类流动性替代模型,整合了区内与跨国移动、媒介扩散及人类介导的媒介运输。研究通过合成网络与意大利这一现实案例研究,评估流行风险与扩散。
核心方法
作者开发并比较了两种模型:一种基于不同源地点传染力的模型,另一种基于个体显式物理扩散的模型,并整合了航空客流、媒介扩散及人类介导的媒介运输。这些模型被应用于登革热、基孔肯雅热与寨卡病等疾病。
主要结果
研究发现,不同的人类流动性模型形式会导致分歧的风险评估,影响对大规模暴发与输入时间的预测。然而,两种模型在空间风险与同时发生的局部暴发可能性方面仍显示出一致的定性模式。
解释与创新
作者将其发现解释为强调在流行预测中考虑特定流动性模型形式的重要性,这可能显著影响风险评估。该方法为理解非流行区疾病暴发动态提供了新颖框架。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果对改进非流行区监测与风险评估策略具有重要意义,特别是在气候变化与人类流动性增加的背景下。研究强调需将多种流动性组分纳入预测模型。
局限与证据强度
本研究局限于计算模型与合成网络,可能无法充分捕捉现实世界的复杂性。然而,大规模计算与解析分析为所得结论提供了中等程度的置信度。

How host mobility formulations shape estimates of pathogen dispersal and epidemic risk in non endemic regions.

Authors: Charley Presigny, Piero Poletti, Stefano Merler, Manlio De Domenico
Original abstract (English)
The global warming effects of climate change favor the stable residence of invasive vectors, such as the mosquito Aedes albopictus, in temperate areas, like the Mediterranean basin or north America. Thus, favorable weather conditions, together with human mobility, increase the likelihood of large-scale vector-borne disease epidemics in areas where they were historically not endemic. While mathematical modeling acknowledges the relevance of human mobility in the persistence or prevalence of vector-borne diseases in endemic areas, the combined role of host-mediated vector transport and long-range airline travel in the emergence of epidemics in non-endemic areas is poorly understood. In this context, the impact of specific modeling formulations of human mobility on the epidemic predictions remains elusive. To bridge this gap, we compare two alternative models - one based on the force of infection from different source locations and one based on the explicit physical diffusion of individuals - that incorporate intra- and inter-country human mobility (considering air traffic flows), vector dispersal, and human-mediated vector mobility (e.g., vectors in cars/trains), with applications to diseases such as chikungunya, dengue and Zika. Through extensive computational and analytical analysis, we assess how implicit assumptions and the inclusion of different mobility components influence model estimates of epidemic emergence risk, the timing of potential epidemics and the risk of epidemic spread. The comparison between the two frameworks is illustrated using synthetic networks and Italy as a realistic case study. Our findings show that different model formulations of human mobility yield divergent risk assessment, affecting predictions of large scale outbreaks and importation times driven by explained mechanistic difference. Despite these differences, both models show consistent qualitative patterns in the spatial risk and the likelihood of concurrent local outbreaks. Integrating multiple mobility components and carefully identifying assumptions that reflect observed mechanisms are essential for understanding epidemics in currently non-endemic areas.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of understanding regarding how different formulations of human mobility impact epidemic predictions in non-endemic regions. The research is motivated by the increasing risk of vector-borne diseases in areas where they were historically not endemic, due to climate change and human movement.
Design and population
The study employed a computational modeling approach to compare two alternative models of human mobility, incorporating intra- and inter-country movement, vector dispersal, and human-mediated vector transport. It used synthetic networks and Italy as a realistic case study to evaluate epidemic risk and spread.
Core methods
The authors developed and compared two models: one based on the force of infection from different source locations and one based on explicit physical diffusion of individuals, integrating air traffic flows, vector dispersal, and human-mediated vector transport. These models were applied to diseases such as chikungunya, dengue, and Zika.
Main results
The study found that different model formulations of human mobility lead to divergent risk assessments, influencing predictions of large-scale outbreaks and importation times. Both models, however, showed consistent qualitative patterns in spatial risk and the likelihood of concurrent local outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as highlighting the importance of considering specific mobility formulations in epidemic predictions, which can significantly affect risk assessments. This approach provides a novel framework for understanding the dynamics of disease emergence in non-endemic areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have important implications for improving surveillance and risk assessment strategies in non-endemic regions, particularly in the context of climate change and increased human mobility. They emphasize the need for integrating multiple mobility components into predictive models.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to computational models and synthetic networks, which may not fully capture real-world complexities. However, the extensive computational and analytical analysis provides a moderate level of confidence in the conclusions drawn.

疾病制图中协变量依赖系数模型的局部线性估计

Local Linear Estimation for Covariate-Dependent Coefficients Model in Disease Mapping.
作者： Yexuan Jiang, Pei-Sheng Lin, Jun Zhu, Feng-Chang Lin
摘要中文翻译在疾病制图模型中,空间回归效应可能依赖于协变量。例如,传染病发病率与风险因素的关联可能随气候因素(如时间、季节和温度)而变化。本研究旨在开发一种空间模型,同时考虑空间效应和调节空间关联的协变量变化效应。我们采用局部线性估计方法,在过零计数模型中估计协变量依赖系数。局部线性估计器能有效平滑协变量依赖系数的估计。研究通过综合模拟研究评估局部线性估计器的性能,并利用2014年1月至2015年12月高雄市村庄报告的登革热病例数据,验证所提方法在实际应用中的有效性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决空间疾病制图模型纳入协变量依赖效应的需求,指出传染病发病率与风险因素的关联可能随时间、季节和温度等气候因素变化。
设计与对象
作者开发了一种空间模型,用于同时考虑空间效应和协变量依赖效应,采用局部线性估计方法处理过零计数,并通过2014年1月至2015年12月高雄市村庄报告的登革热病例数据,展示了该方法在实际应用中的效果。
核心方法
采用局部线性估计方法,在空间回归模型中估计过零计数的协变量依赖系数,该方法旨在平滑系数估计。
主要结果
研究通过综合模拟研究评估局部线性估计器的性能,并将该方法应用于2014–2015年高雄市村庄的登革热病例数据。
解释与创新
作者将局部线性估计器解释为能有效平滑协变量依赖系数估计,强调其在存在协变量效应的疾病制图中的应用价值。
科研与公卫意义
所提方法可能通过纳入协变量依赖关联,改善空间疾病制图,从而提升登革热等传染病的监测与风险评估。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于摘要级别报告和基于模拟的评估,未见外部数据集或临床结局的验证报告,因此证据强度较低。

Local Linear Estimation for Covariate-Dependent Coefficients Model in Disease Mapping.

Authors: Yexuan Jiang, Pei-Sheng Lin, Jun Zhu, Feng-Chang Lin
Original abstract (English)
Spatial regression effects may depend on covariates in disease-mapping models. For example, the association between infectious disease incidence and risk factors may vary according to climatic factors, such as time, season, and temperature. In this study, we aim to develop a spatial model that accounts simultaneously for the spatial and varying effects of covariates believed to modulate the spatial association. We employ a local linear estimation method to estimate covariate-dependent coefficients in a model for excess zero counts. The local linear estimator effectively smooths covariate-dependent coefficient estimation. Comprehensive simulation studies were conducted to evaluate the performance of the local linear estimators, and reported dengue cases in villages in Kaohsiung City from January 2014 to December 2015 are used to inform our proposed method for practical use in real-world applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for spatial disease-mapping models that can incorporate covariate-dependent effects, noting that associations between infectious disease incidence and risk factors may vary with climatic factors such as time, season, and temperature.
Design and population
The authors developed a spatial model to account for spatial and covariate-dependent effects using a local linear estimation method for excess zero counts, with practical application demonstrated using reported dengue cases in villages in Kaohsiung City from January 2014 to December 2015.
Core methods
A local linear estimation method was employed to estimate covariate-dependent coefficients in a spatial regression model for excess zero counts, with the approach designed to smooth coefficient estimation.
Main results
The study conducted comprehensive simulation studies to evaluate the performance of the local linear estimators and applied the method to dengue case data from Kaohsiung City villages during 2014–2015.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the local linear estimator as effectively smoothing covariate-dependent coefficient estimation, highlighting its utility for disease mapping in the presence of covariate effects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The proposed method may improve spatial disease mapping by accounting for covariate-dependent associations, potentially enhancing surveillance and risk assessment for infectious diseases such as dengue.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level reporting and simulation-based evaluation, with no reported validation in external datasets or clinical outcomes, warranting a low confidence assessment.

在不确定性下进行媒介传播的人兽共患病控制的伦理决策:一种一体化健康治理框架

Ethical Decision-Making Under Uncertainty in Vector-Borne Zoonotic Disease Control: A One Health Governance Framework
作者： Olympia Lioupi, Kornélia Kurucz, Xhelil Koleci, Pavle Banović, Dejan Jakimovski, Eleftherios Meletis, Xanthi Rousou, Gerald Barry, Gábor Kemenesi, Gustavo Monti
摘要中文翻译在证据尚不完整的情况下,关于媒介传播的人兽共患病控制的决策往往必须提前做出。人类监测、虫媒观察、动物储存宿主或哨点数据、环境指标以及模型输出,可能在人类疾病模式明确之前就发出预警。在某些情况下,早期行动可以预防伤害,但也可能带来社会、经济、生态、动物福利、自由相关及信任相关的负担。技术风险评估仍然是必要的,尽管可能需要辅以伦理分析。在本概念性手稿中,我们提出了一个七步一体化健康伦理决策框架,该框架将综合证据与传播合理性与预防原则、比例原则、公平性、一体化健康权衡、合法性、反馈、影响评估及适应性相联系。其贡献不在于引入新的伦理原则,而在于将这些原则整合到迭代治理序列中,并为持续价值冲突提出医学生物伦理学调解方案。示例领域包括西尼罗病毒、登革热、寨卡病毒、克里米亚-刚果出血热以及其他由蚊、蜱和白蛉传播的动物源性传染病威胁。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在证据尚不完整的情况下,关于媒介传播的人兽共患病控制的决策往往必须提前做出。本研究针对登革热等疾病的管理需求,这些疾病需要早期行动但同时可能带来重大负担。
设计与对象
本研究为一篇概念性手稿,提出了一个七步一体化健康伦理决策框架。其包括示例领域,如登革病毒、登革病毒感染及其相关临床表现(如登革出血热)。
核心方法
该框架整合了来自人类监测、虫媒观察、动物储存宿主或哨点数据、环境指标及模型输出的证据。它强调技术风险评估与伦理分析并重的必要性。
主要结果
该框架概述了一个序列,将综合证据与传播合理性与预防原则、比例原则、公平性、一体化健康权衡、合法性、反馈、影响评估及适应性相联系。它并未提出新的伦理原则,而是将这些原则整合到治理流程中。
解释与创新
作者将研究发现解释为对疾病控制中伦理决策的贡献,强调将伦理原则整合到结构化治理序列中的重要性。其创新之处在于为持续价值冲突提出医学生物伦理学调解方案。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为监测、诊断及登革病毒的预防与控制策略提供信息,强调决策流程中伦理考量的重要性。同时,研究还表明在管理媒介传播的人兽共患病时,需要采用综合性方法。
局限与证据强度
本研究为概念性研究,未提供实证结果,因此在评估所提框架有效性方面存在局限。证据强度为中等,因其基于理论整合而非实际应用。

Ethical Decision-Making Under Uncertainty in Vector-Borne Zoonotic Disease Control: A One Health Governance Framework

Authors: Olympia Lioupi, Kornélia Kurucz, Xhelil Koleci, Pavle Banović, Dejan Jakimovski, Eleftherios Meletis, Xanthi Rousou, Gerald Barry, Gábor Kemenesi, Gustavo Monti
Original abstract (English)
Decisions about vector-borne zoonotic disease control often have to be made before the evidence is complete. Human surveillance, entomological observations, animal reservoir or sentinel data, environmental indicators, and modelling outputs may provide warning before human disease patterns are clear. In some settings, early action can prevent harm, yet it can also impose social, economic, ecological, animal-welfare, liberty-related, and trust-related burdens. Technical risk assessment remains necessary, although it may need to be complemented by ethical analysis. In this conceptual manuscript, we propose a seven-step One Health ethical decision-making framework that links integrated evidence and transmission plausibility to precaution, proportionality, equity, One Health trade-offs, legitimacy, feedback, impact evaluation, and adaptation. Its contribution lies not in introducing new ethical principles, but in integrating them into an iterative governance sequence and proposing medical bioethics mediation for persistent value conflict. Illustrative domains include West Nile virus, dengue, Zika, Crimean–Congo haemorrhagic fever, and other mosquito-, tick-, and sand fly-borne zoonotic threats.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Decisions about vector-borne zoonotic disease control often have to be made before the evidence is complete. The study addresses the need for ethical frameworks in managing diseases like dengue where early action is critical but carries significant burdens.
Design and population
The study is a conceptual manuscript proposing a seven-step One Health ethical decision-making framework. It includes illustrative domains such as dengue virus, dengue infection, and related manifestations like severe dengue and dengue hemorrhagic fever.
Core methods
The framework integrates evidence from human surveillance, entomological observations, animal reservoir or sentinel data, environmental indicators, and modelling outputs. It emphasizes the necessity of technical risk assessment alongside ethical analysis.
Main results
The framework outlines a sequence that links integrated evidence and transmission plausibility to precaution, proportionality, equity, One Health trade-offs, legitimacy, feedback, impact evaluation, and adaptation. It does not present new ethical principles but integrates them into a governance process.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as a contribution to ethical decision-making in disease control, emphasizing the integration of ethical principles into a structured governance sequence. The novelty lies in proposing medical bioethics mediation for persistent value conflicts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and control strategies for dengue virus by highlighting the importance of ethical considerations in decision-making processes. They also suggest the need for integrated approaches in managing vector-borne zoonotic diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is conceptual and does not present empirical results, which limits its ability to provide direct evidence on the effectiveness of the proposed framework. The evidence strength is moderate as it is based on theoretical integration rather than practical application.

印度阿萨姆邦一所三级医院急性发热疾病的病原谱:一项横断面研究

Etiological Spectrum of Acute Febrile Illness in a Tertiary Care Hospital in Assam, India: A Cross-Sectional Study
作者： Dhritiman Misra, P. Das, J. S. Roy, P. Deka, Saurav Sarma, S. S. Sen, Gitasree Borah
摘要中文翻译引言:急性发热疾病(AFI)是一组以发热为特征的疾病谱系,常由多种感染性病原体引起。病原学多样性在 诊断 和 治疗 中构成挑战,尤其在诊断资源有限的地区。目的:评估导致伴或不伴皮疹急性发热疾病患者的病原谱。材料与方法:2024年1月至2024年12月,在印度阿萨姆邦北拉金普尔的拉金普尔医学院附属医院就诊的1,544名患者纳入本院内横断面研究。纳入标准为体温≥37.5°C且伴或不伴皮疹。采用酶联免疫吸附试验(ELISA)和快速检测试剂盒(RDT)对麻疹、风疹、登革热、水痘-带状疱疹、基孔肯雅病毒、日本脑炎(JE)、恙虫病东方体、钩端螺旋体及肝炎病毒进行血清学检测。数据分析采用描述性与推断性方法。P值<0.05为差异有统计学意义。结果:1,544例(100%)患者均有发热,其次为头痛856例(55.44%)、咳嗽636例(41.19%)、呕吐476例(30.83%)、腹痛347例(22.47%)、痢疾59例(3.82%)、腹泻52例(3.37%)、意识不清46例(2.98%),21例(1.36%)出现皮疹。发热与头痛合并发生856例(55.44%)、与咳嗽合并发生636例(41.19%)、与全身疼痛合并发生481例(31.15%)、与呕吐合并发生476例(30.83%)。研究发现感染率存在季节性变化,高峰出现在雨季。确诊病例中,恙虫病133/817例(16.28%)为最常见,其次为钩端螺旋体病129/473例(27.27%)、登革热84/1,273例(6.59%)、日本脑炎20/87例(22.99%),部分患者存在混合感染。结论:本研究强调综合临床与实验室方法在急性发热疾病诊断中的重要性。恙虫病、钩端螺旋体病及登革热被确定为主要病因,凸显加强 监测 与诊断能力的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
急性发热疾病(AFI)包括一系列以发热为特征的疾病,通常由多种病原体引起。病原体的多样性给诊断和治疗带来了挑战,尤其是在诊断资源有限的地区。
设计与对象
本研究是一项基于医院的横断面研究,于2024年1月至12月期间在印度阿萨姆邦北拉基姆普尔的拉基姆普尔医学系和医院进行,共纳入1,544名患者。所有发热(≥37.5°C)伴有或不伴有皮疹的患者均被纳入研究。
核心方法
对麻疹、风疹、登革热、水痘-带状疱疹病毒、基孔肯雅病毒、日本脑炎(JE)、恙虫病东方体、钩端螺旋体和肝炎病毒进行了血清学检测,使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)和快速检测测试(RDT)试剂盒。数据采用描述性与推断性统计方法进行分析。
主要结果
该研究发现感染率存在季节性变化,高峰出现在季风月份。多数阳性病例归因于:丛林斑疹伤寒 133/817(16.28%)、钩端螺旋体病 129/473(27.27%)、登革热 84/1273(6.59%)及日本脑炎 20/87(22.99%),部分病例为混合感染。
解释与创新
本研究强调了在诊断AFI时整合临床与实验室方法的重要性。恙虫病、钩端螺旋体病和登革热被确定为主要病因,突显了提升监测和诊断能力的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果突显了在资源有限地区改善诊断工具和监测系统的必要性,以应对AFI的多样化病原体。感染率的季节性变化可为暴发预测和资源分配提供公共卫生策略依据。
局限与证据强度
本研究受限于横断面设计,无法建立因果关系或时间关系。由于基于单一医院的数据,且未考虑更广泛的人群趋势,证据强度中等。

Etiological Spectrum of Acute Febrile Illness in a Tertiary Care Hospital in Assam, India: A Cross-Sectional Study

Authors: Dhritiman Misra, P. Das, J. S. Roy, P. Deka, Saurav Sarma, S. S. Sen, Gitasree Borah
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Acute Febrile Illness (AFI) encompasses a spectrum of conditions marked by fever, often caused by various infectious agents. The aetiological diversity poses challenges in diagnosis and treatment, particularly in regions with limited diagnostic resources. Aim: To evaluate the spectrum of pathogens causing AFI with or without rash in patients. Materials and Methods: A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January 2024 to December 2024 on 1,544 patients who had attended Lakhimpur Medical College and Hospital, North Lakhimpur, Assam, India. Patients with fever (≥ 37.5°C) with or without rash were enrolled. Serological investigations for Measles, Rubella, Dengue, Varicella-zoster, Chikungunya viruses, Japanese Encephalitis (JE), Orientia tsutsugamushi, Leptospira, and Hepatitis viruses were performed using Enzyme-linked Immunosorbent assay (ELISA) and Rapid Detection Testing (RDT) kits. Data analysis was performed using both descriptive and inferential methods. A p-value of <0.05 was considered significant. Results: Fever was prevalent in all 1,544 (100%) cases, followed by 856 (55.44%) cases of headache, 636 (41.19%) with cough, 476 (30.83%) with vomiting, 347 (22.47%) with abdominal pain, 59 (3.82%) cases with dysentery, 52 (3.37%) with diarrhoea, 46 (2.98%) cases with unconsciousness, and 21 (1.36%) cases presented with rash. Fever was observed in combination with headache in 856 (55.44%), with cough in 636 (41.19%), with bodyache in 481 (31.15%), and with vomiting in 476 (30.83%) cases. The study identified seasonal variations in infection rates, with peaks during the monsoon months. The majority of positive cases were attributed to Scrub Typhus 133/817 (16.28%), followed by Leptospirosis 129/473 (27.27%), Dengue 84/1273 (6.59%), and JE 20/87 (22.99%), while some had co-infection. Conclusion: The study underscores the importance of integrated clinical and laboratory approaches in diagnosing AFI. Scrub typhus, leptospirosis, and dengue were identified as major causes, emphasising the need for enhanced surveillance and diagnostic capabilities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute Febrile Illness (AFI) encompasses a spectrum of conditions marked by fever, often caused by various infectious agents. The aetiological diversity poses challenges in diagnosis and treatment, particularly in regions with limited diagnostic resources.
Design and population
A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted from January 2024 to December 2024 on 1,544 patients who had attended Lakhimpur Medical College and Hospital, North Lakhimpur, Assam, India. Patients with fever (≥ 37.5°C) with or without rash were enrolled.
Core methods
Serological investigations for Measles, Rubella, Dengue, Varicella-zoster, Chikungunya viruses, Japanese Encephalitis (JE), Orientia tsutsugamushi, Leptospira, and Hepatitis viruses were performed using Enzyme-linked Immunosorbent assay (ELISA) and Rapid Detection Testing (RDT) kits. Data analysis was performed using both descriptive and inferential methods.
Main results
The study identified seasonal variations in infection rates, with peaks during the monsoon months. The majority of positive cases were attributed to Scrub Typhus 133/817 (16.28%), followed by Leptospirosis 129/473 (27.27%), Dengue 84/1273 (6.59%), and JE 20/87 (22.99%), while some had co-infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The study underscores the importance of integrated clinical and laboratory approaches in diagnosing AFI. Scrub typhus, leptospirosis, and dengue were identified as major causes, emphasising the need for enhanced surveillance and diagnostic capabilities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the necessity for improved diagnostic tools and surveillance systems to address the diverse aetiology of AFI, particularly in resource-limited settings. The seasonal variation in infection rates can inform public health strategies for outbreak prediction and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which cannot establish causality or temporal relationships. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single hospital's data and does not account for broader population trends.

基于深度学习的埃及伊蚊红眼GSS幼虫分类与运动行为分析,用于不育昆虫技术(SIT)应用

Deep Learning Classification and Motion-Based Analysis of Aedes aegypti Red-Eye GSS Larvae for SIT Applications.
作者： Gianluca Manduca, Katerina Nikolouli, Kostas Bourtzis, Donato Romano
摘要中文翻译埃及伊蚊是登革热、寨卡病毒病和基孔肯雅热等虫媒病毒病的主要媒介,给全球公共卫生带来重大挑战。有效的控制策略(如不育昆虫技术,SIT)需要准确可靠的性别分选系统,以确保仅释放雄性个体。本研究提出了一种紧凑的深度学习框架,用于基于图像的埃及伊蚊幼虫性连锁眼色表型与龄期分类,并辅以独立的光流分析对幼虫运动行为进行量化。通过对单个幼虫的视频录像生成标注数据集,训练卷积神经网络(CNN)以分类四个实验组。同时,密集光流分析量化幼虫运动活性,生成可解释的运动学描述符。分类器在验证集和测试集上可靠区分所有实验组,且推理适用于低功耗硬件的实时部署。运动分析显示系统性差异:L4幼虫的活动性高于L3幼虫,红眼个体较黑眼个体表现出略高的运动活性,表明发育阶段是运动性的主要决定因素,而色素沉着发挥次要影响。所提框架在实现准确图像分类的同时,独立表征运动行为,从而提升所分析群体的生物学解释力。基于图像的分类器为SIT项目中的自动化幼虫分选提供了实用工具,而独立的运动分析模块则提供了定量行为描述符,可能支持未来蚊虫生物学与虫媒病毒病控制应用的研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃及伊蚊是登革热、寨卡病毒病和基孔肯雅热的主要媒介,迫切需要改进媒介控制策略以应对公共卫生需求。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一种紧凑的深度学习框架,通过单个幼虫的视频录像对埃及伊蚊幼虫的性连锁眼色表型与龄期进行分类。
核心方法
研究通过卷积神经网络(CNN)在标注视频数据集上训练,用于分类四个实验组;同时采用密集光流分析量化幼虫运动活性,生成运动学描述符。
主要结果
分类器在验证集和测试集上可靠区分所有实验组,且在低功耗硬件上具备实时部署可行性;运动分析显示L4幼虫的活动性高于L3幼虫,红眼个体较黑眼个体表现出略高的运动活性。
解释与创新
该框架实现了准确的图像分类与独立的行为表征,提升了幼虫群体的生物学解释力,并为SIT项目中的自动化分选提供了实用工具。
科研与公卫意义
基于图像的分类器与运动分析模块可通过自动化幼虫分选和提供定量行为描述符,增强不育昆虫技术项目,并支持蚊虫生物学与虫媒病毒病控制应用的研究。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告样本量、统计显著性指标或实地环境下的外部验证,限制了结果的普遍性及其在实际部署中的可信度。

Deep Learning Classification and Motion-Based Analysis of Aedes aegypti Red-Eye GSS Larvae for SIT Applications.

Authors: Gianluca Manduca, Katerina Nikolouli, Kostas Bourtzis, Donato Romano
Original abstract (English)
Aedes aegypti is a major vector of arboviral diseases, including dengue, Zika, and chikungunya, posing significant global public health challenges. Effective control strategies, such as the sterile insect technique (SIT), require an accurate and reliable sex sorting system to ensure male only releases. Here, we present a compact deep-learning framework for image-based classification of sex-linked eye-color phenotypes and instar stages of Aedes aegypti larvae, complemented by an independent optical flow analysis of larval locomotor behavior. Video recordings of individual larvae were used to generate annotated datasets for training a convolutional neural network (CNN) to classify four experimental groups. In parallel, dense optical flow analysis quantified larval locomotor activity, producing interpretable kinematic descriptors. The classifier reliably distinguished between all groups across validation and test sets, with inference suitable for real-time deployment on low-power hardware. Motion analysis revealed systematic differences: L4 larvae exhibited higher activity than L3, and red-eye individuals showed slightly elevated movement relative to black-eye counterparts, indicating that developmental stage is the primary determinant of motility, with pigmentation exerting a secondary influence. The proposed framework provides accurate image-based classification while independently characterizing locomotor behavior, thereby improving the biological interpretation of the analyzed groups. The image-based classifier offers a practical tool for automated larval sorting in SIT programs, whereas the independent motion-analysis module provides quantitative behavioral descriptors that may support future studies of mosquito biology and vector-control applications.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Aedes aegypti is a primary vector for dengue, Zika, and chikungunya, creating urgent public health needs for improved vector control strategies.
Design and population
The study developed a compact deep-learning framework to classify sex-linked eye-color phenotypes and instar stages of Aedes aegypti larvae using video recordings of individual larvae.
Core methods
A convolutional neural network (CNN) was trained on annotated datasets from video recordings to classify four experimental groups, while dense optical flow analysis quantified larval locomotor activity to produce kinematic descriptors.
Main results
The classifier reliably distinguished between all groups across validation and test sets, with real-time deployment feasibility on low-power hardware, and motion analysis showed L4 larvae had higher activity than L3, with red-eye individuals exhibiting slightly elevated movement compared to black-eye counterparts.
Interpretation and novelty
The framework provides accurate image-based classification and independent behavioral characterization, improving biological interpretation of larval groups and offering practical tools for automated sorting in SIT programs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The image-based classifier and motion-analysis module can enhance sterile insect technique programs by enabling automated larval sorting and providing quantitative behavioral descriptors for mosquito biology and vector-control applications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report sample sizes, statistical significance metrics, or external validation in field settings, limiting generalisability and confidence in real-world deployment.

通过整数值自回归(INAR)过程分析零膨胀与季节性计数时间序列:以高雄市登革热病例为例

Analysis of Count Time Series Through Integer‐Valued Autoregressive (INAR) Process With Zero‐Inflation and Seasonality
作者： Subhankar Chattopadhyay, Atanu Biswas, Samarjit Das, Raju Maiti, Jing‐Shiang Hwang
摘要中文翻译摘要 本文研究了台湾高雄市2009—2012年期间每周登革热病例的计数时间序列数据,该数据同时存在大量零值(零膨胀)与季节性问题。为同时捕捉这两个特征,我们考虑了一种面向应用的零膨胀季节性PINAR框架,该框架基于零膨胀泊松创新。我们还在创新项中纳入一个外生变量,即每周最高气温,以研究气温对登革热病例数量的影响,因为多项研究已发现气温是全球登革热传播的重要因素。所提模型可同时捕捉前一时间点未恢复的病例与当前时间点新增的病例。本文推导了所提模型的分布性质与预测性质。在适当的正则性条件下,我们证明了CLS估计量的一致性与渐近正态性,并通过模拟实验检验了所提模型在有限样本估计与预测性能。最后,我们将所提模型与若干现有INAR模型进行比较,并以高雄市每周登革热病例数据为例进行实证分析。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决同时存在零膨胀与季节性的计数时间序列数据分析挑战,具体针对台湾高雄市登革热病例数据。
设计与对象
本研究分析了台湾高雄市2009—2012年间每周登革热病例数据,重点关注计数时间序列中的零膨胀与季节性特征。
核心方法
研究人员构建了一个零膨胀季节性PINAR模型,并以每周最高气温作为外生变量,研究其对登革热病例数量的影响。
主要结果
所提模型与现有INAR模型进行了比较,以评估其在捕捉登革热病例数据中零膨胀与季节性特征方面的性能。
解释与创新
该模型同时考虑零膨胀与季节性的能力,为分析登革热病例时间序列数据提供了一种新颖的方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究成果为更好地理解与预测登革热传播提供了方法学工具,可为公共卫生策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于抽象层面的证据,未包含详细统计结果或现实世界验证,证据强度降低至中等。

Analysis of Count Time Series Through Integer‐Valued Autoregressive (INAR) Process With Zero‐Inflation and Seasonality

Authors: Subhankar Chattopadhyay, Atanu Biswas, Samarjit Das, Raju Maiti, Jing‐Shiang Hwang
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT In this article, we study a count time‐series data of weekly dengue cases in Kaohsiung City, Taiwan during the period 2009–2012, where the problems of a large number of zeros or zero‐inflation and seasonality arise together. To capture both features, we consider an application‐oriented zero‐inflated seasonal PINAR framework based on zero‐inflated Poisson innovations. We also incorporate an exogenous variable, namely the weekly maximum temperature, in the innovations to study the effect of temperature on the number of dengue cases as many studies have found that temperature is a significant factor in spreading dengue infections globally. The proposed model can capture both the non‐recovery cases from the previous time point and the new cases coming at the current time point. The distributional and forecasting properties of the proposed model are derived. The consistency and asymptotic normality of the CLS estimator are established under suitable regularity conditions, and simulation experiments are used to examine the finite‐sample estimation and forecasting performance of the proposed model. The proposed model is compared with some existing INAR models. Finally, we analyze the data of weekly dengue cases in Kaohsiung for practical illustration.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges of analyzing count time series data with zero-inflation and seasonality, specifically in the context of dengue cases in Kaohsiung City, Taiwan.
Design and population
The study analyzed weekly dengue case data from Kaohsiung City, Taiwan, between 2009 and 2012, focusing on the presence of zero-inflation and seasonality in the count time series.
Core methods
The researchers developed a zero-inflated seasonal PINAR model using weekly maximum temperature as an exogenous variable to study its effect on dengue case numbers.
Main results
The proposed model was compared with existing INAR models to evaluate its performance in capturing zero-inflation and seasonality in dengue case data.
Interpretation and novelty
The model's ability to account for both zero-inflation and seasonality represents a novel approach to analyzing dengue case time series data.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a methodological tool for better understanding and forecasting dengue outbreaks, which can inform public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence and does not include detailed statistical results or real-world validation, which reduces the strength of the evidence to moderate.
