查库恩古尼亚热相关急性呼吸窘迫综合征:一例罕见致死病例

Chikungunya-Associated Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome: A Rare Fatal Case
作者： Arkaprava Singharoy, Payel Bose, Tanmay Banerjee, Sagnik Das, Prerona Bhowmick
摘要中文翻译查库恩古尼亚热是由披膜病毒科甲病毒属(Togaviridae科,甲病毒属)引起的虫媒病毒性疾病。其常见临床表现为发热、严重关节痛、皮疹和肌痛,而导致急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)的严重肺部受累较为罕见。本文报道一名62岁男性患者,其既往合并肥胖、糖尿病、高血压、阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停、冠心病及间质性肺病。患者于近期赴印度勒克瑙旅行后出现高热、严重关节痛、面部浮肿及进行性呼吸困难。入院时患者严重低氧血症,PaO2/FiO2比值低于100,需行机械通气。插管过程中患者发生心脏骤停,经心肺复苏后自主循环恢复。查库恩古尼亚病毒IgM及逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测均为阳性。胸部影像学显示双侧弥漫性浸润影及磨玻璃样改变,符合重症ARDS表现。排除登革热、疟疾及恙虫病。患者接受肺保护性通气、俯卧位通气及按照Meduri方案应用皮质类固醇治疗,病情一度好转。但其后出现进行性呼吸衰竭及多器官功能障碍,住院41天后最终死亡。尽管查库恩古尼亚病毒通常不被视为呼吸道病毒,但临床医生应认识到其可能引发严重肺部表现,包括ARDS,尤其是在合并显著合并症的老年患者中。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
查库恩古尼亚热是由披膜病毒科甲病毒属(Togaviridae科,甲病毒属)引起的虫媒病毒性疾病。其常见临床表现为发热、严重关节痛、皮疹和肌痛,而导致急性呼吸窘迫综合征(ARDS)的严重肺部受累较为罕见。
设计与对象
本文报道一名62岁男性患者,其既往合并肥胖、糖尿病、高血压、阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停、冠心病及间质性肺病。患者于近期赴印度勒克瑙旅行后出现高热、严重关节痛、面部浮肿及进行性呼吸困难。
核心方法
查库恩古尼亚病毒IgM及逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测均为阳性。排除登革热、疟疾及恙虫病。
主要结果
入院时患者严重低氧血症,PaO2/FiO2比值低于100,需行机械通气。胸部影像学显示双侧弥漫性浸润影及磨玻璃样改变,符合重症ARDS表现。
解释与创新
插管过程中患者发生心脏骤停,经心肺复苏后自主循环恢复。患者接受肺保护性通气、俯卧位通气及按照Meduri方案应用皮质类固醇治疗,病情一度好转。
科研与公卫意义
尽管查库恩古尼亚病毒通常不被视为呼吸道病毒,但临床医生应认识到其可能引发严重肺部表现,包括ARDS,尤其是在合并显著合并症的老年患者中。
局限与证据强度
但其后出现进行性呼吸衰竭及多器官功能障碍,住院41天后最终死亡。本病例报告未报告研究局限性或证据强度。

Chikungunya-Associated Acute Respiratory Distress Syndrome: A Rare Fatal Case

Authors: Arkaprava Singharoy, Payel Bose, Tanmay Banerjee, Sagnik Das, Prerona Bhowmick
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya fever is an arboviral disease caused by an alphavirus belonging to the family Togaviridae. It commonly presents with fever, severe arthralgia, rash, and myalgia, while severe pulmonary involvement causing acute respiratory distress syndrome (ARDS) is rare. We report a 62-year-old man with obesity, diabetes mellitus, hypertension, obstructive sleep apnoea, coronary artery disease, and interstitial lung disease, who presented with high-grade fever, severe arthralgia, facial puffiness, and rapidly progressive dyspnoea, following recent travel to Lucknow, India. On admission, he had severe hypoxemia with a PaO2/FiO2 ratio below 100, requiring mechanical ventilation. During intubation, he developed cardiac arrest but achieved return of spontaneous circulation after cardiopulmonary resuscitation. Chikungunya IgM and reverse transcriptase polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) were positive. Chest imaging showed diffuse bilateral infiltrates and ground-glass opacities consistent with severe ARDS. Dengue, malaria, and scrub typhus were excluded. The patient was managed with lung-protective ventilation, prone positioning, and corticosteroids according to the Meduri protocol with transient improvement. However, he later developed worsening respiratory failure and multiorgan dysfunction and eventually succumbed after 41 days of hospitalization. Although chikungunya is not typically considered a respiratory virus, clinicians should recognize that severe pulmonary manifestations, including ARDS, may occur, particularly in elderly patients with significant comorbidities.

气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病:对监测与基层卫生保健的挑战

Climate Change and Arboviral Diseases: Challenges for Surveillance and Primary Health Care
作者： Maria Terezinha Gomes Pontes, Edylene Maria dos Santos Pereira, Bruno Ramos Brum, Maria Aparecida de Almeida Araújo, Thaita Thaisi Zago, Sérgio Raimundo Ernesto Machado, Sheila Érika Ferro Ramalho Nobre, Neide Moreira de Souza
摘要中文翻译气候变化已成为21世纪最严峻的公共卫生挑战之一,深刻影响着全球传染病的流行病学。在直接受环境变化影响的健康问题中,虫媒病毒性疾病(包括登革热、寨卡病毒病、基孔肯雅热与黄热病)的传播动态对温度、降水、湿度、城市化及生态系统破坏等变化高度敏感。这些气候与环境变化已扩大蚊媒媒介的地理分布,延长传播季节,并增加热带与亚热带地区暴发的频率与强度。在巴西等虫媒病毒已构成显著公共卫生负担的国家,气候变化加剧了既有脆弱性,并为卫生系统带来新的需求,特别是在疾病监测、预防与医疗服务提供方面。本学术论文对气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病负担日益加重之间的关系进行了批判性审视,强调流行病学监测系统与基层卫生保健(PHC)所面临的挑战。讨论涵盖了气候变异与媒介增殖的生态机制、卫生服务的组织层面影响、综合监测的重要性,以及PHC在预防、早期诊断、社区参与与跨部门行动中的战略作用。论文认为,加强气候敏感型卫生治理与韧性基层卫生保健系统,对于减轻气候驱动的虫媒病毒性传播的健康影响并保护脆弱人群至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
气候变化已成为21世纪最严峻的公共卫生挑战之一,深刻影响着全球传染病的流行病学。本研究旨在探讨气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病负担日益加重之间的关系,重点关注登革热,并强调对监测与基层卫生保健的挑战。
设计与对象
本学术论文对气候变化与虫媒病毒性疾病负担日益加重之间的关系进行了批判性审视,强调热带与亚热带地区流行病学监测系统与基层卫生保健(PHC)所面临的挑战。论文讨论了气候变异与媒介增殖的生态机制,以及卫生服务的组织层面影响。
核心方法
本论文通过分析气候变化对虫媒病毒性疾病(包括登革热)的传播动态的影响,探讨了蚊媒媒介的地理分布、传播季节的持续时间,以及暴发的频率与强度。同时,论文还探索了PHC在预防、早期诊断、社区参与与跨部门行动中的战略作用。
主要结果
这些气候与环境变化已扩大蚊媒媒介的地理分布,延长传播季节,并增加热带与亚热带地区暴发的频率与强度。
解释与创新
作者认为,气候变化加剧了既有脆弱性,并为卫生系统带来新的需求,特别是在疾病监测、预防与医疗服务提供方面。论文强调了综合监测的必要性,以及PHC在应对此类挑战中的战略作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,亟需加强气候敏感型卫生治理与韧性基层卫生保健系统,以减轻气候驱动的虫媒病毒性传播的健康影响,并保护脆弱人群。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体局限性或证据强度评估。本论文基于一般性观察与启示,证据强度被认为属于中等水平。

Climate Change and Arboviral Diseases: Challenges for Surveillance and Primary Health Care

Authors: Maria Terezinha Gomes Pontes, Edylene Maria dos Santos Pereira, Bruno Ramos Brum, Maria Aparecida de Almeida Araújo, Thaita Thaisi Zago, Sérgio Raimundo Ernesto Machado, Sheila Érika Ferro Ramalho Nobre, Neide Moreira de Souza
Original abstract (English)
Climate change has emerged as one of the greatest public health challenges of the twenty-first century, profoundly influencing the epidemiology of infectious diseases worldwide. Among the health conditions most directly affected by environmental transformations are arboviral diseases, including dengue, Zika, chikungunya, and yellow fever, whose transmission dynamics are highly sensitive to variations in temperature, rainfall, humidity, urbanization, and ecosystem disruption. These climatic and environmental changes have expanded the geographic distribution of mosquito vectors, prolonged transmission seasons, and increased the frequency and intensity of outbreaks in tropical and subtropical regions. In countries such as Brazil, where arboviruses already represent a significant public health burden, climate change has intensified existing vulnerabilities and imposed new demands on health systems, particularly regarding disease surveillance, prevention, and healthcare delivery. This academic essay critically examines the relationship between climate change and the growing burden of arboviral diseases, emphasizing the challenges faced by epidemiological surveillance systems and Primary Health Care (PHC). The discussion explores the ecological mechanisms linking climate variability to vector proliferation, the organizational implications for health services, the importance of integrated surveillance, and the strategic role of PHC in prevention, early diagnosis, community engagement, and intersectoral action. The essay argues that strengthening climate-sensitive health governance and resilient primary healthcare systems is essential for reducing the health impacts of climate-driven arboviral transmission and protecting vulnerable populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Climate change has emerged as one of the greatest public health challenges of the twenty-first century, profoundly influencing the epidemiology of infectious diseases worldwide. The study aims to examine the relationship between climate change and the growing burden of arboviral diseases, particularly dengue, highlighting the challenges for surveillance and primary health care.
Design and population
This academic essay critically examines the relationship between climate change and the growing burden of arboviral diseases, emphasizing the challenges faced by epidemiological surveillance systems and Primary Health Care (PHC) in tropical and subtropical regions. It discusses the ecological mechanisms linking climate variability to vector proliferation and the organizational implications for health services.
Core methods
The essay analyzes the impact of climate change on the transmission dynamics of arboviral diseases, including dengue, by examining the geographic distribution of mosquito vectors, the duration of transmission seasons, and the frequency and intensity of outbreaks. It also explores the strategic role of PHC in prevention, early diagnosis, community engagement, and intersectoral action.
Main results
These climatic and environmental changes have expanded the geographic distribution of mosquito vectors, prolonged transmission seasons, and increased the frequency and intensity of outbreaks in tropical and subtropical regions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that climate change has intensified existing vulnerabilities and imposed new demands on health systems, particularly regarding disease surveillance, prevention, and healthcare delivery. The essay emphasizes the need for integrated surveillance and the strategic role of PHC in addressing these challenges.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for strengthening climate-sensitive health governance and resilient primary healthcare systems to reduce the health impacts of climate-driven arboviral transmission and protect vulnerable populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment. The essay is based on general observations and implications, and the evidence is considered moderate in strength.

黎巴嫩儿童登革热感染后格林-巴利综合征:病例报告 和文献综述

Guillain-Barré syndrome following dengue infection in a child in Lebanon: a case report and review of the literature
作者： Dima Khreis, Amani Bannout, Mia Maria Abou Younes, Simone Khalifeh, Ghassan S. Dbaibo
摘要中文翻译背景:登革病毒 感染越来越被认为是神经系统并发症的原因,包括感染后免疫介导的神经病,如吉兰-巴利综合征 (GBS)。我们报告了来自黎巴嫩的一例抗 GD1a 阳性的罕见儿科病例,强调了登革热 流行病学 的演变以及在新兴 传播 环境中识别神经系统并发症的重要性。病例介绍:居住在加纳的一名先前健康的 3 岁女孩在从伊拉克旅行一周返回后约两周以及从加纳返回黎巴嫩后约九周出现进行性双侧下肢疼痛和拒绝负重。神经学检查显示反射不断变化,从反射亢进到反射消失,但感觉保留。脑脊液显示白蛋白细胞解离,而脊髓磁共振成像显示马尾神经根增强。电生理学研究支持脱髓鞘性多发性神经病。登革热 血清学 支持近期原发感染(最初 IgM 阳性,IgG 阴性,随后 IgG 血清转换),抗神经节苷脂检测显示抗 GD1a 阳性。该患者接受静脉注射免疫球蛋白(2 g/kg,持续 2 天)治疗,临床症状迅速改善,并在两周内接近完全康复。结论:该病例强调登革热通过涉及抗神经节苷脂抗体的自身免疫机制成为幼儿 GBS 的潜在触发因素。它强调了登革热感染范围扩大到黎巴嫩等以前非流行国家,这可能是由于气候变化、媒介 分布扩大和国际旅行增加所致。这就提出了在中东等新兴地区急性神经病的差异性 诊断 中考虑登革热的重要性,特别是在全球旅行增加和气候变化引起的 媒介 扩张的背景下。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该范围检查 登革病毒(DENV-1 至 DENV-4)作为儿童吉兰-巴利综合征 (GBS) 的触发因素,重点关注黎巴嫩等新兴 传播 环境中的神经系统并发症、诊断标记物和 治疗 反应。
证据基础与方法
提供的证据未报告系统评价、数据库、检索日期、资格标准、选择方法或纳入研究计数;它提供了一个单独的儿科 病例报告 以及文献叙述性回顾。
共识与结论
登革病毒 感染可通过涉及抗神经节苷脂抗体的自身免疫机制引发儿童 GBS,这一点得到抗 GD1a 阳性和脱髓鞘性多发性神经病的电生理学证据的支持。
争议与缺口
当前证据基础仅限于单例儿科病例,导致对登革热相关格林巴利综合征在儿童中的发病频率、风险因素及不同地区与血清型间的普遍性仍存在不确定性。
科研与实践启示
在气候变化驱动媒介扩张与全球旅行增加的背景下,临床医生应在新兴地区(如中东)将登革热纳入急性神经病变的鉴别诊断范围。

Guillain-Barré syndrome following dengue infection in a child in Lebanon: a case report and review of the literature

Authors: Dima Khreis, Amani Bannout, Mia Maria Abou Younes, Simone Khalifeh, Ghassan S. Dbaibo
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue virus infection is increasingly recognized as a cause of neurological complications, including post-infectious immune-mediated neuropathies such as Guillain-Barré syndrome (GBS). We report a rare pediatric case from Lebanon with anti-GD1a positivity, highlighting the evolving epidemiology of dengue and the importance of recognizing neurological complications in emerging transmission settings. CASE PRESENTATION: A previously healthy 3-year-old girl residing in Ghana presented with progressive bilateral lower extremity pain and refusal to bear weight approximately two weeks after returning from a one-week trip to Iraq and nine weeks after returning to Lebanon from Ghana. Neurological examination revealed evolving reflex changes from hyperreflexia to areflexia with preserved sensation. Cerebrospinal fluid demonstrated albuminocytologic dissociation, while spinal magnetic resonance imaging showed cauda equina nerve-root enhancement. Electrophysiological studies supported a demyelinating polyneuropathy. Dengue serology supported recent primary infection (initial IgM positivity with negative IgG, with subsequent IgG seroconversion), and antiganglioside testing revealed anti-GD1a positivity. The patient was treated with intravenous immunoglobulin (2 g/kg over 2 days), resulting in rapid clinical improvement and near-complete recovery within two weeks. CONCLUSION: This case highlights dengue as a potential trigger of GBS in young children through autoimmune mechanisms involving antiganglioside antibodies. It underscores the expanding range of dengue infection to previously non-endemic countries like Lebanon, potentially driven by climate change, expanding vector distribution, and increasing international travel. This raises the importance of considering dengue in the differential diagnosis of acute neuropathies in emerging regions such as the Middle East, particularly in the context of increasing global travel and climate change-induced vector expansion.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The scope examines dengue virus (DENV-1 to DENV-4) as a trigger of Guillain-Barré syndrome (GBS) in children, focusing on neurological complications, diagnostic markers, and treatment responses within emerging transmission settings such as Lebanon.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not report a systematic review, databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, selection method, or included-study count; it presents a single pediatric case report with narrative review of literature.
Consensus and conclusions
Dengue virus infection can trigger GBS in children via autoimmune mechanisms involving antiganglioside antibodies, supported by anti-GD1a positivity and electrophysiological evidence of demyelinating polyneuropathy.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence base is limited to a single pediatric case, leaving uncertainty about the frequency, risk factors, and generalizability of dengue-associated GBS in children across different regions and serotypes.
Research and practice implications
Clinicians should consider dengue in the differential diagnosis of acute neuropathies in emerging regions like the Middle East, particularly amid climate change-driven vector expansion and increased global travel.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

预防登革热的干预措施:映射综述。

Interventions for preventing dengue: a mapping review.
作者： Leslie Choi, Bruno Guedes Alcoforado Aguiar, Susi Wisniewski, Ana Carolina F Modesto, Luis Phillipe Lopes, Jose Salvador Carrillo, Mariana Del Grossi Moura, Anna H Noel-Storr, Roses Parker
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述旨在绘制登革热预防干预的证据图谱,重点关注 登革病毒 1–4 与登革热疾病本身。核心问题是识别并分类现有的登革热预防策略文献,包括原始研究和系统评价,以支持未来研究和公共卫生行动的决策。研究范围严格限定为仅涉及登革热相关实体,除非明确与登革热特定证据相连,否则不包括其他黄病毒。
证据基础与方法
证据基础来源于 2025 年 8 月 6 日对 MEDLINE (Ovid)、Embase (Ovid)、CINAHL (EBSCO)、Cochrane 对照试验中央注册库 (CENTRAL)、Cochrane 图书馆的 Cochrane 系统评价数据库 (CDSR) 和 LILACS (BIREME) 的全面检索。没有应用日期或语言限制。该综述包括随机对照试验(RCT)、半随机试验、非随机干预研究(NRSI)和系统评价。在试点阶段开发了结构化编码框架,并使用该框架提取数据。使用 AMSTAR 2 工具评估系统评价的质量,只有 8 个评价为高质量或中等质量。
共识与结论
该综述共确定了 550 项研究,其中包括 505 项初步研究和 45 项系统评价。大多数研究侧重于针对成人和儿童混合人群的社区一级干预措施,主要集中在南美洲和南亚的高 患病率 地区。社区教育是研究最广泛的干预领域,有 47 个 RCT 和 140 个 NSI。由于方法学上的缺陷,大多数系统评价被评为低质量或极低质量。证据差距图强调需要进行更多高质量的研究,特别是在 疫苗、环境干预措施和 媒介 控制策略方面。
争议与缺口
证据情况被描述为支离破碎,研究在设计、重点和质量上差异很大。大多数系统评价的质量较低或极低,表明证据综合的方法学局限性。明显缺乏综合的、高水平的研究来捕获现有知识的全部范围。此外,该审查还发现了特定干预措施(例如 疫苗 和环境战略)的研究差距,并强调需要在某些地区和人群中进行更多研究。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调了确定关键知识差距的重要性,以便为未来的初步研究和系统审查提供信息。它建议优先考虑 疫苗、环境干预措施和 媒介 控制策略的高质量研究。与 Instituto Veredas 进行了利益相关者参与,以确保研究结果与公共卫生决策相关。研究结果旨在作为资助者、政策制定者和研究人员的决策支持工具,指导未来最需要的研究和公共卫生行动投资。

Interventions for preventing dengue: a mapping review.

Authors: Leslie Choi, Bruno Guedes Alcoforado Aguiar, Susi Wisniewski, Ana Carolina F Modesto, Luis Phillipe Lopes, Jose Salvador Carrillo, Mariana Del Grossi Moura, Anna H Noel-Storr, Roses Parker
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue remains a pressing global health challenge, particularly in low- and middle-income countries (LMIC). Despite a growing volume of research on prevention strategies, there is a notable lack of consolidated, high-level research capturing the full scope of existing knowledge. The current evidence landscape is fragmented, with studies varying widely in design, focus, and quality. A structured map that includes both primary studies and systematic reviews is urgently needed. Such evidence and gap maps (EGMs) serve as a decision-support tool for funders, policymakers, and researchers by identifying where research exists and where future investments are most needed. comprehensively identify the published literature on dengue prevention strategies; 2. critically appraise systematic reviews; 3. present the findings of objectives 1 and 2 in a single evidence gap map; and 4. identify critical knowledge gaps to inform future primary studies and systematic reviews. SEARCH METHODS: We searched MEDLINE (Ovid), Embase (Ovid), CINAHL (EBSCO), Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials (CENTRAL), Cochrane Database of Systematic Reviews (CDSR) in the Cochrane Library, and LILACS (BIREME) on 6 August 2025. We did not apply any date or language restrictions to the searches. SELECTION CRITERIA: We included randomised controlled trials (RCTs), quasi-randomised trials, non-randomised studies of interventions (NRSIs) and systematic reviews. NRSIs had to have a comparator. We included study designs such as cohort studies, interrupted time series designs, controlled before-after studies, and case-control studies. We did not include case studies or animal studies conducted in a laboratory or semi-field environment. We did not have any restrictions on date or language of publication. We included studies that compared dengue prevention strategies against placebo, no intervention, or usual care. We developed intervention and outcomes categories through a structured stakeholder engagement process and an existing theoretical framework. DATA COLLECTION AND ANALYSIS: We used Cochrane Crowd, Cochrane's citizen science platform designed to host classification tasks, to help identify and describe health-related research. The results collectively identified by the Crowd as being potentially relevant to the EGM were then imported into EPPI Reviewer for further assessment and coding by the author team. Two review authors independently extracted data from the included studies using a structured coding framework developed during the pilot stage. Following coding, we exported data from EPPI Reviewer to EPPI Mapper to generate an interactive, online map that visually displays the distribution of evidence and identifies key gaps by intervention and outcome. We sought to identify evidence and map it, but did not seek to provide a critical appraisal of that evidence. However, to support systematic review teams in understanding where there are evidence synthesis gaps, and to minimise research duplication, we decided to critically appraise the systematic reviews. Two review authors independently appraised the quality of systematic reviews, using A MeaSurement Tool to Assess Systematic Reviews (AMSTAR 2). We partnered with Instituto Veredas, a Brazilian non-governmental organisation, that aims to build bridges between public management, academia, and civil society, to conduct stakeholder engagement. MAIN RESULTS: The searches identified 20,355 records. After deduplication, we screened 16,468 titles and abstracts, then assessed 720 full-text articles for further eligibility. In total, we included 550 studies in the EGM. Evidence from primary studies We included a total of 505 primary studies, comprising 197 RCTs and 308 NRSIs, with 219 published in the past decade. Most studies were conducted at the community level with mixed adult and child populations, predominantly in high-prevalence regions of South America and South Asia. Brazil accounted for the largest share of studies (64 studies), and Aedes aegypti was the most frequently targeted vector species. Of our five main intervention domains (vaccines, environmental, community education, targetting of aquatic stages of the vector and targetting of adult vectors), community education was the most extensively studied (47 RCTs and 140 NRSIs), largely focusing on behavioural and entomological outcomes. These interventions overlapped with vector control interventions targeting both aquatic and adult stages of the mosquito. Evidence from systematic reviews We included 45 systematic reviews. We rated only eight as high or moderate quality using the AMSTAR 2 tool, with the majority classified as low or critically low quality due to methodological weaknesses. Common deficiencies included lack of protocol registration, inadequate justification for study exclusions, and insufficient consideration of risk of bias in interpreting findings. Of the eight high- or moderate-quality reviews, most of which evaluated multiple interventions, two included environmental interventions, three evaluated community education, four assessed vaccines, one evaluated vector control interventions exclusively targeting aquatic stages, and three included vector control of adult mosquitoes. Key gaps For community education, a clear gap exists for implementation outcomes, where the substantial evidence base of 25 primary studies has not been synthesised by any high- or moderate-quality systematic reviews. Adult vector interventions present multiple synthesis gaps despite extensive primary studies, as existing reviews are outdated or restricted to Wolbachia-infected mosquitoes. Aquatic-stage interventions show consistent synthesis gaps across all outcome domains. For vaccines, evidence is particularly limited for implementation outcomes and entirely absent for behavioural outcomes, indicating minimal evidence in these domains. Finally, environmental interventions display synthesis gaps for behavioural and implementation outcomes, where primary studies are available but high- or moderate-quality systematic reviews are lacking. AUTHORS' CONCLUSIONS: The primary challenge in the dengue prevention evidence base is not the absence of studies, but the lack of strategic synthesis aligned with decision-making needs. Priority areas for future research include: • updated and comprehensive systematic reviews for adult and aquatic-stage vector interventions; • synthesis of implementation and behavioural evidence across all non-vaccine interventions; and • systematic evaluation of adverse effects beyond vaccine technologies. From a policy perspective, reliance on entomological outcomes alone remains insufficient. Greater emphasis on epidemiological and implementation outcomes is essential to support effective, scalable, and context-sensitive dengue control strategies. Aligning future research agendas with these gaps may substantially enhance the usability of the evidence for public health decision-making, particularly in endemic and resource-constrained settings. FUNDING: The Cochrane Collaboration funded stakeholder engagement work, information specialist expertise, and some of the authors. REGISTRATION: Protocol (2025): DOI: 10.1002/14651858.CD016299.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review aimed to map the evidence on interventions for preventing dengue, focusing on dengue viruses 1–4 and dengue disease. The central question was to identify and categorize the existing literature on dengue prevention strategies, including primary studies and systematic reviews, to support decision-making for future research and public health actions. The topic boundary was strictly defined to include only dengue-related entities, excluding other flaviviruses unless explicitly linked to dengue-specific evidence.
Evidence base and method
The evidence base was derived from a comprehensive search of MEDLINE (Ovid), Embase (Ovid), CINAHL (EBSCO), Cochrane Central Register of Controlled Trials (CENTRAL), Cochrane Database of Systematic Reviews (CDSR) in the Cochrane Library, and LILACS (BIREME) on 6 August 2025. No date or language restrictions were applied. The review included randomised controlled trials (RCTs), quasi-randomised trials, non-randomised studies of interventions (NRSIs), and systematic reviews. A structured coding framework was developed during a pilot stage, and data were extracted using this framework. The quality of systematic reviews was assessed using the AMSTAR 2 tool, with only eight rated as high or moderate quality.
Consensus and conclusions
The review identified 550 studies in total, comprising 505 primary studies and 45 systematic reviews. Most studies focused on community-level interventions with mixed adult and child populations, predominantly in high-prevalence regions of South America and South Asia. Community education was the most extensively studied intervention domain, with 47 RCTs and 140 NRSIs. The majority of systematic reviews were rated as low or critically low quality due to methodological weaknesses. The evidence gap map highlighted the need for more high-quality research, particularly on vaccines, environmental interventions, and vector control strategies.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence landscape was described as fragmented, with studies varying widely in design, focus, and quality. The majority of systematic reviews were of low or critically low quality, indicating methodological limitations in the synthesis of evidence. There was a notable lack of consolidated, high-level research capturing the full scope of existing knowledge. Additionally, the review identified gaps in research on specific interventions, such as vaccines and environmental strategies, and highlighted the need for more studies in certain regions and populations.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasized the importance of identifying critical knowledge gaps to inform future primary studies and systematic reviews. It recommended prioritizing high-quality research on vaccines, environmental interventions, and vector control strategies. Stakeholder engagement was conducted with Instituto Veredas to ensure that the findings were relevant to public health decision-making. The results were intended to serve as a decision-support tool for funders, policymakers, and researchers, guiding where future investments in research and public health actions are most needed.

溶瘤病毒疗法中新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体:综合综述。

Emerging and Re-Emerging Viral Pathogens in Oncolytic Virotherapy: A Comprehensive Review.
作者： Xin An, Nan Liu, Junchao Liu
摘要中文翻译溶瘤病毒疗法最近成为一种有前途的免疫肿瘤学策略,利用病毒病原体的天然溶细胞潜力来选择性地靶向和破坏恶性细胞。尽管各种工程病毒,如腺病毒、牛痘病毒和单纯疱疹病毒,目前正处于临床评估阶段,但具有内在溶瘤潜力的新出现和重新出现的病毒,包括寨卡病毒(ZIKV)、严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2(SARS-CoV-2)、登革病毒(DENV)和西尼罗河病毒(WNV)也显示出有希望的结果。这些新出现和重新出现的病毒病原体表现出特定的肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避策略和免疫原性细胞死亡机制,可用于下一代治疗平台。尽管如此,与安全性、病毒致病性、宿主 免疫应答 以及不受控制的病毒传播风险相关的担忧提出了重大的转化挑战。本综述全面概述了溶瘤病毒治疗中的新型候选病毒,包括 ZIKV、SARS-CoV-2、DENV 和 WNV、它们的肿瘤靶向分子机制,以及旨在提高治疗效果和生物安全性的基因工程的最新进展。此外,这篇综述描述了将新兴病毒治疗药物整合到精准肿瘤学中的临床观点、监管考虑因素和未来方向。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注 登革病毒 (DENV) 及其在溶瘤病毒治疗中的作用,检查其肿瘤趋向性、免疫逃避策略和免疫原性细胞死亡机制。它还探讨了 DENV 作为精准肿瘤学背景下的治疗剂的潜力。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对登革病毒及其在溶瘤病毒治疗中的应用文献的全面分析。涵盖了工程化和天然存在的病毒,并重点关注登革病毒(DENV)。综述讨论了临床视角、监管考量及未来方向,但未明确报告荟萃分析或汇总估计值。
共识与结论
综述强调登革病毒及其他相关黄病毒具有内在溶瘤潜能,具备特定的肿瘤嗜性、免疫逃避策略及免疫原性细胞死亡机制,有望用于下一代治疗平台。同时指出,登革病毒(DENV)是溶瘤病毒治疗的潜在候选之一。
争议与缺口
综述认可了与登革病毒及其他相关病毒的安全性、致病性以及宿主 免疫应答相关的担忧。同时指出,在肿瘤靶向机制的精确理解及此类病毒在治疗应用中的长期生物安全性方面仍存在认知缺口。
科研与实践启示
综述建议需进一步研究以提升登革病毒在溶瘤病毒治疗中的治疗效果与生物安全性。同时强调将新兴病毒治疗剂纳入精准肿瘤学框架(包括临床试验与监管指南)的重要性。

Emerging and Re-Emerging Viral Pathogens in Oncolytic Virotherapy: A Comprehensive Review.

Authors: Xin An, Nan Liu, Junchao Liu
Original abstract (English)
Oncolytic virotherapy has recently emerged as a promising immuno-oncological strategy, utilising the natural cytolytic potentials of viral pathogens to selectively target and destroy malignant cells. Although various engineered viruses, such as adenoviruses, vaccinia virus, and herpes simplex virus, are currently in clinical evaluation, emerging and re-emerging viruses with intrinsic oncolytic potential, including Zika virus (ZIKV), Severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), Dengue virus (DENV), and West Nile virus (WNV), have also shown promising results. These emerging and re-emerging viral pathogens show specific tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms that could be harnessed for next-generation therapeutic platforms. Nonetheless, concerns related to safety, viral pathogenicity, host immune responses, and the risk of uncontrolled viral dissemination present significant translational challenges. The present review provides a comprehensive overview of novel viral candidates, including ZIKV, SARS-CoV-2, DENV, and WNV, in oncolytic virotherapy, their molecular mechanisms of tumour targeting, and current advancements in genetic engineering aimed at improving therapeutic efficacy and biosafety. Moreover, this review describes clinical perspectives, regulatory considerations, and future directions in integrating emerging virotherapeutic agents into precision oncology.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on dengue virus (DENV) and its role in oncolytic virotherapy, examining its tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms. It also explores the potential of DENV as a therapeutic agent in the context of precision oncology.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive analysis of the literature on dengue virus and its applications in oncolytic virotherapy. It includes both engineered and naturally occurring viruses, with an emphasis on DENV. The review discusses clinical perspectives, regulatory considerations, and future directions, but does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that dengue virus and other related flaviviruses have intrinsic oncolytic potential, with specific tumour tropism, immune evasion strategies, and immunogenic cell death mechanisms that could be harnessed for next-generation therapeutic platforms. It also notes that DENV is among the promising candidates for oncolytic virotherapy.
Controversies and gaps
The review acknowledges concerns related to the safety, pathogenicity, and host immune responses of dengue virus and other related viruses. It also identifies gaps in understanding the precise mechanisms of tumour targeting and the long-term biosafety of these viruses in therapeutic applications.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that further research is needed to improve the therapeutic efficacy and biosafety of dengue virus in oncolytic virotherapy. It also emphasizes the importance of integrating emerging virotherapeutic agents into precision oncology frameworks, including clinical trials and regulatory guidelines.

登革病毒感染中循环微小RNA的系统性综述与荟萃分析:生物标志物、发病机制及临床意义

Systematic review and meta-analysis of circulating microRNAs in dengue infection: biomarkers, pathogenesis, and clinical implications

查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于登革病毒及其相关疾病,探讨循环微小RNA(miRNA)作为预测重症病例(如登革出血热与登革休克综合征)生物标志物的潜力。核心问题是miRNA是否可作为疾病严重程度与进展的可靠指标。
证据基础与方法
本综述为五项符合条件研究的系统性综合,数据来源包括PubMed、Scopus、Science Direct及Web of Science。研究质量采用纽卡斯尔-渥太华量表评估。使用RStudio进行定量荟萃分析,评估诊断曲线下面积(AUC)指标及差异miRNA表达的标准化均数差(SMD)。
共识与结论
确定了miR-146a、miR-150、miR-378、miR-21-5p及miR-1246等关键miRNA,其可调控免疫应答、炎症及病毒复制。荟萃分析显示循环miRNA具有高诊断准确性,AUC值范围为0.79至1.00,但存在显著统计异质性(I平方值超过89%)。
争议与缺口
综述指出,尽管miRNA在生物标志物方面显示出潜力,但其失调并非一类统一的临床工具。候选miRNA跨越不同的转化层级。此外,综述强调在优先考虑miRNA候选物用于临床转化时,需关注预分析与生物学异质性。
科研与实践启示
综述建议未来研究应重点验证最具可行性的miRNA候选物用于临床,特别是在重症登革热背景下。同时强调需解决生物学与预分析变异性,以提高基于miRNA诊断的可靠性。

Systematic review and meta-analysis of circulating microRNAs in dengue infection: biomarkers, pathogenesis, and clinical implications

Authors: Gokulakannan Venkatesan, Wan Suriana Wan Ab Rahman, Wan Nazatul Shima Shahidan, Norliana Ghazali, Hisham Atan Edinur
Original abstract (English)
This systematic review and meta-analysis synthesises findings from studies on circulating miRNAs in dengue infection, sourced from PubMed, Scopus, Science Direct, and Web of Science, with quality assessed by the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale (PROSPERO: CRD42024521685). Rather than viewing dysregulated microRNAs as a uniform class of clinical tools, this review highlights that candidate microRNAs span distinct translational tiers. Data availability No datasets were generated or analysed during the current study. Available from: /pmc/articles/PMC3510884/ - Heilman JM, De Wolff J, Beards GM, Basden BJ (2014) Dengue fever: A Wikipedia clinical review. https://doi.org/10.1128/JCM.00221-19 - Li X, Liao C, Wu J, Yi B, Zha R, Deng Q, et al (2024) Distinct serum exosomal miRNA profiles detected in acute and asymptomatic dengue infections: A community-based study in Baiyun District, Guangzhou. https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pntd.0010836 - Moallemi S, Lloyd AR, Rodrigo C (2023) Early biomarkers for prediction of severe manifestations of dengue fever: a systematic review and a meta-analysis. Abstract Dengue infection is a mosquito-borne disease that can cause mild illness or severe haemorrhagic fever. Predictive biomarkers for severe cases, especially those with bleeding manifestations or plasma leakage, are currently lacking. Quantitative meta-analyses were conducted using RStudio to evaluate diagnostic Area Under the Curve (AUC) metrics across five eligible studies and Standardised Mean Differences (SMD) for differential miRNA expression. Key miRNAs, such as miR-146a, miR-150, miR-378, miR-21-5p, and miR-1246, were found to regulate immune responses, inflammation, and viral replication. Meta-analysis demonstrated high overall diagnostic accuracy for circulating miRNAs, with individual...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on dengue virus and its associated disease, examining circulating microRNAs (miRNAs) as potential biomarkers for predicting severe cases, such as dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF) and dengue shock syndrome (DSS). The central question is whether miRNAs can serve as reliable indicators of disease severity and progression.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic synthesis of findings from five eligible studies, sourced from PubMed, Scopus, Science Direct, and Web of Science. The quality of these studies was assessed using the Newcastle–Ottawa Scale. Quantitative meta-analyses were conducted using RStudio to evaluate diagnostic Area Under the Curve (AUC) metrics and Standardised Mean Differences (SMD) for differential miRNA expression.
Consensus and conclusions
Key miRNAs such as miR-146a, miR-150, miR-378, miR-21-5p, and miR-1246 were identified as regulators of immune responses, inflammation, and viral replication. The meta-analysis showed high diagnostic accuracy for circulating miRNAs, with AUC values ranging from 0.79 to 1.00, although substantial statistical heterogeneity was observed (I-squared exceeding 89%).
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that while miRNAs show promise as biomarkers, their dysregulation is not a uniform class of clinical tools. Instead, candidate miRNAs span distinct translational tiers. Additionally, the review highlights the need to consider pre-analytical and biological heterogeneity when prioritizing miRNA candidates for clinical translation.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on validating the most viable miRNA candidates for clinical use, particularly in the context of severe dengue. It also emphasizes the importance of addressing biological and pre-analytical variability to improve the reliability of miRNA-based diagnostics.

登革病毒在变化的流行病学图景中的现状:现有证据、未解决的挑战及公共卫生考量

Dengue Vaccines in a Changing Epidemiological Landscape: Current Evidence, Unresolved Challenges, and Public Health Considerations
作者： Susanna Esposito, Nicola Principi
摘要中文翻译背景:登革病毒已迅速扩展至传统热带和亚热带地区以外,这主要由气候变化、城市化、人口流动以及高效传播的媒介蚊种Aedes 媒介的扩散所驱动。疫苗接种是登革病毒预防的重要组成部分,但其发展受到四种病毒血清型、基础血清状态的变异性以及需要平衡且持久的四价免疫的挑战。方法:我们对PubMed/MEDLINE、Google Scholar、ClinicalTrials.gov及相关公共卫生和监管来源进行了叙述性综述。对登革病毒流行病学、免疫发病机制、已批准的疫苗、先进候选疫苗、有效性、免疫原性、安全性、持久性和实施证据进行了批判性评估。优先考虑随机试验、长期随访研究、监管评估和监测数据。证据以描述性方式综合,未进行正式的荟萃分析或偏倚风险评估。结果:CYD-TDV是首个获批的登革病毒疫苗,但仅限于有既往感染记录的个体,因为血清阴性受试者可能面临更高的重症登革热风险。TAK-003在基础血清阳性和基础血清阴性人群中均显示出对病毒学确诊登革热及登革热相关住院的总体有效性。然而,其保护效果在不同血清型之间存在异质性,某些血清型与血清状态的交叉群体的证据仍有限或不确定。Butantan-DV提供了一种有前景的单剂量策略,但其更广泛的应用需要额外的长期安全性、有效性及血清型特异性数据。灭活、DNA、病毒载体、类病毒颗粒和mRNA 疫苗仍处于研究阶段。结论:登革病毒疫苗接种应与监测、媒介蚊种媒介控制、临床准备和风险沟通相结合。在高传播地区,基于人群的疫苗接种最为合适,而在温带地区,选择性、基于风险的策略则更可取。持续的药物不良反应监测和有效性追踪对于指导安全和公平的实施至关重要。

CYD-TDV是首个获批的登革病毒疫苗,但仅限于有既往感染记录的个体,因为血清阴性受试者可能面临更高的重症登革热风险。TAK-003在基础血清阳性和基础血清阴性人群中均显示出对病毒学确诊登革热及登革热相关住院的总体有效性。Butantan-DV提供了一种有前景的单剂量策略,但其更广泛的应用需要额外的长期安全性、有效性及血清型特异性数据。灭活、DNA、病毒载体、类病毒颗粒和mRNA 疫苗仍处于研究阶段。

登革病毒疫苗接种应与监测、媒介蚊种媒介控制、临床准备和风险沟通相结合。持续的药物不良反应监测和有效性追踪对于指导安全和公平的实施至关重要。在高传播地区,基于人群的疫苗接种最为合适,而在温带地区,选择性、基于风险的策略则更可取。

Dengue Vaccines in a Changing Epidemiological Landscape: Current Evidence, Unresolved Challenges, and Public Health Considerations

Authors: Susanna Esposito, Nicola Principi
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue has expanded rapidly beyond traditional tropical and subtropical regions, driven by climate change, urbanization, population mobility, and the spread of competent Aedes vectors. Vaccination is an increasingly important component of dengue prevention, but development has been complicated by four viral serotypes, antibody-dependent enhancement, variable baseline serostatus, and the need for balanced and durable tetravalent immunity. Methods: We conducted a narrative review of PubMed/MEDLINE, Google Scholar, ClinicalTrials.gov, and relevant public health and regulatory sources. Evidence on dengue epidemiology, immunopathogenesis, licensed vaccines, advanced candidates, efficacy, immunogenicity, safety, durability, and implementation was critically evaluated. Priority was given to randomized trials, long-term follow-up studies, regulatory assessments, and surveillance data. Evidence was synthesized descriptively without formal meta-analysis or risk-of-bias assessment. Results: CYD-TDV was the first licensed dengue vaccine but is restricted to individuals with documented previous infection because seronegative recipients may experience an increased risk of severe dengue. TAK-003 has demonstrated overall efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue and dengue-related hospitalization in both baseline-seropositive and baseline-seronegative populations. However, protection is heterogeneous by serotype, and evidence remains limited or uncertain for some serotype-by-serostatus strata. Butantan-DV offers a promising single-dose strategy, but broader use requires additional long-term safety, effectiveness, and serotype-specific data. Inactivated, DNA, viral-vectored, virus-like particle, and mRNA vaccines remain investigational. Conclusions: Dengue vaccination should be integrated with surveillance, vector control, clinical preparedness, and risk communication. Population-based vaccination is most appropriate in high-transmission settings, whereas selective, risk-based strategies are preferable in temperate regions. Continued pharmacovigilance and effectiveness monitoring are essential to guide safe and equitable implementation.

Evidence was synthesized descriptively without formal meta-analysis or risk-of-bias assessment.
Consensus and conclusions
CYD-TDV was the first licensed dengue vaccine but is restricted to individuals with documented previous infection because seronegative recipients may experience an increased risk of severe dengue. TAK-003 has demonstrated overall efficacy against virologically confirmed dengue and dengue-related hospitalization in both baseline-seropositive and baseline-seronegative populations. Butantan-DV offers a promising single-dose strategy, but broader use requires additional long-term safety, effectiveness, and serotype-specific data. Inactivated, DNA, viral-vectored, virus-like particle, and mRNA vaccines remain investigational.

Continued pharmacovigilance and effectiveness monitoring are essential to guide safe and equitable implementation. Population-based vaccination is most appropriate in high-transmission settings, whereas selective, risk-based strategies are preferable in temperate regions.

登革病毒 诱导的内皮功能障碍的机械驱动因素

Mechanical drivers of dengue virus-induced endothelial dysfunction
作者： Sofía Mayordomo-Barrero, Miguel Vicente-Manzanares, Miguel Vicente‐Manzanares
摘要中文翻译全球有超过十亿人面临感染登革热的风险。登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种由伊蚊传播的 ssRNA(+)-黄病毒。虽然大多数感染是轻微的(无并发症的登革热),但严重/出血性登革热携带大量 死亡率 且缺乏特定的 死亡率。重症登革热 的特点是内皮功能障碍,在原发和继发感染期间都可能出现。在后者中,感染的 抗体依赖性增强 是一个公认的危险因素,尽管在这两种情况下病毒感染与内皮屏障衰竭之间的机制联系仍不清楚。除了对内皮细胞造成炎症损伤外,DENV 和其他黄病毒还可以引发内皮细胞向间充质迁移表型的转分化,从而削弱细胞间的连接并损害血管完整性。在这篇综述中,我们讨论了黄病毒诱导的内皮转分化的机械成分及其对内皮功能障碍的影响,强调了治疗干预的前瞻性机械生物学目标。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于登革病毒诱导的内皮功能障碍的机械驱动因素,重点探讨登革病毒 1–4如何促进内皮细胞横向分化及血管完整性受损。核心问题是机械力如何影响登革热疾病中的内皮屏障功能衰竭。
证据基础与方法
本综述为既往文献中关于登革病毒及其对内皮细胞影响的叙事性综合。未进行荟萃分析或汇总估计,但讨论了登革病毒诱导内皮功能障碍的生物学机制及其意义。综述包含登革病毒的背景信息及其传播,以及其与重症的关联。
共识与结论
综述得出结论:内皮功能障碍是重症登革热的关键特征,且在继发感染期间的抗体依赖性增强是一项公认的重症风险因素。综述强调,尽管内皮的炎症性损伤是已知机制,但病毒感染与内皮屏障功能衰竭之间的确切机制性关联仍不清楚。此外,综述指出登革病毒(DENV)及其他黄病毒可触发内皮细胞向间充质、迁移表型横向分化,从而削弱细胞间连接并损害血管完整性。
争议与缺口
综述指出,在原发及继发感染中,病毒感染与内皮屏障功能衰竭之间的机制性关联仍存在不明确之处。同时指出,尽管内皮横向分化被提出为一种机制,但支持该机制的证据尚未完全确立。综述承认,除炎症性损伤外,其他机械因素可能参与内皮功能障碍,但这些因素尚未得到充分表征。
科研与实践启示
综述建议,理解登革病毒诱导内皮功能障碍的机械驱动因素,或可助力识别前瞻性机械生物学治疗靶标。综述强调需进一步研究以明确内皮屏障功能衰竭的机制,并探索预防或治疗重症登革热的潜在策略。

Mechanical drivers of dengue virus-induced endothelial dysfunction

Authors: Sofía Mayordomo-Barrero, Miguel Vicente-Manzanares, Miguel Vicente‐Manzanares
Original abstract (English)
More than one billion individuals worldwide are at risk of contracting dengue. Dengue virus (DENV) is a ssRNA(+)-flavivirus transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes. While most infections are mild (uncomplicated dengue), severe/hemorrhagic dengue carries substantial mortality and lacks specific treatment. Severe dengue is characterized by endothelial dysfunction, which can arise during both primary and secondary infection. In the latter, antibody-dependent enhancement of infection is a well-recognized risk factor, although the mechanistic links between viral infection and endothelial barrier failure remain unclear in either setting. Beyond inflammatory injury to the endothelium, DENV and other flaviviruses can trigger endothelial trans-differentiation toward a mesenchymal, migratory phenotype, weakening cell–cell junctions and compromising vascular integrity. In this review, we discuss the mechanical component of flavivirus-induced endothelial trans-differentiation and its impact on endothelial dysfunction, highlighting prospective mechanobiological targets for therapeutic intervention.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the mechanical drivers of dengue virus-induced endothelial dysfunction, specifically examining how dengue viruses 1–4 contribute to endothelial trans-differentiation and vascular integrity compromise. The central question is how mechanical forces influence endothelial barrier failure in dengue disease.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature on dengue virus and its effects on endothelial cells. It does not report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but discusses the biological mechanisms and implications of dengue-induced endothelial dysfunction. The review includes general background information on dengue virus and its transmission, as well as its association with severe disease.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that endothelial dysfunction is a key feature of severe dengue, and that antibody-dependent enhancement during secondary infection is a well-recognized risk factor. It highlights that while the inflammatory injury to the endothelium is a known mechanism, the precise mechanistic links between viral infection and endothelial barrier failure remain unclear. Additionally, it notes that DENV and other flaviviruses can trigger endothelial trans-differentiation toward a mesenchymal, migratory phenotype, which weakens cell–cell junctions and compromises vascular integrity.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies a lack of clarity regarding the mechanistic links between viral infection and endothelial barrier failure in both primary and secondary infections. It also points out that while endothelial trans-differentiation is a proposed mechanism, the evidence supporting this is not fully established. The review acknowledges that beyond inflammatory injury, other mechanical factors may contribute to endothelial dysfunction, but these are not yet well characterized.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that understanding the mechanical drivers of dengue virus-induced endothelial dysfunction could lead to the identification of prospective mechanobiological targets for therapeutic intervention. It emphasizes the need for further research to clarify the mechanisms underlying endothelial barrier failure and to explore potential strategies for preventing or treating severe dengue.

共流行环境中基孔肯雅病毒血清学交叉反应:机制、诊断意义和流行病学后果

Chikungunya Virus serological cross-reactivity in co-endemic settings: Mechanisms, Diagnostic implications, and epidemiological consequences
作者： Ikeoluwa Feyisayo Aina, O. Opaleye, Dorcas Ayomide Atilade, Foluke Julianah Adetutu
摘要中文翻译基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 会引起急性发热性疾病并伴有关节痛,血清学检测仍然是其 诊断 和 监测 的核心。然而,在多种虫媒病毒共同循环的环境中,基于抗体的结果因交叉反应而变得复杂。本综述区分了影响 CHIKV 血清学 的三种相关但不同的现象:生物交叉反应性,由相关病毒之间真正的抗原重叠引起;与检测相关的非目标反应性,其中诊断平台在病毒之间没有建立生物学关系的情况下产生阳性信号;诊断错误分类,将两者混为一谈的下游流行病学后果。将该框架应用于五个病毒对,我们发现CHIKV-O'nyong-nyong病毒(ONNV)、CHIKV-Mayaro病毒(MAYV)和CHIKV-罗斯河病毒(RRV)关系得到了共享的甲病毒抗原结构的支持,并且在一些研究中还得到了功能性交叉中和的支持。相比之下,登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 相关血清中报告的 CHIKV 反应性缺乏既定的生物学基础,最好归类为未解决机制的检测相关非靶标反应性。这些区别很重要:未经调整的血清阳性率可能会夸大或掩盖 CHIKV 传播 的估计值,并且没有一个诊断平台可以解决每个病毒对的问题。我们认为,共流行环境中的 CHIKV 诊断 需要分层策略:急性期进行分子检测,随后进行抗原告知和多重 血清学,以及基于中和的确认(其中归因至关重要),并结合 监测 的交叉反应感知统计模型。加强分析验证和确认性检测,特别是在尼日利亚等 CHIKV、DENV 和相关甲病毒共同传播的非洲地区,对于提高诊断准确性和流行病学推断至关重要。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述研究了在共流行环境中影响基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 诊断的血清学交叉反应性,重点关注病原体 CHIKV、其疾病表现,以及如何改进基于抗体的检测以避免在多种虫媒病毒共同传播的地区发生错误分类的核心问题。
证据基础与方法
这是一篇叙述性综述,使用 CHIKV 和相关甲病毒(包括 CHIKV-O'nyong-nyong 病毒 (ONNV)、CHIKV-Mayaro 病毒 (MAYV) 和 CHIKV-Ross River 病毒 (RRV))研究的证据区分了三种现象——生物交叉反应性、检测相关的非靶标反应性和诊断错误分类,同时指出 CHIKV 反应性缺乏生物学基础。登革病毒 (DENV) 和寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 相关血清。
共识与结论
该综述得出的结论是,CHIKV 与 ONNV、MAYV 和 RRV 具有相同的抗原结构,并得到多项研究中共享的甲病毒抗原结构和功能性交叉中和的支持,而 DENV 和 ZIKV 相关血清中的 CHIKV 反应性在没有既定生物学基础的情况下,更好地归类为与检测相关的非靶标反应性。
争议与缺口
该审查强调,未经调整的血清阳性率可能会夸大或掩盖 CHIKV 传播 的估计值,并且没有一个诊断平台可以解决每个病毒对的交叉反应性,特别是在多种虫媒病毒一起传播的共流行环境中。
科研与实践启示
该综述建议在共流行环境下对 CHIKV 采取分层诊断策略:急性期进行分子检测,之后进行抗原信息和多重 血清学,基于中和的确认(其中归因至关重要),以及 监测 的交叉反应感知统计模型,重点是加强尼日利亚等非洲地区的测定验证和确认性测试。

Chikungunya Virus serological cross-reactivity in co-endemic settings: Mechanisms, Diagnostic implications, and epidemiological consequences

Authors: Ikeoluwa Feyisayo Aina, O. Opaleye, Dorcas Ayomide Atilade, Foluke Julianah Adetutu
Original abstract (English)
Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) causes an acute febrile illness with debilitating arthralgia, and serological assays remain central to its diagnosis and surveillance. However, antibody-based results are complicated by cross-reactivity in settings where multiple arboviruses co-circulate. This review distinguishes three related but distinct phenomena affecting CHIKV serology: biological cross-reactivity, arising from genuine antigenic overlap between related viruses; assay-associated non-target reactivity, in which a diagnostic platform generates a positive signal without an established biological relationship between the viruses; and diagnostic misclassification, the downstream epidemiological consequence of conflating the two. Applying this framework to five virus pairs, we find that CHIKV–O'nyong-nyong virus (ONNV), CHIKV–Mayaro virus (MAYV) and CHIKV–Ross River virus (RRV) relationships are supported by shared alphavirus antigenic architecture and, in several studies, by functional cross-neutralisation. By contrast, CHIKV reactivity reported in dengue virus (DENV)- and Zika virus (ZIKV)-associated sera lacks an established biological basis and is better classified as assay-associated non-target reactivity of unresolved mechanism. These distinctions matter: unadjusted seropositivity can inflate or obscure CHIKV transmission estimates, and no single diagnostic platform resolves the problem for every virus pair. We argue that CHIKV diagnosis in co-endemic settings requires a layered strategy: molecular detection during the acute phase, antigenically informed and multiplex serology thereafter, and neutralisation-based confirmation where attribution is critical, combined with cross-reactivity-aware statistical modelling for surveillance. Strengthening assay validation and confirmatory testing, particularly in African settings such as Nigeria where CHIKV, DENV and related alphaviruses circulate together, is essential for improving diagnostic accuracy and epidemiological inference.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review examines serological cross-reactivity affecting Chikungunya virus (CHIKV) diagnostics in co-endemic settings, focusing on the pathogen CHIKV, its disease manifestations, and the central question of how antibody-based assays can be improved to avoid misclassification in regions where multiple arboviruses co-circulate.
Evidence base and method
This is a narrative review that distinguishes three phenomena—biological cross-reactivity, assay-associated non-target reactivity, and diagnostic misclassification—using evidence from studies on CHIKV and related alphaviruses, including CHIKV–O'nyong-nyong virus (ONNV), CHIKV–Mayaro virus (MAYV), and CHIKV–Ross River virus (RRV), while noting the lack of biological basis for CHIKV reactivity in dengue virus (DENV)- and Zika virus (ZIKV)-associated sera.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that CHIKV shares antigenic architecture with ONNV, MAYV, and RRV, supported by shared alphavirus antigenic structure and functional cross-neutralisation in several studies, whereas CHIKV reactivity in DENV- and ZIKV-associated sera is better classified as assay-associated non-target reactivity without an established biological basis.
Controversies and gaps
The review highlights that unadjusted seropositivity can inflate or obscure CHIKV transmission estimates, and no single diagnostic platform resolves cross-reactivity for every virus pair, particularly in co-endemic settings where multiple arboviruses circulate together.
Research and practice implications
The review recommends a layered diagnostic strategy for CHIKV in co-endemic settings: molecular detection during the acute phase, antigenically informed and multiplex serology thereafter, neutralisation-based confirmation where attribution is critical, and cross-reactivity-aware statistical modelling for surveillance, with a focus on strengthening assay validation and confirmatory testing in African settings such as Nigeria.

内科医生面临新出现的虫媒病毒疾病:是时候做好准备了。

Internists facing emerging arboviral diseases: time to get prepared.
作者： Emanuele Durante-Mangoni, Augusto Delle Femine, Raffaella Gallo, Ciro di Gennaro, EFIM working group on Infectious Diseases in Internal Medicine (see supplementary file for list), Emanuele Durante‐Mangoni, the EFIM working group on Infectious Diseases in Internal Medicine (see supplementary file for list), Agnès Lefort des Ylouses, José Manuel Ramos-Rincón, Fausto Roxo

查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注登革病毒及其相关疾病,包括登革热、登革出血热、登革热休克综合征和重症登革热。核心问题是内科医生如何准备和管理新出现的虫媒病毒疾病,特别是由 登革病毒 引起的疾病。
证据基础与方法
该综述是对 登革病毒 和相关虫媒病毒现有文献的叙述性综合。它不报告特定的数据库、搜索日期或荟萃分析方法。该综述包括一般背景和方法信息,但没有有关研究设计或人群选择的明确细节。
共识与结论
该综述强调,登革病毒 1-4 会导致登革热疾病,其范围从无症状到严重形式,例如登革出血热和登革休克综合征。它还指出,寨卡病毒、西尼罗河病毒和黄热病等其他黄病毒在生物学上相关,但不是本次审查的主要焦点。
争议与缺口
该综述指出了由于非特异性临床表现和潜在的血清学交叉反应而导致诊断登革热感染的挑战。它还指出,具体的 抗病毒药物 疗法尚不可用,因此支持治疗和 媒介 控制作为关键的管理策略。
科研与实践启示
该审查强调在管理登革热感染方面需要进行早期临床怀疑、适当的实验室检测和支持性护理。它还强调了内科医生在指导鉴别监测、识别重症病例以及支持监测和预防工作方面的重要性。

Internists facing emerging arboviral diseases: time to get prepared.

Authors: Emanuele Durante-Mangoni, Augusto Delle Femine, Raffaella Gallo, Ciro di Gennaro, EFIM working group on Infectious Diseases in Internal Medicine (see supplementary file for list), Emanuele Durante‐Mangoni, the EFIM working group on Infectious Diseases in Internal Medicine (see supplementary file for list), Agnès Lefort des Ylouses, José Manuel Ramos-Rincón, Fausto Roxo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Arboviral diseases are emerging and affect approximately 60 million individuals yearly. Climate change, vector migration patterns, urbanisation and global travel are boosting their spread beyond traditionally endemic regions, including Europe. This narrative review provides a practical and clinically oriented overview of the main arthropod-borne viral infections. We summarize their epidemiology, virological features, and transmission, highlighting shared as well as distinctive clinical presentations, diagnostic approaches and current management strategies. Although most infections are asymptomatic or self-limiting, severe complications may occur, including dengue haemorrhagic manifestations and shock, West Nile neuroinvasive disease, congenital and neurological complications of Zika, and chronic disabling arthritis after Chikungunya virus infection. Diagnosis remains challenging due to often nonspecific presentations, and laboratory interpretation may be complicated by timing of sampling and serological cross-reactivity. Since specific antiviral therapies remain largely unavailable, early clinical suspicion, appropriate laboratory testing, supportive care, vaccination (where applicable) and vector-control measures are central to patient management and public health response. Internists frequently evaluate patients with undifferentiated febrile illness in emergency departments and internal medicine units, and play a key role in the correct management of these infections, guiding differential diagnosis, identifying severe presentations, and supporting surveillance and prevention efforts in an era of expanding arboviral risk.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on dengue virus and its associated diseases, including dengue fever, dengue hemorrhagic fever, dengue shock syndrome, and severe dengue. The central question is how internists can prepare for and manage emerging arboviral diseases, particularly those caused by dengue viruses.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature on dengue viruses and related arboviruses. It does not report specific databases, search dates, or meta-analysis methods. The review includes general background and methods information, but no explicit details on study design or population selection.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that dengue viruses 1–4 cause dengue disease, which can range from asymptomatic to severe forms such as dengue hemorrhagic fever and dengue shock syndrome. It also notes that other flaviviruses like Zika, West Nile, and yellow fever are biologically related but not the primary focus of this review.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies challenges in diagnosing dengue infections due to nonspecific clinical presentations and potential serological cross-reactivity. It also notes that specific antiviral therapies are not yet available, leaving supportive care and vector control as critical management strategies.
Research and practice implications
The review emphasizes the need for early clinical suspicion, appropriate laboratory testing, and supportive care in managing dengue infections. It also highlights the importance of internists in guiding differential diagnosis, identifying severe cases, and supporting surveillance and prevention efforts.

气候变化下的新发感染性疾病

Innovative Diseases of Infection and Changes in the Climate
作者： Ayat Munaf Hameed
摘要中文翻译新发感染性疾病(EIDs)正在全球范围内扩散,而气候变化正在加速这一进程。气候变化正在影响疾病的生态循环、媒介和宿主,主要通过气温上升、降水模式改变以及极端天气事件频率增加所致。本叙事综述简要总结了针对气候敏感性传染病的研究及其主要作用机制。干旱、洪水和基础设施故障与食源性及水源性疾病(如霍乱和钩端螺旋体病)的关联日益密切。由媒介传播的疾病正在向温带和高原地区扩散,包括登革热、基孔肯雅热、疟疾和莱姆病。在环境变化的背景下,更多的人兽共患跨物种溢出(如埃博拉病毒、尼帕病毒和SARS-CoV-2)被观察到。这还涉及新的挑战,如永久冻土层中微生物的释放以及嗜热真菌感染。本综述未提供新的流行病学模型,而是利用了《全球疾病负担》(GBD)的当前估计值。根据估计,气候变化影响下的脆弱地区(如南亚、拉丁美洲和撒哈拉以南非洲)的传染病负担预计将上升。监测系统不足、气候变化引发的健康不平等以及数据源中断是应对工作面临的最大缺口。病原体基因组进展、卫星监测、基于人工智能的预测以及‘一个健康’方法为提前防控提供了有效途径。构建跨学科、平等且对气候变化有响应的公共卫生系统,对于降低与气候变化相关的传染病风险至关重要。未来针对传染病与气候变化的研究应侧重于非媒介传播的、直接传播的病原体,并扩大在热带地区的研究。在低收入和中等收入国家,当地研究通常被忽视。缺乏全面、社会包容且区域均衡的传染病研究,阻碍了我们对气候变化对健康真实影响的理解。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述关注气候变化对登革热的影响,包括气候因素如何影响该病毒的传播,以及与其传播和影响相关的新挑战。
证据基础与方法
本综述是现有气候敏感性传染病文献的叙事综合,采用《全球疾病负担》(GBD)的当前估计值,未引入新的流行病学模型。它包含关于气候变化对疾病生态和媒介影响的一般性观察。
共识与结论
本综述强调,气候变化正在通过气温上升、降水模式改变和极端天气事件影响疾病、媒介和宿主的生态循环。同时指出,包括登革热在内的传染病负担预计将在南亚、拉丁美洲和撒哈拉以南非洲等脆弱地区上升。
争议与缺口
本综述指出,监测系统不足、气候变化引发的健康不平等以及数据源中断是应对气候相关传染病的主要挑战。同时强调缺乏全面、社会包容且区域均衡的研究,特别是在低收入和中等收入国家。
科研与实践启示
本综述建议未来研究应侧重于非媒介传播的、直接传播的病原体,并扩大在热带地区的研究。同时强调整合病原体基因组进展、卫星监测、基于人工智能的预测以及‘一个健康’方法,以有效应对这些挑战。

Innovative Diseases of Infection and Changes in the Climate

Authors: Ayat Munaf Hameed
Original abstract (English)
Emerging infectious diseases (EIDs) are on the move, and with climate change.Climate change is influencing the global spread of EIDs. Climate change is impacting the ecological cycles of diseases, vectors, and hosts because of increasing temperatures, changes in rainfall and increased frequency of extreme weather events. This narrative review briefly summarizes the research conducted on climate-sensitive infectious diseases and the main mechanisms through which these changes are thought to occur. Drought, flood and infrastructure failures are increasingly associated with food- and water-borne diseases (FWBD), such as cholera and leptospirosis. Vector-borne diseases that are spreading to temperate and highland areas include dengue, chikungunya, malaria, and Lyme disease. More zoonotic spillovers (such as Ebola, Nipah and SARS-CoV-2) are observed in the environment where there are changes. This covers new challenges such as microorganisms from perma frost and thermotolerant infections of fungus. Instead of offering new epidemiological modeling, this review makes use of current estimates of the Global Burden of Disease (GBD). According to the estimations, the burden of infectious diseases is expected to rise in susceptible regions affected by climate change, such as South Asia, Latin America, and sub-Saharan Africa. Inadequate monitoring systems, health inequities brought on by climate change, and disrupted data sources are some of the biggest gaps in response.Pathogen genome advancements, satellite surveillance, AI-based forecasting, and One Health approaches provide useful ways to stop issues before they start. Creating transdisciplinary, egalitarian, and climate-responsive public health systems is essential to reducing the rising risks of infectious illnesses connected with climate change. Further research on infectious diseases and climate change should concentrate on non-vector-borne, direct-transmission pathogens and expand research in tropical areas. In low- and middle-income countries, local research was usually ignored. The dearth of thorough, socially inclusive, and regionally balanced research on infectious diseases has hindered our comprehension of the true effects of climate change on health.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the impact of climate change on dengue virus and dengue disease, including how climate factors influence the spread of the virus and the emergence of new challenges related to its transmission and effects.
Evidence base and method
The review is a narrative synthesis of existing literature on climate-sensitive infectious diseases, utilizing current estimates from the Global Burden of Disease (GBD) without introducing new epidemiological modeling. It includes general observations about climate change's influence on disease ecology and vectors.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that climate change is affecting the ecological cycles of diseases, vectors, and hosts, particularly through rising temperatures, altered rainfall patterns, and extreme weather events. It also notes that the burden of infectious diseases, including dengue, is expected to increase in vulnerable regions such as South Asia, Latin America, and sub-Saharan Africa.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies gaps in monitoring systems, health inequities, and disrupted data sources as major challenges in responding to climate-related infectious diseases. It also points out the lack of thorough, socially inclusive, and regionally balanced research, especially in low- and middle-income countries.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on non-vector-borne, direct-transmission pathogens and expand studies in tropical areas. It also emphasizes the importance of integrating pathogen genome advancements, satellite surveillance, AI-based forecasting, and One Health approaches to address these challenges effectively.

Vanuatu Dengue Outbreak Situation Report #10 (South-West Efate, Shefa Province) - ReliefWeb
新闻简报瓦努阿图登革热疫情目前在谢法省南-西埃法特地区持续活跃,自疫情暴发以来已报告91例实验室确诊病例,其中EW33周新增15例,为迄今最高单周病例数。流行血清型为登革病毒1型(DENV-1)。尚未报告登革热重症病例或死亡病例,共报告14例登革热相关住院病例,所有患者均已康复出院。登革热疫情仍在持续,社区传播活动遍及埃法特岛多个地区,包括埃拉科尔、维拉港、埃拉塔普和潘戈。本报告发布于2026年8月21日,为部分摘要,原文全文正文无法提取。疫情仍在监测中,未报告严重的卫生系统影响。

登革热疫情发生在瓦努阿图谢法省南-西埃法特地区,涉及埃拉科尔、维拉港、埃拉塔普和潘戈等多个区域。

A high priorityPublic-health news

Vanuatu Dengue Outbreak Situation Report #10 (South-West Efate, Shefa Province) - ReliefWeb

News Brief
Vanuatu's dengue outbreak, currently active in South-West Efate, Shefa Province, has reported 91 laboratory-confirmed cases since its start, including 15 new cases in EW33, the highest weekly count to date. Dengue Virus Type 1 (DENV-1) is the circulating serotype. No severe dengue cases or deaths have been recorded, and 14 dengue-related hospitalisations have been reported, with all patients recovering and discharged. The outbreak is ongoing with community transmission across multiple areas of Efate Island, such as Erakor, Port Vila, Eratap, and Pango. The report, published on 21 August 2026, is a partial summary, and the full original article body could not be extracted. The situation remains under monitoring, with no indication of severe health-system effects reported.

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 25 August 2026 - ReliefWeb
新闻简报截至2026年8月25日,太平洋多个地区报告登革热活动持续或升高,并发布血清型特异性预警。美属萨摩亚自其暴发暴发起累计报告1,044例实验室确诊病例,近期每周病例数已降低,主要集中在青少年及年轻成人。马绍尔群岛于8月21日在发现8例确诊病例后宣布启动活跃的登革热 暴发,并采取协调应对措施,包括主动病例搜索及媒介防控。新喀里多尼亚于8月16日宣布登革热疫情结束,自1月以来累计报告2,235例病例及4例死亡,流行株仅为DENV-1。萨摩亚新增病例较前一周上升47%,瓦努阿图西南埃法特暴发累计报告91例确诊病例且无重症病例。瓦利斯和富图纳报告70例确诊或疑似病例,其中传播主要集中在富图纳。血清型数据显示多数地区以DENV-1为主,萨摩亚同时存在DENV-2共同流行。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布时间:2026年8月25日;事件发生时间范围:2026年1月1日至8月21日
地点与对象
美属萨摩亚(美国领地)、马绍尔群岛(马朱罗环礁)、新喀里多尼亚、萨摩亚、瓦努阿图(西南埃法特岛,谢法省)、瓦利斯和富图纳
事件
太平洋多个地区报告登革热暴发或活跃传播,并发布血清型特异性预警:美属萨摩亚(DENV-1与DENV-2蓝色预警)、马绍尔群岛(DENV-?红色预警)、萨摩亚(DENV-1与DENV-2蓝色预警)、瓦努阿图(DENV-1红色预警)、瓦利斯和富图纳(DENV-1红色预警)。新喀里多尼亚于2026年8月16日宣布登革热疫情结束。
规模、影响与风险
美属萨摩亚:自暴发暴发起累计1,044例实验室确诊病例;近期每周病例数较低(EpiWeek 30–32为2–3例)。马绍尔群岛:8例确诊,15例阴性,无死亡;暴发于2026年8月21日宣布。新喀里多尼亚:2,235例病例(2,028例为本地感染),80例住院,4例死亡(1例合并钩端螺旋体病,3例存在合并症);仅流行DENV-1;疫情宣布结束。萨摩亚:EpiWeek 32新增22例(较前一周上升47%),3例实验室确诊;自2025年1月1日起累计临床诊断20,157例及实验室确诊5,536例,9例死亡;流行株为DENV-1(52%)与DENV-2(48%)。
应对、状态与不确定性
美属萨摩亚:自2026年1月高峰期以来每周病例数持续下降;病例主要集中在11–20岁年龄组;蓝色预警仍维持。马绍尔群岛:在EpiNet与国家灾害委员会协调下开展主动暴发应对;包括主动病例搜索、媒介防控、实验室转诊及公共卫生宣教;住宅周边喷洒杀幼虫剂;血清型检测待出。新喀里多尼亚:疫情宣布结束;仍存在低水平传播;大努美阿地区实施沃尔巴克氏菌干预。萨摩亚:蓝色预警仍维持;两种血清型共同流行;73%的病例年龄低于15岁。
A high priorityPublic-health news

Epidemic and emerging disease alerts in the Pacific as of 25 August 2026 - ReliefWeb

News Brief
As of 25 August 2026, multiple Pacific territories reported ongoing or elevated dengue activity with serotype-specific alerts. American Samoa recorded 1,044 laboratory-confirmed cases since the start of its outbreak, with weekly counts now low and concentrated among adolescents and young adults. The Marshall Islands declared an active dengue fever outbreak on 21 August after identifying eight confirmed cases and initiating coordinated response measures including active case finding and vector control. New Caledonia declared its dengue epidemic over on 16 August, having reported 2,235 cases and four deaths since January, with DENV-1 as the sole circulating serotype. Samoa saw a 47% weekly increase in new cases, while Vanuatu’s South-West Efate outbreak reached 91 confirmed cases with no severe outcomes. Wallis and Futuna reported 70 confirmed or probable cases, with transmission focused in Futuna. Serotype data indicate DENV-1 predominance in most locations, with DENV-2 co-circulating in Samoa.
